F_ENUM(KIND, ID_CODE, RES_KIND, ERROR_IF_EMPTY)→ D_STR_CODE DETERMINISTIC
KIND VARCHAR(50)
ID_CODE VARCHAR(50)
RES_KIND VARCHAR(50)
ERROR_IF_EMPTY SMALLINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_ENUM(
    KIND D_STR_CODE,
    ID_CODE D_STR_CODE,
    RES_KIND D_STR_CODE,
    ERROR_IF_EMPTY D_SMALLINT = 1)
RETURNS D_STR_CODE DETERMINISTIC
AS
begin
  -- возвращает значение enum (констант)

  if (res_kind not in ('id', 'code', 'name')) then
    exception e_default 'Invalid res_kind: ' || coalesce(res_kind, '<NULL>');

  if (res_kind = 'id') then
    return f_enum_id(kind, id_code, error_if_empty);

  if (res_kind = 'code') then
    return f_enum_code(kind, id_code, error_if_empty);

  if (res_kind = 'name') then
    return f_enum_name(kind, id_code, error_if_empty);
end
F_ENUM_CODE(KIND, ID_CODE, ERROR_IF_EMPTY)→ D_STR_CODE DETERMINISTIC
Кодовое значение enum (константы)
KIND VARCHAR(50)
ID_CODE VARCHAR(50)
ERROR_IF_EMPTY SMALLINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_ENUM_CODE(
    KIND D_STR_CODE,
    ID_CODE D_STR_CODE,
    ERROR_IF_EMPTY D_SMALLINT = 1)
RETURNS D_STR_CODE DETERMINISTIC
AS
declare variable code d_str_code;
declare variable num d_integer;
begin
  -- возвращает Code значения enum (констант)

  begin
    num = id_code;
    when any do
      num = null;
  end

  if (num is not null) then
  begin
    select el_code
    from enum_list
    where el_kind = :kind and
          el_id = :num
    into :code;
  end else
  begin
    select el_code
    from enum_list
    where el_kind = :kind and
          el_code = :id_code
    into :code;
  end

  if (row_count = 0 and error_if_empty = 1) then
    exception e_default f_format('Enum not found, kind: %1, id_code: %2', kind, id_code);

  return code;
end
F_ENUM_ID(KIND, ID_CODE, ERROR_IF_EMPTY)→ D_INTEGER DETERMINISTIC
ID значение enum (константы)
KIND VARCHAR(50)
ID_CODE VARCHAR(50)
ERROR_IF_EMPTY SMALLINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_ENUM_ID(
    KIND D_STR_CODE,
    ID_CODE D_STR_CODE,
    ERROR_IF_EMPTY D_SMALLINT = 1)
RETURNS D_INTEGER DETERMINISTIC
AS
declare variable id d_integer;
declare variable num d_integer;
begin
  -- возвращает ID значения enum (констант)

  begin
    num = id_code;
    when any do
      num = null;
  end

  if (num is not null) then
  begin
    select el_id
    from enum_list
    where el_kind = :kind and
          el_id = :num
    into :id;
  end else
  begin
    select el_id
    from enum_list
    where el_kind = :kind and
          el_code = :id_code
    into :id;
  end

  if (row_count = 0 and error_if_empty = 1) then
    exception e_default f_format('Enum not found, kind: %1, id_code: %2', kind, id_code);

  return id;
end
F_ENUM_NAME(KIND, ID_CODE, ERROR_IF_EMPTY)→ D_STR_CODE DETERMINISTIC
Наименование enum (константы)
KIND VARCHAR(50)
ID_CODE VARCHAR(50)
ERROR_IF_EMPTY SMALLINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_ENUM_NAME(
    KIND D_STR_CODE,
    ID_CODE D_STR_CODE,
    ERROR_IF_EMPTY D_SMALLINT = 1)
RETURNS D_STR_CODE DETERMINISTIC
AS
declare variable name d_str_code;
declare variable num d_integer;
begin
  -- возвращает Name значения enum (констант)
  begin
    num = id_code;
    when any do
      num = null;
  end

  if (num is not null) then
  begin
    select el_name
    from enum_list
    where el_kind = :kind and
          el_id = :num
    into :name;
  end else
  begin
    select el_name
    from enum_list
    where el_kind = :kind and
          el_code = :id_code
    into :name;
  end

  if (row_count = 0 and error_if_empty = 1) then
    exception e_default f_format('Enum not found, kind: %1, id_code: %2', kind, id_code);

  return name;
end
F_FINCERT_PASSPORT_HASH(PASS_NO)→ D_STR_64
PASS_NO VARCHAR(50)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_FINCERT_PASSPORT_HASH(
    PASS_NO D_STR_CODE)
RETURNS D_STR_64
AS
declare variable HASH_PASS_NO varchar(32) character set OCTETS;
declare variable HASH_HEX D_STR_64;
begin
  -- RFCRU-3625 функция, которая по номеру паспорта клиента вернет хэш (используя метод криптографии)
  pass_no = replace(pass_no, '№', '');
  pass_no = replace(pass_no, ' ', '');
  pass_no = upper(pass_no);

  if (coalesce(pass_no, '') <> '') then
  begin
    hash_pass_no = crypt_hash(cast(:pass_no as varchar(4096) character set octets) using SHA256); -- вернет хэш в бинарном виде

    -- конвертируем хеш в строку utf-8
    hash_hex = hex_encode(hash_pass_no);
  end

  return lower(hash_hex);
end
F_FINCERT_PHONE_CHECK(PHONE_NUMBER)→ D_BOOL
PHONE_NUMBER VARCHAR(20)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_FINCERT_PHONE_CHECK(
    PHONE_NUMBER D_STR_20)
RETURNS D_BOOL
AS
declare variable RES D_BOOL;
declare variable FASTPAY_NUMBER_ID D_INTEGER;
declare variable PHONE_NUMBER_ID D_INTEGER;
begin
  res = 0;
  fastpay_number_id = f_enum_id('fincert_file', 'fastpay_number');
  phone_number_id = f_enum_id('fincert_file', 'phone_number');

  if (exists(select *
             from fincert_files t
             where t.ff_file_kind = :fastpay_number_id
             and t.ff_file_body containing :phone_number)) then
    res = 1;
  else if (exists(select *
             from fincert_files t
             where t.ff_file_kind = :phone_number_id
             and t.ff_file_body containing :phone_number)) then
    res = 1;
  return res;
end
F_FINCERT_SNILS_HASH(SNILS)→ D_STR_64
SNILS VARCHAR(50)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_FINCERT_SNILS_HASH(
    SNILS D_STR_CODE)
RETURNS D_STR_64
AS
declare variable HASH_SNILS_NO varchar(32) character set OCTETS;
declare variable HASH_HEX D_STR_64;
begin
  -- RFCRU-4205 функция, которая по СНИЛС вернет хэш (используя метод криптографии)
  snils = replace(snils, '№', '');
  snils = replace(snils, ' ', '');
  snils = upper(snils);

  if (coalesce(snils, '') <> '') then
  begin
    hash_snils_no = crypt_hash(cast(:snils as varchar(4096) character set octets) using SHA256);

    -- конвертируем хеш в строку utf-8
    hash_hex = hex_encode(hash_snils_no);
  end

  return lower(hash_hex);
end
F_FMT_ACC(ACC_ID)→ D_STR_CODE
ACC_ID DECIMAL(18,0)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_FMT_ACC(
    ACC_ID ID_TYPE)
RETURNS D_STR_CODE
AS
declare variable res_code d_str_code;
declare variable acc_code d_str_code;
declare variable sab_acc_mask d_str_255;
declare variable n integer;
declare variable mask_len integer;
declare variable n_code integer;
begin
  res_code = '';
  if (acc_id is null) then
    return res_code;

  select acc_code, sab_acc_mask
  from ref_account, sys_accbalance
  where acc_id = :acc_id and
        sab_id = acc_from_sab_id
  into :acc_code, sab_acc_mask;

  if (coalesce(sab_acc_mask, '') = '') then
    sab_acc_mask = 'BBBBB.VVV.0000.NNNNNNNN';
  sab_acc_mask = replace(sab_acc_mask, 'SSSSSS', 'SSSSS.S');
  mask_len = char_length(sab_acc_mask);

  n = 0;
  res_code = '';
  n_code = 0;
  while (n < mask_len) do
  begin
    n = n + 1;
    if (substring(sab_acc_mask from n for 1) = '.') then
    begin
      if (n_code >= 5) then
        res_code = res_code || '.';
    end
    else
    begin
      n_code = n_code + 1;
      res_code = res_code || substring(acc_code from n_code for 1);
    end
  end

  return res_code;
end
F_FORMAT(IN_TEXT, VAR1, VAR2, VAR3, VAR4, VAR5)→ D_STR_8100
Функция подмены параметров в тексте. / Пример: / f_format('Если %1 умножить на %2, получится %3.', a, b, a*b) / a = 5 / b = 3 / -> Если 5 умножить на 3, получится 15.
IN_TEXT VARCHAR(8100)
VAR1 VARCHAR(4096)
VAR2 VARCHAR(4096)
VAR3 VARCHAR(4096)
VAR4 VARCHAR(4096)
VAR5 VARCHAR(4096)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_FORMAT(
    IN_TEXT D_STR_8100,
    VAR1 D_STR_4096 = null,
    VAR2 D_STR_4096 = null,
    VAR3 D_STR_4096 = null,
    VAR4 D_STR_4096 = null,
    VAR5 D_STR_4096 = null)
RETURNS D_STR_8100
AS
declare variable res_text d_str_8100;
begin
  if (in_text is null) then
    in_text = '';
  res_text = in_text;

  res_text = replace(res_text, '%1', coalesce(var1, ''));
  res_text = replace(res_text, '%2', coalesce(var2, ''));
  res_text = replace(res_text, '%3', coalesce(var3, ''));
  res_text = replace(res_text, '%4', coalesce(var4, ''));
  res_text = replace(res_text, '%5', coalesce(var5, ''));

  return res_text;
end
F_FREEZE_GET_REJECT_STATUS(MA_ID)→ D_BOOL
MA_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_FREEZE_GET_REJECT_STATUS(
    MA_ID D_ID)
RETURNS D_BOOL
AS
declare variable k_id d_id;
declare variable freeze_datetime_end d_datetime;
declare variable db_id d_integer;
begin
  db_id = f_get_db_id();

  select k_id
  from kontract
  where k_from_ma_app_id = :ma_id
    and k_status >= 0
  order by k_status desc, k_id desc
  rows 1
  into k_id;

  if (k_id is null) then
    return 0;

  select freeze_datetime_end
  from hp_get_freeze_data(:k_id)
  into freeze_datetime_end;

  if (freeze_datetime_end is null) then
    return 0;

  -- RFCRU-5900 для РФ (db_id = 81001) кнопка доступна на любом этапе после подписания договора,
  -- без проверки истечения периода охлаждения
  if (db_id = 81001) then
    return 1;

  return iif(freeze_datetime_end < current_timestamp, 0, 1);
end
F_FSM_CLIENT_STATUS(C_ID)→ D_SMALLINT
C_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_FSM_CLIENT_STATUS(
    C_ID D_ID)
RETURNS D_SMALLINT
AS
declare variable MCL_ID D_ID;
declare variable OS D_STR_100;
declare variable MCL_APP_VERSION D_STR_10;
declare variable RESULT D_SMALLINT;
begin
    /*  Функция возращает статус КМП по клиенту
        0 - КМП не установлено
        1 - установлено, но версия устаревшая (клиент работать с ним не может)
        2 - установлено, версия соответствует последней.
    */

    select ml.mcl_id,
           trim((case ml.mcl_os when 2 then 'kmp_client_ios_ver'
                                else 'kmp_client_android_ver' end)),
           ml.mcl_app_version
    from mc_login ml
    where ml.mcl_from_c_id = :c_id
    and ml.mcl_is_delete = 0
    order by ml.mcl_id desc
    rows 1
    into :mcl_id, :os, :mcl_app_version;
    
    if (mcl_id is null) then
        result = 0;
    else
    begin
        result = 1;

        if (f_get_const(:os) = :mcl_app_version) then
            result = 2;
    end

    return result;
end
F_FT_VALID(C_ID, CHECK_DATE, CHECK_EDOC)→ D_SMALLINT
C_ID BIGINT
CHECK_DATE DATE
CHECK_EDOC SMALLINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_FT_VALID(
    C_ID D_ID NOT NULL,
    CHECK_DATE D_DATE NOT NULL,
    CHECK_EDOC D_BOOL = 0)
RETURNS D_SMALLINT
AS
declare variable res_id d_smallint;
declare variable edl_blank_date d_date;
declare variable edc_id d_integer;
declare variable edl_id d_id;
declare variable fal_need_check d_bool;
declare variable regular_client d_smallint;
declare variable check_zalog d_bool;
declare variable k_id d_id;
begin
  -- Функция вовращает валидность Анкеты-опросника в зависимости от валидности договора АСП
  -- Если анкета не валидная, вместе с договором займа формируется Анкета-опросник
  -- Возможные результаты:

  -- -1 (need_check) Нестандартные ответы. Чтобы продолжить требуется аннулировать анкету вручную.
  -- 0 (invalid) Необходимо печатать анкету
  -- 1 (valid)   Не нужно печатать накету

  -- Валидный договор АСП (ONLINE), означает, что:
  -- после подписания договора АСП клиент хоть один раз получил займ посещая офис
  -- или документ подписан вручную и в базе имеется скан копия этого договора

  -- если не указана дата проверки валидности, проверяем на текущую дату
  if (check_date is null) then
    check_date = current_date;

  res_id = 0; -- invalid
  regular_client = 0;
  -- получаем ID актуальной конфигурации договора АСП (ЭЦП)
  select edc_id
  from edoc_cfg
  where edc_code = 'asp.agreement' and
        edc_state = 3 and
        edc_date_begin <= :check_date and
        coalesce(edc_date_end, cast('31.12.9999' as d_date)) > :check_date
  into edc_id;

  -- если конфигурация отсутствует, значит в стране не активирован электронная подпись
  if (edc_id is null) then
    return res_id;

  edl_id = null;
  select edl_id, edl_blank_date
  from edoc_list
  where edl_object_kind = f_objkind('clients', 'id') and
        edl_object_id = :c_id and
        edl_from_edc_id = :edc_id and
        edl_status = 1 and -- подписанный
        :check_date between edl_blank_date and coalesce(edl_date_end, :check_date)
  order by edl_id desc
  rows 1
  into edl_id, edl_blank_date;

  if (edl_id is not null) then
  begin
    -- проверяем наличие активного займа на дату подписания или выданного после этой даты
    -- активный на дату проверки или выдан после этой даты - из-за этоо ks_date_begin исключается из условия
    if (exists(select *
               from knclient, kontstate, kontract
               where kc_from_c_id = :c_id and
                     kc_amount_approved > 0 and
                     kc_from_k_id = k_id and
                     kc_from_k_id = ks_from_k_id and
                     ks_ol_is_delete = 0 and
                     ks_state = 1 and
                     ks_date_end >= :edl_blank_date and
                     ks_date_begin <= :check_date)) then
    begin
      res_id = 1; -- valid
      regular_client = 1;
    end
  end

  if (check_edoc = 1 and
      f_get_const('ft_anketa_state') in (1, 3)) then
  begin
    for select fal_need_check
        from edoc_list, ft_anketa_list
        where edl_object_kind = f_objkind('ft_anketa_list', 'id') and
              edl_object_id = fal_id and
              edl_from_edc_id = f_edoc_kind('ft.anketa', 'id') and
              fal_from_c_id = :c_id and
              edl_status = 1 and -- подписанный
              :check_date between edl_blank_date and coalesce(edl_date_end, :check_date)
        into fal_need_check
    do
      if (fal_need_check = 1) then
        res_id = -1; -- ТРЕБУЕТСЯ ПРОВЕРКА (АННУЛИРОВАНИЕ). Т.к. с такими ответами выдавать нельзя
      else
        res_id = 1; -- valid
  end

  if (regular_client = 0 and
      res_id = 1) then
  begin
    for select z.z_from_k_id
        from zalog z
        inner join knclient knc on knc.kc_from_k_id = z.z_from_k_id and
              knc.kc_amount_approved > 0
        where z.z_from_c_id = :c_id and
              z.z_from_c_id <> knc.kc_from_c_id
        into :k_id
    do
    begin
      if (exists(select *
                 from kontstate ks
                 inner join kontract k on k.k_id = ks.ks_from_k_id
                 where ks.ks_from_k_id = :k_id and
                       ks.ks_state = 0 and
                       :check_date between ks.ks_date_begin and ks.ks_date_end and
                       ks.ks_ol_is_delete = 0 and
                       k.k_status >= 0)) then
      begin
        if (not exists(select fal_need_check
                       from edoc_list, ft_anketa_list fal, ft_anketa_kind fak
                       where edl_object_kind = f_objkind('ft_anketa_list', 'id') and
                             edl_object_id = fal_id and
                             edl_from_edc_id = f_edoc_kind('ft.anketa', 'id') and
                             fal.fal_from_fak_id = fak.fak_id and
                             fak.fak_code = 'zalog' and
                             fal_from_c_id = :c_id and
                             edl_status = 1 and -- подписанный
                             :check_date between edl_blank_date and coalesce(edl_date_end, :check_date))) then
        begin
          res_id = 0;
        end
      end
    end
  end
  return res_id;
end
F_GEN_PASSWORD(PASS_LENGTH, NEED_UPPERS, NEED_LOWERS, NEED_DIGITS, NEED_SYMBOLS)→ D_STR_255
PASS_LENGTH INTEGER — Количество символов пароля
NEED_UPPERS SMALLINT — Требуются большие латинские буквы
NEED_LOWERS SMALLINT — Требуются маленькие латинские буквы
NEED_DIGITS SMALLINT — Требуются цифры
NEED_SYMBOLS SMALLINT — Требуются символы
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GEN_PASSWORD(
    PASS_LENGTH INTEGER NOT NULL,
    NEED_UPPERS D_BOOL NOT NULL,
    NEED_LOWERS D_BOOL NOT NULL,
    NEED_DIGITS D_BOOL NOT NULL,
    NEED_SYMBOLS D_SMALLINT NOT NULL)
RETURNS D_STR_255
AS
declare variable chars_pwd d_str_255;
declare variable chars_uppers d_str_code;
declare variable chars_lowers d_str_code;
declare variable chars_digits d_str_code;
declare variable chars_symbols d_str_code;
declare variable need_regen_pwd d_bool;
declare variable i d_integer;
declare variable new_pass d_str_255;
begin
  -- Функция генерации пароля заданной длины и сложности

  -- need_symbols = 2, это костыль для соответствия политике AD
  -- Если символы разные, пользователи жалуются на сложность ввода.
  -- После запуска Элмы, когда пароль будет генерировать в сервисе Камиля
  -- убрать эту доработку и вызывать с need_symbols=0

  -- В массивах ниже убраны визуально похожие символы, например 0 и O
  chars_uppers = 'ABCDEFGHKMNPQRSUVWXYZ';
  chars_lowers = 'abcdefghkmnpqrsuvwxyz';
  chars_digits = '123456789';
  chars_symbols = '!@#$%&'; -- Сделать вариант только с @
  chars_pwd = '';

  if (pass_length < 1) then
    exception e_default 'Необходимо увеличить количество символов в пароле';
  if (need_uppers = 0 and
      need_lowers = 0 and
      need_digits = 0 and
      need_symbols = 0) then
    exception e_default 'Необходимо выбрать как минимум одну группу символов для генерации пароля';

  if (need_uppers = 1) then chars_pwd = chars_pwd || chars_uppers;
  if (need_lowers = 1) then chars_pwd = chars_pwd || chars_lowers;
  if (need_digits = 1) then chars_pwd = chars_pwd || chars_digits;
  if (need_symbols = 1) then chars_pwd = chars_pwd || chars_symbols;
  if (need_symbols = 2) then chars_pwd = chars_pwd || '@';

  need_regen_pwd = 1;
  while (need_regen_pwd = 1) do
  begin
    new_pass = '';
    i = 0;
    while (i < pass_length) do
    begin
      new_pass = new_pass || substring(chars_pwd from trunc(rand()* char_length(chars_pwd) + 1) for 1);
      i = i + 1;
    end
    if ((need_uppers = 0 or new_pass similar to '%[' || chars_uppers || ']%') and
        (need_lowers = 0 or new_pass similar to '%[' || chars_lowers || ']%') and
        (need_digits = 0 or new_pass similar to '%[' || chars_digits || ']%') and
        (need_symbols = 0 or (need_symbols = 1 and new_pass similar to '%[' || chars_symbols || ']%')or
        (need_symbols = 2 and new_pass containing '@'))) then
    need_regen_pwd = 0;
  end
  return new_pass;
end
F_GET_ACC_AMOUNT(KC_ID, SAB_ID, REP_DATE_BEGIN, REP_DATE_END, AMOUNT_KIND)→ CURR_TYPE
KC_ID DECIMAL(18,0)
SAB_ID DECIMAL(18,0)
REP_DATE_BEGIN DATE
REP_DATE_END DATE
AMOUNT_KIND VARCHAR(50)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_ACC_AMOUNT(
    KC_ID ID_TYPE,
    SAB_ID ID_TYPE,
    REP_DATE_BEGIN DATE,
    REP_DATE_END DATE,
    AMOUNT_KIND D_STR_CODE)
RETURNS CURR_TYPE
AS
declare variable acc_id id_type;
declare variable amount curr_type;
declare variable tmp_amount curr_type = 0;
begin
  if (amount_kind not in ('dt', 'kt', 'bal')) then
    exception e_default 'Incorrect amount kind: ' || coalesce(amount_kind, '<null>');

  amount = 0;
  if (amount_kind = 'bal') then
  begin
    for select acc_id
        from ref_account
        where acc_object_kind = 8 and
              acc_object_id = :kc_id and
              acc_from_sab_id = :sab_id
        into :acc_id
    do
    begin
      tmp_amount = f_acc_bal(acc_id, rep_date_end);
      amount = amount + tmp_amount;
    end
  end

  if (amount_kind = 'dt') then
  begin
    for select acc_id
        from ref_account
        where acc_object_kind = 8 and
              acc_object_id = :kc_id and
              acc_from_sab_id = :sab_id
        into :acc_id
    do
    begin
      tmp_amount = 0;
      select sum(kobs_dt_sum)
      from kn_obsummary
      where kobs_acc_id = :acc_id and
            kobs_date between :rep_date_begin and :rep_date_end
      into :tmp_amount;
      if (tmp_amount is null) then
        tmp_amount = 0;
      amount = amount + tmp_amount;
    end
  end

  if (amount_kind = 'kt') then
  begin
    for select acc_id
        from ref_account
        where acc_object_kind = 8 and
              acc_object_id = :kc_id and
              acc_from_sab_id = :sab_id
        into :acc_id
    do
    begin
      tmp_amount = 0;
      select sum(kobs_kt_sum)
      from kn_obsummary
      where kobs_acc_id = :acc_id and
            kobs_date between :rep_date_begin and :rep_date_end
      into :tmp_amount;
      if (tmp_amount is null) then
        tmp_amount = 0;
      amount = amount + tmp_amount;
    end
  end

  return amount;
end
F_GET_ACC_COMMON_ID(ACC_ID, ACC_CODE, SYS_FULL_CODE, ACC_OBJECT_KIND, ACC_OBJECT_ID)→ D_ID
ACC_ID BIGINT
ACC_CODE VARCHAR(50)
SYS_FULL_CODE VARCHAR(50)
ACC_OBJECT_KIND INTEGER
ACC_OBJECT_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_ACC_COMMON_ID(
    ACC_ID D_ID,
    ACC_CODE D_STR_CODE,
    SYS_FULL_CODE D_STR_CODE,
    ACC_OBJECT_KIND D_INTEGER,
    ACC_OBJECT_ID D_ID)
RETURNS D_ID
AS
declare variable res_acc_id d_id;
begin
  res_acc_id = acc_id;

  if ((sys_full_code starting 'Микрозаймы.' and
      acc_object_kind = f_objkind('knclient', 'id') and acc_object_id >= 9 and
      sys_full_code <> 'Микрозаймы.Погашено.ЗалогСТДО') or
      (sys_full_code = 'Сотрудники.Зарплата' and acc_object_kind = f_objkind('clients', 'id')) or
      (sys_full_code = 'Расчеты.ЛоялПартнеры' and acc_object_kind = f_objkind('agreements', 'id'))) then
  begin
    if (sys_full_code = 'Расчеты.ЛоялПартнеры')
    then acc_code = left(acc_code, 13) || '0000001';
    else acc_code = left(acc_code, 12) || '00000001';

    res_acc_id = null;
    select acc_id
    from ref_account
    where acc_code = :acc_code
    into res_acc_id;

    if (res_acc_id is null) then
      exception e_default 'Account not found: ' || coalesce(acc_code, 'null');
  end

  return res_acc_id;
end
F_GET_ACC_ID(ACC_CODE)→ ID_TYPE
ACC_CODE VARCHAR(50)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_ACC_ID(
    ACC_CODE D_STR_CODE)
RETURNS ID_TYPE
AS
declare variable res_id id_type;
begin
  -- возвращает ID счета по его коду
  select acc_id
  from ref_account
  where acc_code = :acc_code
  into :res_id;

  return res_id;
end
F_GET_ACTION_COUNT_MO(C_ID)→ INTEGER
C_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_ACTION_COUNT_MO(
    C_ID D_ID)
RETURNS INTEGER
AS
declare variable RES D_INTEGER;
declare variable RD_COUNT D_INTEGER;
declare variable REG_ID D_ID;
declare variable KC_UN_ID D_ID;
begin
  res = 0;

  select ps.ps_from_reg_id
  from hr_pstatus ps
  where ps.ps_from_c_id = :c_id and
        ps.ps_is_on_staff = 1 and 
        ps.ps_is_working = 1 and 
        ps.ps_status = 1 and
        current_date between ps.ps_date_begin and ps.ps_date_end
  rows 1
  into :reg_id;

  for select un.un_id       -- находим КС-ов по офису менеджера
  from username un
  join hr_pstatus ps on ps.ps_from_c_id = un.un_from_c_id
  join joblist jl on jl.jl_id = ps.ps_from_jl_id
  inner join jobkind jk on jl.jl_kind = jk.jk_id
        and jk.jk_system_code = 'JobSystem.LO_Standart'
  where ps.ps_from_reg_id = :reg_id
        and ps.ps_is_on_staff = 1
        and ps.ps_is_working = 1
        and ps.ps_status = 1
        and current_date between ps.ps_date_begin and ps.ps_date_end
  into :kc_un_id
  do
  begin
    for select rd_count
    from fm_anketa_kind_list(:kc_un_id)
    where rd_id = 8941 -- RD_ID Ошибка в досье
    into :rd_count
    do
    begin
     res = res + rd_count;
    end
  end
  return res;
end
F_GET_ADDRESS_STR(LANG_KIND, ADL_FROM_PL_ID, ADL_STREET_ID, ADL_PLACE_PLS_ID, ADL_STREET_PLS_ID, ADL_DEPART_NO, ADL_FLAT_NO, ADL_BUILDING_NO, ADL_SECTION_NO, ADL_POST_INDEX)→ D_STR_255
LANG_KIND SMALLINT
ADL_FROM_PL_ID BIGINT
ADL_STREET_ID BIGINT
ADL_PLACE_PLS_ID BIGINT
ADL_STREET_PLS_ID BIGINT
ADL_DEPART_NO VARCHAR(20)
ADL_FLAT_NO VARCHAR(20)
ADL_BUILDING_NO VARCHAR(20)
ADL_SECTION_NO VARCHAR(20)
ADL_POST_INDEX VARCHAR(20)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_ADDRESS_STR(
    LANG_KIND D_SMALLINT,
    ADL_FROM_PL_ID D_ID,
    ADL_STREET_ID D_ID,
    ADL_PLACE_PLS_ID D_ID,
    ADL_STREET_PLS_ID D_ID,
    ADL_DEPART_NO D_STR_20,
    ADL_FLAT_NO D_STR_20,
    ADL_BUILDING_NO D_STR_20,
    ADL_SECTION_NO D_STR_20,
    ADL_POST_INDEX D_STR_20)
RETURNS D_STR_255
AS
declare variable address_str d_str_255;
declare variable db_id d_integer;
begin
  address_str = '';
  if (adl_street_id is null and
      adl_from_pl_id is null) then
    return address_str;

  db_id = f_get_db_id();

  select address_str
  from hp_make_addr_place(:lang_kind, :adl_from_pl_id, :adl_street_id, :adl_place_pls_id, :adl_street_pls_id, :adl_post_index)
  into :address_str;

  if (coalesce(adl_depart_no, '') = '') then
    adl_depart_no = '0';
  if (coalesce(adl_flat_no, '') = '') then
    adl_flat_no = '0';
  if (coalesce(adl_building_no, '') = '') then
    adl_building_no = '0';
  if (coalesce(adl_section_no, '') = '') then
    adl_section_no = '0';

  if (adl_depart_no = '0') then
    adl_depart_no = '';
  if (adl_flat_no = '0') then
    adl_flat_no = '';
  if (adl_building_no = '0') then
    adl_building_no = '';
  if (adl_section_no = '0') then
    adl_section_no = '';

  if (db_id = 94401) then
  begin
    if (adl_depart_no <> '') then
      address_str = address_str || ', ev.' || adl_depart_no;
    if (adl_flat_no <> '') then
      address_str = address_str || ', m.' || adl_flat_no;
    if (adl_building_no <> '') then
      address_str = address_str || ', tik.' || adl_building_no;
    if (adl_section_no <> '') then
      address_str = address_str || ', korp.' || adl_section_no;
  end
  else
  begin
    if (lang_kind = 2 and
        db_id in (39801, 41701)) then
    begin
      if (adl_depart_no <> '') then
        address_str = address_str || ', ' || adl_depart_no || ' үй';
      if (adl_flat_no <> '') then
        address_str = address_str || ', ' || adl_flat_no || ' бат.';
      if (adl_building_no <> '') then
        address_str = address_str || ', ' || adl_building_no || ' корп.';
      if (adl_section_no <> '') then
        address_str = address_str || ', ' || adl_section_no || ' имарат';
    end
    else
    begin
      if (adl_depart_no <> '') then
        address_str = address_str || ', д.' || adl_depart_no;
      if (adl_flat_no <> '') then
        address_str = address_str || ', кв.' || adl_flat_no;
      if (adl_building_no <> '') then
        address_str = address_str || ', стр.' || adl_building_no;
      if (adl_section_no <> '') then
        address_str = address_str || ', корп.' || adl_section_no;
    end
  end

  if (adl_depart_no = '' and
      adl_flat_no = '' and
      adl_building_no = '' and
      adl_section_no = '') then
  begin
    if (db_id = 94401) then
      address_str = address_str || ', Nömrəsiz ev';
    else
    if (db_id in (39801, 41701) and
        lang_kind = 2) then
      address_str = address_str || ', н/ж.';
    else
      address_str = address_str || ', б/н.';
  end

  address_str = f_replace_all(address_str, '  ', ' ');
  address_str = trim(address_str);
  address_str = replace(address_str, '. ', '.');

  return address_str;
end
F_GET_CARDSYNCCFG(OBJ, RES_KIND)→ D_STR_100 DETERMINISTIC
OBJ VARCHAR(100)
RES_KIND VARCHAR(50)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_CARDSYNCCFG(
    OBJ D_STR_100 NOT NULL,
    RES_KIND D_STR_CODE NOT NULL)
RETURNS D_STR_100 DETERMINISTIC
AS
declare variable result d_str_100;
begin
  if (res_kind not in ('id', 'code')) then
    exception e_default 'Invalid res_kind: ' || coalesce(res_kind, '<NULL>');

  if (res_kind = 'id') then
    result = (select csc_id from cardsynccfg where csc_code = :obj);
  else if (res_kind = 'code') then
    result = (select csc_code from cardsynccfg where csc_id = :obj);

  return result;
end
F_GET_CARDSYNCCFG_CODE(CSC_ID)→ D_STR_100 DETERMINISTIC
CSC_ID INTEGER
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_CARDSYNCCFG_CODE(
    CSC_ID D_INTEGER NOT NULL)
RETURNS D_STR_100 DETERMINISTIC
AS
declare variable csc_code d_str_100;
begin
  csc_code = (select csc_code from cardsynccfg where csc_id = :csc_id);
  return csc_code;
end

CSC_CODE VARCHAR(100)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_CARDSYNCCFG_ID(
    CSC_CODE D_STR_100 NOT NULL)
RETURNS INTEGER DETERMINISTIC
AS
declare variable csc_id d_integer;
begin
  csc_id = (select csc_id from cardsynccfg where csc_code = :csc_code);
  return csc_id;
end
F_GET_COMMON_JSON(KEY_NAME, LANG)→ D_STR_8100
KEY_NAME VARCHAR(255)
LANG VARCHAR(100)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_COMMON_JSON(
    KEY_NAME D_STR_255,
    LANG D_STR_100 = 'ru')
RETURNS D_STR_8100
AS
declare variable temp_json d_str_8100;
begin
    select gr_json_value
    from mc_common_ref t
    where t.gr_is_active = 1
        and t.gr_key_name = :key_name
        and t.gr_lang containing '.'||:lang||'.'
    into temp_json;

    return temp_json;
end
F_GET_CONST(CONST_NAME)→ D_STR_255 DETERMINISTIC
CONST_NAME VARCHAR(255)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_CONST(
    CONST_NAME D_STR_255)
RETURNS D_STR_255 DETERMINISTIC
AS
declare variable res_text d_str_255;
declare variable db_id d_integer;
begin
  db_id = f_get_db_id();

  res_text = '$_Unknown_$';

  if (db_id = 81001) then
  begin
    res_text = (case const_name
                  when 'cm_default_currency' then '810'
                  when 'operbook_kind' then 1
                  when 'check_info_kind' then '1' -- в зависимости 0 или 1, будет открываться старое или новое окно проверки данных в КК
                  when 'html_font_name' then 'Calibri Light' -- Шрифт отображаемый при просмотре HTML (MainForm.ShowHTML)
                  when 'moneta_acc' then '65562035'
                  when 'moneta_oivo_acc' then '47582496'
                  when 'wirebank_acc' then '16378'
                  when 'wirebank_oivo_acc' then '16834'
                  when 'test_c_id' then '1010100254158'
                  when 'new_contdata' then 0  -- работаем по ContData (новая структура) или ContInfo (старая структура)
                  when 'new_clientdocs' then 1 -- работа с ClientDocs через механизм Docrecog (новая форма)
                  when 'company_cell' then '89037676000' -- Сотовый номер компании для входящих смс
                  when 'new_reschedule' then '1' -- Применять новое окно реструктуризации
                  when 'blank_default_lang' then 'ru' -- Язык по умолчанию для бланков
                  when 'edoc_new_mode' then '1' -- Использовать новую структуре eDoc
                  else '$_Unknown_$'
                end);
  end

  if (db_id = 41701) then
  begin
    res_text = (case const_name
                  when 'cm_default_currency' then '417'
                  when 'operbook_kind' then 0
                  when 'check_info_kind' then '0'
                  when 'html_font_name' then 'Calibri Light' -- Шрифт отображаемый при просмотре HTML (MainForm.ShowHTML)
                  when 'new_contdata' then 0
                  when 'new_clientdocs' then 1 -- работа с ClientDocs через механизм Docrecog (новая форма)
                  when 'company_cell' then '+996220600600' -- Сотовый номер компании для входящих смс
                  when 'new_reschedule' then '1' -- Применять новое окно реструктуризации
                  when 'blank_default_lang' then 'ru' -- Язык по умолчанию для бланков
                  when 'edoc_new_mode' then '1' -- Использовать новую структуре eDoc
                  else '$_Unknown_$'
                end);
  end

  if (db_id = 39801) then
  begin
    res_text = (case const_name
                  when 'cm_default_currency' then '398'
                  when 'operbook_kind' then 0
                  when 'check_info_kind' then '0'
                  when 'html_font_name' then 'Calibri Light' -- Шрифт отображаемый при просмотре HTML (MainForm.ShowHTML)
                  when 'new_contdata' then 0
                  when 'new_clientdocs' then 1 -- работа с ClientDocs через механизм Docrecog (новая форма)
                  when 'company_cell' then '' -- Сотовый номер компании для входящих смс
                  when 'new_reschedule' then '1' -- Применять новое окно реструктуризации
                  when 'blank_default_lang' then 'ru' -- Язык по умолчанию для бланков
                  when 'edoc_new_mode' then '1' -- Использовать новую структуре eDoc
                  when 'processing_kz_acc' then '00000000000777' -- счет processing kz
                  else '$_Unknown_$'
                end);
  end

  if (db_id = 94401) then
  begin
    res_text = (case const_name
                  when 'cm_default_currency' then '944'
                  when 'operbook_kind' then 0
                  when 'check_info_kind' then '0'
                  when 'html_font_name' then 'Calibri Light' -- Шрифт отображаемый при просмотре HTML (MainForm.ShowHTML)
                  when 'new_contdata' then 0
                  when 'new_clientdocs' then 0 -- работа с ClientDocs через механизм Docrecog (новая форма)
                  when 'company_cell' then '+994552222059' -- Сотовый номер компании для входящих смс
                  when 'new_reschedule' then '1' -- Применять новое окно реструктуризации
                  when 'blank_default_lang' then 'az' -- Язык по умолчанию для бланков
                  when 'edoc_new_mode' then '1' -- Использовать новую структуре eDoc
                  else '$_Unknown_$'
                end);
  end

  if (res_text = '$_Unknown_$') then
  begin
    -- если значение выше не найдено, ищем среди констант в таблице
    select cv_value
    from const_var
    where cv_code = :const_name
    into res_text;

    if (row_count = 0) then
      exception e_default 'Unknown constant name: ' || const_name || ' in db_id: ' || db_id;
  end

  return res_text;
end
F_GET_DB_ID()→ INTEGER DETERMINISTIC
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_DB_ID
RETURNS INTEGER DETERMINISTIC
AS
declare variable db_id integer;
begin
  db_id = rdb$get_context('USER_SESSION', 'DB_ID');
  if (db_id is null) then
  begin
    select cm_database_id
    from company
    rows 1
    into db_id;
    rdb$set_context('USER_SESSION', 'DB_ID', db_id);
  when any do begin end -- Может быть Update conflicts with concurrent update, игнорируем
  end
  return db_id;
end

F_GET_DISB_SOURCE(K_ID)→ D_STR_CODE
K_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_DISB_SOURCE(
    K_ID D_ID)
RETURNS D_STR_CODE
AS
declare variable res d_str_code;
begin
  -- возвращает  признак вызачи займа для KZ
  -- bc(бизнес-карта) | balance(баланс оплат)
  res = 'bc';

  if (res not in ('bc', 'balance')) then
    exception e_message f_format('Источник "%1" - для выдачи не определен."', res);

  return res;
end
F_GET_FIRST_ACC_ID(ACC_ID, ACC_CODE)→ ID_TYPE
ACC_ID DECIMAL(18,0)
ACC_CODE VARCHAR(50)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_FIRST_ACC_ID(
    ACC_ID ID_TYPE,
    ACC_CODE D_STR_CODE)
RETURNS ID_TYPE
AS
declare variable res_id id_type;
begin
-- возвращает свернутый вериант лицевого счета по ЕПС
  res_id = acc_id;
  acc_code = left(acc_code, 12) || '00000001';
  select acc_id
  from ref_account
  where acc_code = :acc_code
  into :res_id;

  return res_id;
end
F_GET_INTEREST_RATE(K_ID, DT_CALC, TP_RATE)→ PERC_TYPE
K_ID DECIMAL(18,0)
DT_CALC DATE
TP_RATE VARCHAR(100)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_INTEREST_RATE(
    K_ID ID_TYPE NOT NULL,
    DT_CALC DATE NOT NULL = current_date,
    TP_RATE STR_100 NOT NULL = 'YEARLY')
RETURNS PERC_TYPE
AS
declare variable RATE PERC_TYPE;
begin
  rate = 0;
  select
         case :tp_rate
           -- ежедневная
           when 'daily' then kd_percent_per_day
           -- годовая
           when 'yearly' then kd_percent_per_day * iif((pr_code containing '.IPF.' and pr_percent_for_days = 30), 360, 365)
           -- эффективная общая
           when 'effective' then kd_percent_effect
           -- эффективная локальная для страны
           when 'effective_fifd' then kd_percent_effect_fifd
           else 0
         end
  from kontract
  inner join product on pr_id = k_from_pr_id
  inner join kontdata on kd_from_k_id = k_id and
        :dt_calc between kd_ol_date and kd_date_end and
        kd_ol_is_delete = 0
  where k_id = :k_id
  into rate;
  return rate;
end
F_GET_IS_REF(K_ID, REP_DATE)→ INTEGER
K_ID DECIMAL(18,0)
REP_DATE DATE
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_IS_REF(
    K_ID DECIMAL(18,0),
    REP_DATE DATE)
RETURNS INTEGER
AS
declare variable is_ref integer;
begin
  is_ref = 0;
  select 1
  from kontract, kontrefinance
  where k_id = :k_id and
        kr_from_ma_id = k_from_ma_app_id and
        kr_date <= :rep_date rows 1
  into :is_ref;
  return is_ref;
end
F_GET_JOBKIND_CODE(UN_ID, DT_CALC)→ STR_100
UN_ID DECIMAL(18,0)
DT_CALC DATE
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_JOBKIND_CODE(
    UN_ID ID_TYPE,
    DT_CALC DATE NOT NULL = current_date)
RETURNS STR_100
AS
declare variable c_id id_type;
declare variable jk_code str_100;
begin
  if (un_id > 0) then
    select un_from_c_id
    from username
    where un_id = :un_id
    into :c_id;
  else
    c_id = -un_id;

  select jk_system_code
  from hr_pstatus
  inner join joblist on jl_id = ps_from_jl_id
  inner join jobkind on jk_id = jl_kind
  where ps_from_c_id = :c_id and
        :dt_calc between ps_date_begin and ps_date_end
  into :jk_code;
  return coalesce(jk_code, '');
end
F_GET_JSON_FROM_SQL(KEY_NAME, DB_ID, MCL_PROJECT)→ D_BLOB_TEXT
KEY_NAME VARCHAR(255)
DB_ID INTEGER
MCL_PROJECT VARCHAR(10)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_JSON_FROM_SQL(
    KEY_NAME D_STR_255,
    DB_ID D_INTEGER,
    MCL_PROJECT D_STR_10 = '')
RETURNS D_BLOB_TEXT
AS
declare variable sql_text d_blob_text;
begin
    :sql_text =
        case
            when :key_name = 'city_list'
                then 'select city_id, city_name from mc_city_list'

            when :key_name = 'magazine_list'
                then 'select magazine_id, magazine_name, longitude, latitude, address, magazine_city_id, work_hours, phone from mc_magazine_list'

            when :key_name = 'passkind'
                then 'select pk_id, pk_name from passkind where pk_is_delete = 0'

            when :key_name = 'office_list' and :db_id = 81001 and :mcl_project = 'kmp'
                then 'select office_id, office_name, office_city_id, address, longitude, latitude from mc_office_list where office_id not in (1050600, 1031600)'

            when :key_name = 'office_list'
                then 'select office_id, office_name, office_city_id, address, longitude, latitude from mc_office_list'

            else ''
        end;

    if (:sql_text <> '') then
        return (select res_json from hp_json_from_sql('array', :key_name, :sql_text));
end
F_GET_KDN(KC_ID)→ D_CURRENCY
KC_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_KDN(
    KC_ID D_ID)
RETURNS D_CURRENCY
AS
declare variable DB_ID D_INTEGER;
declare variable MA_ID D_ID;
declare variable MAC_ID D_ID;
declare variable C_ID D_ID;
declare variable RESULT_VALUE D_CURRENCY;
begin
    db_id = f_get_db_id();
    result_value = 0;

    /* Казахстан:
       возвращаем КДН, рассчитанный до выдачи займа */
    if (db_id = 39801) then
    begin
        select
            k.k_from_ma_app_id,
            kc.kc_from_c_id
        from kontract k
        inner join knclient kc on kc_from_k_id = k.k_id
        where kc.kc_id = :kc_id
        into
            :ma_id,
            :c_id;

        select first 1
            mac.mac_id
        from miniappclient mac
        where mac.mac_from_ma_id = :ma_id
          and mac.mac_amount > 0
          and mac.mac_from_c_id = :c_id
        order by mac.mac_id
        into
            :mac_id;

        select first 1
            kdn.cakdn_kdn
        from cfp_anketa_kdn kdn
        where kdn.cakdn_from_c_id = :c_id
          and kdn.cakdn_from_mac_id = :mac_id
        into
            :result_value;
    end

    /* РФ:
       возвращаем ПДН, который был рассчитан
       на момент выдачи займа */
    else if (db_id = 81001) then
    begin
        select coalesce(kc_pdn_koef, 0)
        from knclient_pdn
        where kc_id = :kc_id and
              kcp_kind = 0
        into
            :result_value;
    end

    return coalesce(result_value, 0);
end
F_GET_KONTRACT_DATE(K_ID, K_KONTRACT_DATE)→ D_DATE
K_ID BIGINT
K_KONTRACT_DATE DATE
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_KONTRACT_DATE(
    K_ID D_ID,
    K_KONTRACT_DATE D_DATE = null)
RETURNS D_DATE
AS
begin
  if (k_kontract_date is null) then
    select k_kontract_date
    from kontract
    where k_id = :k_id
    into k_kontract_date;

  if (k_kontract_date < '01.09.2025') then
    return k_kontract_date;

  select edl_create_datetime
  from edoc_list, edoc_cfg
  where edc_id = edl_from_edc_id
    and edl_object_kind = 9
    and edl_object_id = :k_id
    and edc_code = 'loan.agreement'
    and edl_status >= 0
  order by edl_create_datetime desc
  rows 1
  into k_kontract_date;

  return k_kontract_date;
end

F_GET_LANG_CODE(UN_ID, MCL_ID)→ D_STR_LANG
UN_ID BIGINT
MCL_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_LANG_CODE(
    UN_ID D_ID,
    MCL_ID D_ID)
RETURNS D_STR_LANG
AS
declare variable res_lang_code d_str_lang;
begin
  res_lang_code = '';
  if (un_id is not null) then
  begin
    select un_lang_code
    from username
    where un_id = :un_id
    into :res_lang_code;
  end
  else
  if (mcl_id is not null) then
  begin
    select mcl_lang_code
    from mc_login
    where mcl_id = :mcl_id
    into :res_lang_code;
  end

  if (res_lang_code = '') then
  begin
    res_lang_code = 'ru';
    if (f_get_db_id() = 94401) then
      res_lang_code = 'az';
  end

  return res_lang_code;
end
F_GET_MCL_PROJECT(OBJ_KIND, OBJ_ID)→ INTEGER
OBJ_KIND INTEGER
OBJ_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_MCL_PROJECT(
    OBJ_KIND D_INTEGER,
    OBJ_ID D_ID)
RETURNS INTEGER
AS
declare variable mcl_project d_integer;
begin
    /*
        RFCRU-4254
        Функция возврващает параметры отправки sms/push для заданной пары object_kind/object_id
        0 - пуш на обе платформы (КМП, ОЙВО) / СМС от имени МБулак
        1 - пуш только в КМП МБулак / СМС от имени МБулак
        2 - пуш только в КМП ОЙВО / СМС от имени ОЙВО

        Ожидает на вход obj_kind: clients/miniapp/kontract. Если переданный obj_kind отличается от ожидаемых,
        возвращает 0
    */

    if (f_get_db_id() <> 81001) then
        return 1;

    mcl_project = 0;

    if (obj_kind = f_objkind('miniapp', 'id')) then
        select iif(pr.pr_code not containing '.oivo_pack.', 1, 2)
        from miniapp ma
        inner join product pr on ma.ma_from_pr_id = pr.pr_id
        where ma.ma_id = :obj_id
            and ma.ma_is_active = 1
            and ma.ma_date_end >= '31.12.2999'
        into :mcl_project;
    else if (obj_kind = f_objkind('kontract', 'id')) then
        select iif(pr.pr_code not containing '.oivo_pack.', 1, 2)
        from kontract k
        inner join product pr on k.k_from_pr_id = pr.pr_id
        where k.k_id = :obj_id
        into :mcl_project;
    else if (obj_kind = f_objkind('clients', 'id')) then
        select mcl.mcl_project
        from mc_login mcl
        where mcl.mcl_from_c_id = :obj_id
            and mcl.mcl_is_delete = 0
        order by mcl.mcl_last_login_datetime desc
        rows 1
        into :mcl_project;

    return coalesce(mcl_project, 0);
end
F_GET_MSG_KIND(C_ID, PROC_NAME, CFG_NAME, DB_ID, INACTIVE_DAYS)→ D_STR_CODE
C_ID BIGINT
PROC_NAME VARCHAR(50)
CFG_NAME VARCHAR(50)
DB_ID INTEGER
INACTIVE_DAYS INTEGER
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_MSG_KIND(
    C_ID D_ID,
    PROC_NAME D_STR_CODE,
    CFG_NAME D_STR_CODE,
    DB_ID D_INTEGER,
    INACTIVE_DAYS D_INTEGER = 90)
RETURNS D_STR_CODE
AS
declare variable res_kind d_str_code;
begin
  -- Процедура возвращае транспорт, как отправлять сообщение
  res_kind = 'sms';
  inactive_days = coalesce(inactive_days, 90);

  db_id = coalesce(db_id, f_get_db_id());
  if (db_id in (94401, 41701)) then
    return res_kind;

  if (db_id = 81001) then
  begin
    if (proc_name in ('aarep_sms_repay_3day', 'fsrobot_sms_dismissed_lo', 'hp_cancel_kontract')) then
      res_kind = 'push_sms';
    else
    if (proc_name = 'aarep_sms_kont_delinq') then
    begin
      -- По просрочкам в РФ только на первый день высылаем СМС, остальные PUSH
      if (cfg_name = '1') then
        res_kind = 'sms';
      else
        res_kind = 'push';
    end
    else
    if (proc_name in ('aarep_sms_birthday', 'aarep_cli_passport_actual_check', 'hp_send_court_notification', 'fsrobot_mailing_sms_repay_ok')) then
      res_kind = 'push';
  end

  if (res_kind in ('push', 'push_sms')) then
  begin
    if (exists(select *
               from mc_login
               where mcl_from_c_id = :c_id and
                     mcl_is_delete = 0 and
                     datediff(day, mcl_last_login_datetime, current_date) <= :inactive_days and
                     coalesce(mcl_google_id, '') <> '')) then
      res_kind = 'push';
    else
      res_kind = decode(res_kind, 'push', 'none', 'push_sms', 'sms');
  end

  -- Варианты только push и sms и none (не отправлять)
  if (res_kind not in ('sms', 'push', 'none')) then
    exception e_default 'Bad res_kind, should be "sms", "push" or "none"';
  return res_kind;
end
F_GET_OBLAST_KG(FULL_ADDRESS)→ D_STR_100
FULL_ADDRESS VARCHAR(255)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_OBLAST_KG(
    FULL_ADDRESS D_STR_255)
RETURNS D_STR_100
AS
begin
  -- Функция возвращает область КР из заданной строки
  -- Пример использования:
  -- select f_get_oblast_kg('722030, Кыргызстан, Джалал-Абадская область, Аксыйский район, с.Джаны-Джол, б/н.') from rdb$database
  -- Результат: 'Жалал-Абадская область'

  if (full_address similar to '(%иссык%обл%|%исык%обл%|%ысык%обл%|%ыссык%обл%)') then
    return 'Ыссык-Кульская область';

  if (full_address similar to '(%жалал_абад%|%жалал_абда%|%жалалабат%|%таш_комур%|%кара_кол_ш%)') then
    return 'Жалал-Абадская область';

  if (full_address similar to '%нарын%обл%|%кочкорский_район%') then
    return 'Нарынская область';

  if (full_address similar to '(%баткен%|%лейлек)%') then
    return 'Баткенская область';

  if (full_address similar to '(%ош%обл%|%обл%ош%|%алай%|%кара_суу_р_ну%)') then
    return 'Ошская область';

  if (full_address similar to '%талас%') then
    return 'Таласская область';

  if (full_address similar to '(%чуй%обл%|%чүй%обл%)') then
    return 'Чуйская область';

  if (full_address similar to '(%бишкек%|%ж/м%)') then
    return 'г. Бишкек';

  if (full_address similar to '(%ош%ш%|%ош%г%|%г%ош%)') then
    return 'г. Ош';

  if (full_address similar to '(Не указан%|%россия%)') then
    return 'Не указан';

  return '';
end
F_GET_PAYMENT_CARD_STATUS(OBJ_ID)→ INTEGER
OBJ_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_PAYMENT_CARD_STATUS(
    OBJ_ID D_ID)
RETURNS INTEGER
AS
declare variable C_ID D_ID;
declare variable PAY_STATUS D_SMALLINT;
begin
  select kc.kc_from_c_id
  from knclient kc
  where kc.kc_from_k_id = :obj_id
    and kc.kc_amount_approved > 0
  into c_id;

  select cpr.cpr_status
  from cnp_payment_requests cpr
  where cpr.cpr_from_c_id = :c_id and
    cpr.cpr_from_k_id = :obj_id
  order by cpr.cpr_datetime desc
  rows 1
  into :pay_status;

  if (pay_status = -2) then
    return -2;
  else if (pay_status = 2) then
    return 2;
  else
    return 0;

  -- -2 неуспешный перевод на карту клиента
  -- 2 успешный перевод на карту клиента
end
F_GET_PA_ID(PA_COMMAND)→ INTEGER DETERMINISTIC

Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_PA_ID(
    PA_COMMAND STR_100)
RETURNS INTEGER DETERMINISTIC
AS

  select first 1 pa_id
  from permactions
  where pa_command_name = :pa_command
  into :pa_id;
  return pa_id;
end
F_GET_PERC_AVERAGED(K_ID)→ D_PERCENT
K_ID DECIMAL(18,0)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_PERC_AVERAGED(
    K_ID ID_TYPE)
RETURNS D_PERCENT
AS
declare variable k_add_month smallint;
declare variable k_disb_date date;
declare variable kd_ol_date date;
declare variable kd_date_end date;
declare variable add_month_perc d_percent;
declare variable add_month d_percent;
declare variable k_percent d_percent;
declare variable osn_percent d_percent;
declare variable osn_period d_percent;
declare variable k_kont_last_date d_date;
declare variable k_period d_percent;
declare variable rep_sps_averaged d_percent;
begin
  rep_sps_averaged = null;

  select kont.k_add_month, k_disb_date, kont.k_percent, kont.k_kont_last_date
  from kontract kont
  where k_id = :k_id
  into :k_add_month, :k_disb_date, k_percent, k_kont_last_date;

  if (k_add_month > 0 and
      f_get_db_id() in (39801, 41701)) then
  begin
    select kd_ol_date, kd.kd_date_end, kd.kd_percent
    from kontdata kd
    where kd.kd_from_k_id = :k_id and
          kd_ol_is_delete = 0 and
          :k_disb_date between kd_ol_date and kd_date_end
    into kd_ol_date, kd_date_end, add_month_perc;

    if (kd_date_end < k_kont_last_date) then
    begin
      add_month = (kd_date_end - kd_ol_date) / (365.0000000 / 12.0000000);

      select kd.kd_percent
      from kontdata kd
      where kd.kd_from_k_id = :k_id and
            kd_ol_is_delete = 0 and
            :kd_date_end + 1 between kd_ol_date and kd_date_end
      into osn_percent;

      osn_period = (k_kont_last_date - kd_date_end) / (365.0000000 / 12.0000000);

      /* MRAKHMANOV
      Новая методика расчета СПС в системе Финстарт по формуле:
      СПС кредита = (количество месяцев льготного срока*льготную % + количество месяцев основного срока*основную %) / весь срок кредита    
      sps_addmonth = (add_month * add_month_perc + osn_period * osn_percent) / k_period;
                      =(E7*F7/100+G7*H7/100)/D7*100
      Например, (3 мес*15% + 9 мес*30%) / 12 мес = 26,25
      Месяцы можно поменять на количество дней, если так будет удобнее для реализации.     */

      k_period = osn_period + add_month;
      rep_sps_averaged = (add_month * add_month_perc + osn_period * osn_percent) / cast(k_period as double precision);
    end
  end

  rep_sps_averaged = coalesce(rep_sps_averaged, k_percent, 0.0000000);

  return rep_sps_averaged;
end
F_GET_POSITION(UN_ID, ON_DATE)→ D_STR_100
UN_ID BIGINT
ON_DATE DATE
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_POSITION(
    UN_ID D_ID,
    ON_DATE D_DATE = current_date)
RETURNS D_STR_100
AS
declare res_position d_str_100;
begin
  res_position = (
    select
      jl_name
    from username
      inner join hr_pstatus
        on ps_from_c_id = un_from_c_id
      inner join joblist
        on jl_id = ps_from_jl_id
    where un_id = :un_id
      and :on_date between ps_date_begin and ps_date_end
      and ps_status = 1);
  return res_position;
end

F_GET_SAB_ID(ACC_CODE, ACC_FROM_AB_ID, ACC_FROM_CU_ID, ACC_FROM_REG_ID, ACC_FROM_INX_ID, ACC_OBJECT_KIND, ACC_OBJECT_ID)→ D_ID
ACC_CODE VARCHAR(50)
ACC_FROM_AB_ID BIGINT
ACC_FROM_CU_ID VARCHAR(3)
ACC_FROM_REG_ID BIGINT
ACC_FROM_INX_ID BIGINT
ACC_OBJECT_KIND INTEGER
ACC_OBJECT_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_SAB_ID(
    ACC_CODE D_STR_CODE,
    ACC_FROM_AB_ID D_ID,
    ACC_FROM_CU_ID D_STR_LANG,
    ACC_FROM_REG_ID D_ID,
    ACC_FROM_INX_ID D_ID,
    ACC_OBJECT_KIND D_INTEGER,
    ACC_OBJECT_ID D_ID)
RETURNS D_ID
AS
declare variable acc_mask d_str_code;
declare variable code d_str_code;
declare variable n d_integer;
declare variable len d_integer;
declare variable tmp_mask d_str_code;
declare variable ab_code d_str_code;
declare variable sab_id d_id;
declare variable pair_ab_id d_id;
declare variable pos d_integer;
declare variable tmp_acc d_str_code;
begin
  select ab_code, ab_pair_ab_id
  from ref_accbalance
  where ab_id = :acc_from_ab_id
  into ab_code, pair_ab_id;

  if (ab_code is null) then
    exception e_default 'Balance account not defined: ' || coalesce(acc_code, '<null>');

  if (acc_object_id is not null and
      acc_object_kind is null) then
    exception e_default 'Object kind not defined: ' || coalesce(acc_code, '<null>');

  for select sab_id, sab_acc_mask, 1
      from sys_accbalance
      where sab_fixed_ab_id in (:acc_from_ab_id, :pair_ab_id) and
            ((:acc_object_id is null and
            position('X', sab_acc_mask) = 0) or
            :acc_object_id is not null)
      union
      select sab_id, sab_acc_mask, 2
      from sys_accbalance, sys_accballink
      where sab_id = sabl_sab_id and
            (sabl_resident_ab_id in (:acc_from_ab_id, :pair_ab_id) or
            sabl_non_resident_ab_id in (:acc_from_ab_id, :pair_ab_id)) and
            ((:acc_object_id is null and
            position('X', sab_acc_mask) = 0) or
            :acc_object_id is not null)

      union
      select sab_id, sab_acc_mask, 7 - char_length(ab_code)
      from sys_accbalance, ref_accbalance
      where sab_fixed_ab_id = ab_id and
            ab_code <> :ab_code and
            :ab_code starting ab_code and
            ((:acc_object_id is null and
            position('X', sab_acc_mask) = 0) or
            :acc_object_id is not null)
      order by 3, 1
      into sab_id, acc_mask, pos
  do
  begin
    acc_mask = replace(acc_mask, '.', '');
    tmp_acc = left(acc_mask, 5);
    tmp_acc = replace(tmp_acc, 'A', 'B');
    acc_mask = tmp_acc || substring(acc_mask from 6);

    acc_mask = replace(acc_mask, 'A', '_');
    acc_mask = replace(acc_mask, 'N', '_');

    if (position('-', ab_code) > 0) then
      acc_mask = 'RRRRVVVBBBBBNNNNNN'; -- значит это старые счета РФ
    if (acc_mask is null) then
      continue;

    if (position('BBBBB', acc_mask) > 0) then
    begin
      code = ab_code;
      acc_mask = replace(acc_mask, 'BBBBB', code);
    end

    if (position('VVV', acc_mask) > 0 and
        acc_from_cu_id is not null) then
    begin
      code = trim(cast(acc_from_cu_id as varchar(50)));
      acc_mask = replace(acc_mask, 'VVV', code);
    end

    if (position('RRRR', acc_mask) > 0 and
        acc_from_reg_id is not null) then
    begin
      code = null;
      select reg_code
      from region
      where reg_unicode = :acc_from_reg_id
      into code;

      acc_mask = replace(acc_mask, 'RRRR', code);
    end

    if (position('X', acc_mask) > 0 and
        acc_object_id is not null) then
    begin
      code = null;
      tmp_mask = '';
      len = 0;
      n = 0;
      while (n < char_length(acc_mask)) do
      begin
        n = n + 1;
        if (substring(acc_mask from n for 1) = 'X') then
        begin
          tmp_mask = tmp_mask || 'X';
          len = len + 1;
        end
        else
        if (len > 0) then
          break;
      end

      code = trim(cast(acc_object_id as varchar(20)));
      code = right('00000000000000000000' || code, len);

      acc_mask = replace(acc_mask, tmp_mask, code);
    end

    if (position('S', acc_mask) > 0) then
    begin
      code = null;
      tmp_mask = '';
      len = 0;
      n = 0;
      while (n < char_length(acc_mask)) do
      begin
        n = n + 1;
        if (substring(acc_mask from n for 1) = 'S') then
        begin
          tmp_mask = tmp_mask || 'S';
          len = len + 1;
        end
        else
        if (len > 0) then
          break;
      end

      select inx_code
      from ref_income_expense
      where inx_id = :acc_from_inx_id
      into code;

      if (len = 6 and
          char_length(code) = 5) then
        code = code || '0';

      acc_mask = replace(acc_mask, tmp_mask, code);
    end

    if (acc_code = acc_mask) then
      return sab_id;

    if (position('_', acc_mask) > 0) then
      if (acc_code like acc_mask) then
        return sab_id;
  end

  return null;
end
F_GET_STR_ID(PL_ID)→ D_INTEGER
PL_ID DECIMAL(18,0)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_STR_ID(
    PL_ID ID_TYPE)
RETURNS D_INTEGER
AS
declare variable str_id d_integer;
begin
  str_id = null;

  while (str_id is null and pl_id is not null) do
  begin
    -- Проверяем, нашли ли значение
    select str_id
    from statregion
    where str_from_pl_id = :pl_id
    order by str_id desc rows 1
    into str_id;

    -- если нет, берем родительскую ветку и проверим её
    if (str_id is null) then
    begin
      select parent
      from places
      where pl_id = :pl_id
      into :pl_id;
    end
  end
  return str_id;
end
F_GET_TIME(BEG, ENDS)→ D_STR_CODE
BEG TIMESTAMP
ENDS TIMESTAMP
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_TIME(
    BEG TIMESTAMP,
    ENDS TIMESTAMP)
RETURNS D_STR_CODE
AS
declare variable interval double precision;
declare variable hours integer;
declare variable mins integer;
declare variable sec integer;
declare variable days integer;
declare variable in_time str_20;
begin
  interval = beg - ends;
  if (interval < 0) then
    interval = -interval;
  interval = interval * 24;
  if (cast(interval as int) > interval) then
    hours = interval - 1;
  else
    hours = interval;
  interval = (interval - hours) * 60;
  if (cast(interval as int) > interval) then
    mins = interval - 1;
  else
    mins = interval;
  sec = (interval - mins) * 60;
  if (sec = 60) then
    sec = 59;/* милисекунды могут сделать бяку, потому так */
  if (hours > 23) then
  begin
    days = hours / 24;
    hours = hours - days * 24;
  end

  in_time = coalesce(days || '-дн. ', '') || hours || ':' || --
case
  when mins > 9 then mins
  else '0' || mins
end || ':' || --
case
  when sec > 9 then sec
  else '0' || sec
end;
  return in_time;
end
F_GET_VERIFY_MEMO(OBM_MEMO, KIND)→ D_STR_8100
OBM_MEMO VARCHAR(8100)
KIND VARCHAR(50)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GET_VERIFY_MEMO(
    OBM_MEMO D_STR_8100,
    KIND D_STR_CODE)
RETURNS D_STR_8100
AS
declare variable res_text d_str_8100;
declare variable pos_left_bracket d_integer;
declare variable pos_right_bracket d_integer;
declare variable pos_hash d_integer;
begin
  res_text = '';

  pos_left_bracket = position('[', obm_memo);
  if (pos_left_bracket > 0) then
    pos_right_bracket = position(']', obm_memo, pos_left_bracket);
  pos_hash = position('#', obm_memo);

  if (kind = 'reason_memo') then
  begin
    -- Возвращаем причину (в квадратных скобках и текст комментария)
    -- Удаляем код комментария, который находится после решетки в конце строки
    -- Если его нет, возвращаем все, т.к. раньше кода не было, были причина и комментарий
    res_text = obm_memo;
    if (pos_hash > 0) then
      res_text = left(obm_memo, pos_hash - 1);

    return trim(res_text);
  end

  if (kind = 'code') then
  begin
    -- Возвращаем только код комментария, находится после решетки в конце строки
    if (pos_hash > 0) then
      res_text = substring(obm_memo from pos_hash + 1);

    return trim(res_text);
  end

  if (kind = 'reason') then
  begin
    -- Возвращаем только причину, лежит в квадратных скобках, учитываем что могли обрезать справа
    if (pos_right_bracket > 0) then
      res_text = substring(obm_memo from pos_left_bracket + 1 for pos_right_bracket - pos_left_bracket - 1);
    else if (pos_left_bracket > 0) then
      res_text = substring(obm_memo from pos_left_bracket);
    else
      res_text = '';

    return trim(res_text);
  end

  if (kind = 'memo') then
  begin
    res_text = obm_memo;
    if (pos_hash > 0) then
      res_text = left(obm_memo, pos_hash - 1);
    if (pos_right_bracket > 0) then
      res_text = substring(res_text from pos_right_bracket + 1);

    return trim(res_text);
  end
end
F_GPS_DISTANCE(LAT_1, LONG_1, LAT_2, LONG_2)→ D_CURRENCY
Расстояние в метрах между двумя GPS кординатами (Широта/Долгота)
LAT_1 DOUBLE PRECISION
LONG_1 DOUBLE PRECISION
LAT_2 DOUBLE PRECISION
LONG_2 DOUBLE PRECISION
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_GPS_DISTANCE(
    LAT_1 D_DOUBLE,
    LONG_1 D_DOUBLE,
    LAT_2 D_DOUBLE,
    LONG_2 D_DOUBLE)
RETURNS D_CURRENCY
AS
declare variable dist d_double; /* Растояние, в метрах */
declare variable radius d_double; /* Средний радиус */
declare variable tmp d_double;
begin
  -- Функция возвращает расстояние между двумя GPS кординатами (Широта/Долгота)

  /*
  Кратчайшее расстояние между двумя точками на земной поверхности (если принять ее за сферу) определяется зависимостью:
  cos(d) = sin(fА) * sin(fB) + cos(fА) * cos(fB) * cos(lА - lB)
  где
  fА и fB — широты (в радианах),
  lА, lB — долготы данных пунктов (в радианах),
  d — расстояние между пунктами (в радианах)
  перевод в метры осуществляется по формуле:
  L = d * R
  где R = 6372795.0 м — средний радиус земного шара
  */

  dist = 0;
  long_1 = cast(long_1 * pi() as double precision) / cast(180.0 as double precision);
  lat_1 = cast(lat_1 * pi() as double precision) / cast(180.0 as double precision);
  long_2 = cast(long_2 * pi() as double precision) / cast(180.0 as double precision);
  lat_2 = cast(lat_2 * pi() as double precision) / cast(180.0 as double precision);

  if (long_1 + long_2 + lat_1 + lat_2 is null) then
    return 0;
  tmp = sin(lat_1) * sin(lat_2) + cos(lat_1) * cos(lat_2) * cos(long_1 - long_2);
  if (tmp > 1) then tmp = 1;
  if (tmp <-1) then tmp = -1;

  dist = acos(tmp);
  radius = 6372795.0;
  dist = round(dist * radius, 2);

  return dist;
end

F_HQ_ID(DB_ID)→ INTEGER DETERMINISTIC

CREATE FUNCTION F_HQ_ID(
    DB_ID D_INTEGER = null)
RETURNS INTEGER DETERMINISTIC
AS
begin
  if (db_id is null) then
    db_id = f_get_db_id();
 
  if (db_id = 41701) then
      return 1090100;
    else
      return 1010100;
end
F_HTML_TABLE_ROW(VAR1, VAR2, VAR3, VAR4, ALIGN1, ALIGN2, ALIGN3, ALIGN4, COL_COUNT, CLASS_NAME)→ D_BLOB_TEXT
VAR1 VARCHAR(2000)
VAR2 VARCHAR(2000)
VAR3 VARCHAR(2000)
VAR4 VARCHAR(2000)
ALIGN1 INTEGER
ALIGN2 INTEGER
ALIGN3 INTEGER
ALIGN4 INTEGER
COL_COUNT INTEGER
CLASS_NAME VARCHAR(50)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_HTML_TABLE_ROW(
    VAR1 VARCHAR(2000),
    VAR2 VARCHAR(2000),
    VAR3 VARCHAR(2000),
    VAR4 VARCHAR(2000),
    ALIGN1 INTEGER,
    ALIGN2 INTEGER,
    ALIGN3 INTEGER,
    ALIGN4 INTEGER,
    COL_COUNT INTEGER = 4,
    CLASS_NAME D_STR_CODE = '')
RETURNS D_BLOB_TEXT
AS
declare variable a1 d_str_code;
declare variable a2 d_str_code;
declare variable a3 d_str_code;
declare variable a4 d_str_code;
begin
  -- Возвращает строку таблицы HTML
  var1 = coalesce(var1, '');
  var2 = coalesce(var2, '');
  var3 = coalesce(var3, '');
  var4 = coalesce(var4, '');

  a1 = trim(decode(align1, -1, 'left', 1, 'right', 'center'));
  a2 = trim(decode(align2, -1, 'left', 1, 'right', 'center'));
  a3 = trim(decode(align3, -1, 'left', 1, 'right', 'center'));
  a4 = trim(decode(align4, -1, 'left', 1, 'right', 'center'));

  class_name = coalesce(class_name, '');
  if (var2 = '' and var3 = '' and var4 = '') then class_name = 'filled';
  if (class_name <> '') then class_name = ' class="' || class_name || '"';

  if (col_count is null) then col_count = 4;
  if (col_count < 1) then col_count = 1;

  return trim(f_format('<tr><td%1 align="%2">', class_name, a1) || var1 || '</td>') ||
    trim(iif(col_count >= 2, f_format('<td%1 align="%2">', class_name, a2) || var2 || '</td>', '')) ||
    trim(iif(col_count >= 3, f_format('<td%1 align="%2">', class_name, a3) || var3 || '</td>', '')) ||
    trim(iif(col_count >= 4, f_format('<td%1 align="%2">', class_name, a4) || var4 || '</td>', '')) ||
    '</tr>';
end
F_IMAGE_ITEM(ID_KIND, ID, ALT_CAPTION, UN_ID, EXT_FORM, CAN_SAVE, CAN_PRINT)→ D_STR_LONG
ID_KIND VARCHAR(50)
ID BIGINT
ALT_CAPTION VARCHAR(255)
UN_ID BIGINT
EXT_FORM SMALLINT
CAN_SAVE SMALLINT
CAN_PRINT SMALLINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_IMAGE_ITEM(
    ID_KIND D_STR_CODE,
    ID D_ID NOT NULL,
    ALT_CAPTION D_STR_255,
    UN_ID D_ID,
    EXT_FORM D_SMALLINT = 0,
    CAN_SAVE D_SMALLINT = 0,
    CAN_PRINT D_SMALLINT = 0)
RETURNS D_STR_LONG
AS
declare variable img_json d_str_long;
declare variable bls_filename d_str_100;
declare variable bls_detail d_str_255;
declare variable bls_datetime d_datetime;
declare variable bls_caption d_str_255;
declare variable blob_file_pair d_str_255;
declare variable need_ext_blob d_bool;
declare variable edf_is_online d_bool;
declare variable uuid_pair d_str_100;
declare variable edl_id d_id;
begin
  -- Функция возвращает json-строку с параметрами изображения
  -- Данная строка применяется при отображении изображения в окне ImagePreviewForm

  blob_file_pair = '';
  uuid_pair = '';
  if (alt_caption is null)
    then alt_caption = '';
  alt_caption = trim(alt_caption);

  if (trim(coalesce(id_kind, '')) = '') then
    id_kind = 'blobstore';

  bls_caption = alt_caption;

  if (id_kind = 'blobstore') then
  begin
    need_ext_blob = 0;
    select bls_datetime, bls_filename,
           iif(:alt_caption <> '', :alt_caption, bls_caption) || ' / ' || f_blob_kind(bls_source_system, bls_kind),
           iif(bls_blob is null, 1, 0)
    from blobstore
    where bls_id = :id
    into bls_datetime, bls_filename, bls_detail, need_ext_blob;
    -- Тестовая БД может содержать блобы в bls_blob
    -- Если так, не возвращаем blob_db
    if (need_ext_blob = 1) then
      blob_file_pair = ',"blob_db":' || f_json_escape(f_db_blob_name(id), 'str');
  end else
  if (id_kind = 'edoc_blank') then
  begin
    bls_filename = id || '.pdf';
    bls_detail = alt_caption;

    select edl_id, edl_create_datetime, edf_is_online
    from edoc_blank, edoc_list, edoc_frx
    where edb_id = :id and
          edb_from_edl_id = edl_id and
          edb_from_edf_id = edf_id
    into edl_id, bls_datetime, edf_is_online;
    if (edf_is_online = 1) then
    begin
      id_kind = 'edoc_blank_online';
      select f_format(',"uuid":"%1"', f_uuid('edoc_sign', eds_id))
      from edoc_sign
      where eds_from_edl_id = :edl_id
        and eds_from_c_id is not null
      rows 1
      into uuid_pair;
    end
  end else
    exception e_default 'Unknown id_kind: ' || id_kind;

  if (bls_datetime is not null) then
    bls_detail = bls_detail || ' ' || f_datetimetostr(bls_datetime);

  if (bls_caption = '') then
    bls_caption = bls_detail;

  img_json = f_format('{"id_kind":"%1","id":%2,"detail":"%3","caption":"%4","file_name":"%5"', id_kind, id, bls_detail, bls_caption, bls_filename) ||
             f_format(',"ext_form":%1,"can_save":%2,"can_print":%3%4%5}', ext_form, can_save, can_print, blob_file_pair, uuid_pair);

  return img_json;
end
F_IPF_NEWREPMODE(REP_DATE, K_ID, IS_WRITEOFF)→ D_BOOL
REP_DATE DATE
K_ID BIGINT
IS_WRITEOFF SMALLINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_IPF_NEWREPMODE(
    REP_DATE D_DATE,
    K_ID D_ID,
    IS_WRITEOFF D_SMALLINT = null)
RETURNS D_BOOL
AS
declare variable res_id d_bool;
declare variable start_date d_date;
begin
  res_id = 0;

  if (coalesce(is_writeoff, 0) = 1) then
    return res_id;

  start_date = cast('01.03.2026' as date);     -- Начала действия нового алгоритма

  if (rep_date < start_date or                 -- Дата нового алгоритма не наступил
      f_get_db_id() <> 41701) then             -- Действует только для KG
    return res_id;

  if (exists(select * from kontract, product
             where k_id = :k_id and
                   k_disb_date >= cast('10.11.2025' as date) and -- Выданные с 10.11.1025
                   k_from_pr_id = pr_id and
                   pr_percent_kind = 2)) then                    -- ИПФ
  begin
    if (exists(select * from knclient
               where kc_from_k_id = :k_id and
                     kc_loan_target = 63 and       -- Потреб.кредиты (KnClient.KC_LOAN_TARGET)
                     kc_amount_approved > 0)) then
    res_id = 1;
  end

  return res_id;
end
F_IS_ASP_CLIENT(C_ID)→ INTEGER
C_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_IS_ASP_CLIENT(
    C_ID D_ID)
RETURNS INTEGER
AS
declare variable JSON_TEXT D_BLOB_TEXT;
declare variable BLS_ID D_ID;
declare variable STATE D_STR_64;
begin
  select eil.eil_from_bls_id
  from income_check_querys icq
  inner join extinfolist eil on eil.eil_id = icq.icq_from_eil_id
  where icq.icq_from_c_id = :c_id and
        icq.icq_status = 2 and
        icq.icq_config = 'asp_check'
  order by icq.icq_status_datetime desc
  rows 1
  into :bls_id;

  select cast(blx_blob as d_blob_text)
  from hp_get_blobex_blob(:bls_id)
  into json_text;

  if (row_count is null or coalesce(json_text, '') = '') then
    return -2;

  state = f_json_str_get_value(:json_text, 'state', '');

  if (state containing 'ASP') then
    return 1;
  else if (state containing 'NO') then
    return 0;

end

  -- ПДН; Профсуждение;
  if (exists(
    select *
    from kk_income
    where ki_from_k_id = :k_id
      and ki_is_manual = 0))
  then

  return is_auto;
end

F_IS_STOPCRED_ACTUAL(SERVICE_ANSWER_DATE)→ INTEGER
SERVICE_ANSWER_DATE DATE
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_IS_STOPCRED_ACTUAL(
    SERVICE_ANSWER_DATE D_DATE)
RETURNS INTEGER
AS
declare variable stop_credit_info_actual_days d_integer;
begin
    /*
        Функция проверяет актуальность полученного ответа от сервиса стоп-кредит
    */

    select eic.eic_info_actual_days from extinfocfg eic where eic.eic_code = 'stop_credit'
    into :stop_credit_info_actual_days;
    
    return iif(datediff(day, :service_answer_date, current_date) <= :stop_credit_info_actual_days, 1, 0);
end
F_IS_STOPCRED_DOC_LOADED(OBJ_KIND, OBJ_ID)→ SMALLINT
OBJ_KIND INTEGER
OBJ_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_IS_STOPCRED_DOC_LOADED(
    OBJ_KIND D_INTEGER,
    OBJ_ID D_ID)
RETURNS SMALLINT
AS
declare variable C_ID D_ID;
declare variable STOP_CREDIT_PK_ID D_INTEGER;
declare variable IS_DOC_LOADED D_SMALLINT;
begin
    -- RFCKZ-23
    is_doc_loaded = 0;

    if (obj_kind = f_objkind('miniapp', 'id')) then
    begin
        select mac.mac_from_c_id
        from miniappclient mac
        where
            mac.mac_from_ma_id = :obj_id and
            mac.mac_is_cashier = 1
        rows 1
        into :c_id;
    end
    else if (obj_kind = f_objkind('kontract', 'id')) then
    begin
        select kc.kc_from_c_id
        from knclient kc
        where
            kc.kc_from_k_id = :obj_id and
            kc.kc_amount_approved > 0 and
            kc.kc_status = 2 -- Казначей
        rows 1
        into :c_id;
    end
    else
        exception e_default f_format('Invalid obj_kind. Expected: miniapp/kontract, actual: %1', f_objkind(:obj_kind, 'name'));

    select pkk.pk_id
    from passkind pkk
    inner join passdoctype pdt on pkk.pk_from_pdt_id = pdt.pdt_id
    where
        pkk.pk_country_id = (select rd.rd_id from refdata rd where rd.rd_code = 'Citizen.KZ')
        and pdt.pdt_code = 'stop_cred'
        into :stop_credit_pk_id;

    if (exists(
                select 1
                from clientdocs cds
                where
                    cds.cds_from_c_id = :c_id and
                    cds.cds_from_pk_id = :stop_credit_pk_id and
                    cds.cds_is_delete = 0 and
                    f_is_stopcred_actual(cast(cds.cds_datetime as date)) = 1 -- Дата добавления документа должна быть актуальной
                )
    ) then
        is_doc_loaded = 1;

    return is_doc_loaded;
end
F_IS_VALID_DATE(DATE_STR, NOT_NULL)→ D_BOOL
DATE_STR VARCHAR(50)
NOT_NULL SMALLINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_IS_VALID_DATE(
    DATE_STR D_STR_CODE,
    NOT_NULL D_BOOL = 0)
RETURNS D_BOOL
AS
declare variable dd d_date;
begin
  -- Функция возвращает 1, если в date_str передана валидная дата, или 0, если невалидная
  -- Если в date_str передан null, то результат зависит от параметра not_null
  -- not_null = 1, date_str = null, результат 0
  -- not_null = 0, date_str = null, результат 1
  -- По-умолчанию not_null = 0

  not_null = coalesce(not_null, 0);
  if (date_str is null and not_null = 1) then
    return 0;
  begin
    dd = date_str;
    return 1;
  when sqlcode -413 do -- convert_error
    return 0;
  end
  return 1;
end

    PR_ID INTEGER)
