             from product
             where pr_id = :pr_id and
                   pr_code containing '.ipf.')) then
    res = 'yes';
  else
    res = 'no';
  return res;
end
F_JIRA_ADD(DATETIME_START, ADD_WORK_SECOND)→ D_DATETIME
DATETIME_START TIMESTAMP
ADD_WORK_SECOND INTEGER
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_JIRA_ADD(
    DATETIME_START D_DATETIME,
    ADD_WORK_SECOND D_INTEGER)
RETURNS D_DATETIME
AS
declare variable db_id d_integer;
declare variable day1_begin d_time;
declare variable day1_end d_time;
declare variable day2_begin d_time;
declare variable day2_end d_time;
declare variable tmp_start_date d_date;
declare variable tmp_start_time d_time;
declare variable tmp_delta d_integer;
declare variable res d_datetime;
declare variable is_workday d_bool;
begin
  db_id = f_get_db_id();

  if (db_id in (39801, 41701)) then
  begin
    day1_begin = '8:30';
    day1_end = '12:00';

    day2_begin = '13:00';
    day2_end = '17:30';
  end else
  begin
    day1_begin = '9:00';
    day1_end = '13:00';

    day2_begin = '14:00';
    day2_end = '18:00';
  end

  tmp_start_date = cast(datetime_start as date);
  tmp_start_time = cast(datetime_start as time);

  if (tmp_start_time < day1_begin) then
    tmp_start_time = day1_begin;

  if (tmp_start_time between day1_end and day2_begin) then
    tmp_start_time = day2_begin;

  if (tmp_start_time > day2_end) then
    tmp_start_time = day2_end;

  if (add_work_second < 0) then
    add_work_second = 0;

  while (add_work_second > 0) do
  begin
    is_workday = f_jira_workday(tmp_start_date);

    if (is_workday = 1) then
    begin
      if (add_work_second > 0 and tmp_start_time between day1_begin and day1_end) then
      begin
        tmp_delta = datediff(second, tmp_start_time, day1_end);
        if (tmp_delta < add_work_second) then
        begin
          tmp_start_time = day2_begin;
          add_work_second = add_work_second - tmp_delta;
        end else
        begin
          tmp_start_time = dateadd(second, add_work_second, tmp_start_time);
          add_work_second = 0;
        end
      end
    
      if (add_work_second > 0 and tmp_start_time between day2_begin and day2_end) then
      begin
        tmp_delta = datediff(second, tmp_start_time, day2_end);
        if (tmp_delta < add_work_second) then
        begin
          tmp_start_time = day1_begin;
          add_work_second = add_work_second - tmp_delta;
        end else
        begin
          tmp_start_time = dateadd(second, add_work_second, tmp_start_time);
          add_work_second = 0;
        end
      end
    end else
      tmp_start_time = day1_begin;

    if (add_work_second > 0) then
      tmp_start_date = dateadd(day, 1, tmp_start_date);
  end

  tmp_delta = (extract(hour from tmp_start_time) * 60 + extract(minute from tmp_start_time)) * 60 + extract(second from tmp_start_time);
  res = tmp_start_date + tmp_start_time;

  return res;
end
F_JIRA_DIFF(DATETIME_BEGIN, DATETIME_END, DEV_TIMEZONE)→ D_INTEGER
DATETIME_BEGIN TIMESTAMP
DATETIME_END TIMESTAMP
DEV_TIMEZONE SMALLINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_JIRA_DIFF(
    DATETIME_BEGIN D_DATETIME,
    DATETIME_END D_DATETIME,
    DEV_TIMEZONE D_SMALLINT)
RETURNS D_INTEGER
AS
declare variable tmp_delta d_integer;
declare variable delta d_integer;
declare variable db_id d_integer;
declare variable day_begin d_time;
declare variable day_end d_time;
declare variable lunch_begin d_time;
declare variable lunch_end d_time;
declare variable tmp_begin_time d_time;
declare variable tmp_begin_date d_date;
declare variable tmp_end_time d_time;
declare variable tmp_end_date d_date;
declare variable tmp_date d_date;
declare variable diff_timezone d_smallint;
begin
  delta = 0;

  if (datetime_begin is null or datetime_end is null or datetime_begin is not distinct from datetime_end) then
    return delta;

  if (datetime_begin >= datetime_end) then
    return delta;

  db_id = f_get_db_id();

  if (dev_timezone is null) then
    dev_timezone = 0;

  diff_timezone = 0;
  if (db_id in (39801, 41701)) then
  begin
    day_begin = '8:30';
    day_end = '17:30';
    lunch_begin = '12:00';
    lunch_end = '13:00';

    if (dev_timezone <> 0) then
      diff_timezone = dev_timezone - 6;
  end else
  begin
    day_begin = '9:00';
    day_end = '18:00';
    lunch_begin = '13:00';
    lunch_end = '14:00';

    if (dev_timezone <> 0) then
      diff_timezone = dev_timezone - 3;
  end

  if (diff_timezone <> 0) then
  begin
    datetime_begin = dateadd(hour, diff_timezone, datetime_begin);
    datetime_end = dateadd(hour, diff_timezone, datetime_end);
  end

  tmp_begin_date = cast(datetime_begin as date);
  tmp_begin_time = cast(datetime_begin as time);
  tmp_end_date = cast(datetime_end as date);
  tmp_end_time = cast(datetime_end as time);

  if (tmp_begin_time < day_begin or f_jira_workday(tmp_begin_date) = 0) then
    tmp_begin_time = day_begin;
  else
  if (tmp_begin_time > day_end) then
    tmp_begin_time = day_end;

  if (tmp_end_time < day_begin) then
    tmp_end_time = day_begin;
  else
  if (tmp_end_time > day_end) then
    tmp_end_time = day_end;

  tmp_date = tmp_begin_date;
  while (tmp_date <= tmp_end_date) do
  begin
    if (f_jira_workday(tmp_date) = 1) then
      delta = delta + 8 * 60 * 60;

    tmp_date = tmp_date + 1;
  end

  if (f_jira_workday(tmp_begin_date) = 1) then
  begin
    tmp_delta = datediff(second, day_begin, tmp_begin_time);

    if (tmp_begin_time > lunch_begin) then
      tmp_delta = tmp_delta - datediff(second, lunch_begin, minvalue(tmp_begin_time, lunch_end));

    delta = delta - tmp_delta;
  end

  if (f_jira_workday(tmp_end_date) = 1) then
  begin
    tmp_delta = datediff(second, tmp_end_time, day_end);

    if (tmp_end_time < lunch_end) then
      tmp_delta = tmp_delta - datediff(second, maxvalue(tmp_end_time, lunch_begin), lunch_end);

    delta = delta - tmp_delta;
  end

  if (delta = 0) then
    delta = 1;

  return delta;
end
F_JIRA_PERIOD(IN_SECONDS)→ D_STR_CODE
IN_SECONDS INTEGER
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_JIRA_PERIOD(
    IN_SECONDS D_INTEGER)
RETURNS D_STR_CODE
AS
declare variable res_str d_str_code;
declare variable tmp_number d_integer;
begin
  res_str = '';

  tmp_number = mod(in_seconds, 60);
  if (tmp_number <> 0) then
    res_str = tmp_number || 's'  || trim(res_str);
  in_seconds = trunc(in_seconds / 60);

  tmp_number = mod(in_seconds, 60);
  if (tmp_number <> 0) then
    res_str = tmp_number || 'm'  || trim(res_str);
  in_seconds = trunc(in_seconds / 60);

  tmp_number = mod(in_seconds, 8);
  if (tmp_number <> 0) then
    res_str = tmp_number || 'h'  || trim(res_str);
  in_seconds = trunc(in_seconds / 8);

  tmp_number = mod(in_seconds, 5);
  if (tmp_number <> 0) then
    res_str = tmp_number || 'd'  || trim(res_str);
  in_seconds = trunc(in_seconds / 5);

  if (in_seconds <> 0) then
    res_str = in_seconds || 'w'  || trim(res_str);

  res_str = replace(res_str, 'w', 'w ');
  res_str = replace(res_str, 'd', 'd ');
  res_str = replace(res_str, 'h', 'h ');
  res_str = replace(res_str, 'm', 'm ');
  res_str = replace(res_str, 's', 's ');
  res_str = trim(res_str);

  if (res_str = '') then
    res_str = '-';

  return res_str;
end
F_JIRA_WORKDAY(CHECK_DATE)→ D_BOOL
CHECK_DATE DATE
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_JIRA_WORKDAY(
    CHECK_DATE D_DATE)
RETURNS D_BOOL
AS
begin
  if (exists(select *
      from sa_calendays
      where scd_work_hours = 0 and
            scd_date = :check_date)) then
    return 0;
  else
    return 1;
end
F_JSON_BLOB_GET_COUNT(STR_OBJECT, ARRAY_PATH)→ D_INTEGER
Возврщает количество элементов массива внутри JSON, входящий параметр BLOB TEXT
STR_OBJECT BLOB SUB_TYPE TEXT SEGMENT SIZE 80
ARRAY_PATH VARCHAR(1024)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_JSON_BLOB_GET_COUNT(
    STR_OBJECT D_BLOB_TEXT,
    ARRAY_PATH D_STR_LONG)
RETURNS D_INTEGER
AS
begin
  -- Возвращает количество элементов массива, или -1, если не найден массив
  -- Названия объектов в array_path разделяются точкой.
  -- Если объект является массивом, после названия объекта в квадратных скобках нужно указать номер элемента.
  -- Первый элемент начинается с 0
  -- Пример ключа: Glossary.GlossList[2].id

  return udf_json_blob_get_count(str_object, array_path);
end
F_JSON_BLOB_GET_VALUE(STR_OBJECT, PATH, VALUE_IF_NOT_FOUND)→ D_STR_8100
STR_OBJECT BLOB SUB_TYPE TEXT SEGMENT SIZE 80
PATH VARCHAR(1024)
VALUE_IF_NOT_FOUND VARCHAR(255)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_JSON_BLOB_GET_VALUE(
    STR_OBJECT D_BLOB_TEXT,
    PATH D_STR_LONG,
    VALUE_IF_NOT_FOUND D_STR_255)
RETURNS D_STR_8100
AS
declare variable json_value d_str_8100;
declare variable marker d_str_255;
declare variable err_marker d_str_255;
declare variable null_marker d_str_255;
begin
-- Возвращает значение JSON по ключу.
-- Ключ должен содержать полный путь начиная самого первого объекта.
-- Названия объектов разделяются точкой.
-- Если объект является массивом, после названия объекта в квадратных скобках нужно указать номер элемента.
-- Первый элемент начинается с 0
-- Пример ключа: Glossary.GlossList[2].id

  marker = '!ZCXdEZ9AaxBsVY';
  err_marker = 'Err' || marker;
  null_marker = 'Null' || marker;

  json_value = udf_json_blob_get(str_object, path);
  if (json_value = null_marker) then
    json_value = value_if_not_found;
  else if (json_value starting err_marker) then
    exception e_default substring(json_value from char_length(err_marker) + 1);
  return json_value;
end
F_JSON_BLOB_SET_VALUE(STR_OBJECT, PATH, NEW_VALUE)→ D_BLOB_TEXT
Редактирует значение ключа и возвращает новый JSON. / Если ключ не найден, добавляет пару ключ-значение
STR_OBJECT BLOB SUB_TYPE TEXT SEGMENT SIZE 80
PATH VARCHAR(1024)
NEW_VALUE VARCHAR(8100)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_JSON_BLOB_SET_VALUE(
    STR_OBJECT D_BLOB_TEXT,
    PATH D_STR_LONG,
    NEW_VALUE D_STR_8100)
RETURNS D_BLOB_TEXT
AS
declare variable json_value d_blob_text;
declare variable marker d_str_255;
declare variable err_marker d_str_255;
begin
-- Заменяет значение JSON по ключу.
-- Ключ должен содержать полный путь начиная самого первого объекта.
-- Названия объектов разделяются точкой.
-- Если объект является массивом, после названия объекта в квадратных скобках нужно указать номер элемента.
-- Первый элемент начинается с 0
-- Пример ключа: Glossary.GlossList[2].id

  marker = '!ZCXdEZ9AaxBsVY';
  err_marker = 'Err' || marker;

  json_value = udf_json_blob_set(str_object, path, new_value);
  if (json_value starting err_marker) then
    exception e_default substring(json_value from char_length(err_marker) + 1);
  return json_value;
end
F_JSON_ESCAPE(JSON_VALUE, JSON_DATATYPE)→ D_STR_8100
JSON_VALUE VARCHAR(8100)
JSON_DATATYPE VARCHAR(50)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_JSON_ESCAPE(
    JSON_VALUE D_STR_8100,
    JSON_DATATYPE D_STR_CODE)
RETURNS D_STR_8100
AS
declare variable txt d_str_8100;
declare variable err d_integer;
declare variable big_num d_double;
declare variable dd d_date;
declare variable datatype d_str_code;
declare variable dt d_datetime;
begin
  txt = '';
  err = 0;

  if (json_datatype in ('array', 'object')) then
    return json_value;

  if (coalesce(json_datatype, '') = '') then
    datatype = '';
  else
  if (json_datatype in ('str', 'string', 'memo', 'blob') or json_datatype starting('d_str_') or json_datatype starting('d_blob_')) then
    datatype = 'str';
  else
  if (json_datatype in ('date', 'd_date')) then
    datatype = 'date';
  else
  if (json_datatype in ('datetime', 'd_datetime')) then
    datatype = 'datetime';
  else
    datatype = 'num';

  if (datatype = '') then
    exception e_default 'Invalid data type: ' || datatype;

  if (json_value = '') then
    txt = '""';
  else
  if (json_value is null) then
    txt = 'null';
  else
  if (datatype = 'num') then
  begin
    big_num = cast(json_value as double precision);
    txt = json_value;
  end
  else
  if (datatype = 'date') then
  begin
    dd = cast(json_value as date);
    json_value = f_datetostr(dd);
    txt = '"' || json_value || '"';
  end
  else
  if (datatype = 'datetime') then
  begin
    dt = cast(json_value as timestamp);
    json_value = f_datetimetostr(dt);
    txt = '"' || json_value || '"';
  end
  else
  if (datatype = 'str') then
  begin
    json_value = replace(json_value, '\', '\\');
    json_value = replace(json_value, '"', '\"');
    json_value = replace(json_value, '/', '\/');
    json_value = replace(json_value, ascii_char(8), '\b');
    json_value = replace(json_value, ascii_char(12), '\f');
    json_value = replace(json_value, ascii_char(10), '\n');
    json_value = replace(json_value, ascii_char(13), '\r');
    json_value = replace(json_value, ascii_char(9), '\t');
    txt = '"' || json_value || '"';
  end
  else
  begin
    begin
      dd = cast(json_value as date);
      json_value = f_datetostr(dd);
      txt = '"' || json_value || '"';
      when any do
        err = 1;
    end
    if (err = 1) then
    begin
      if (left(json_value, 1) <> '0') then
      begin
        err = 0;
        big_num = cast(json_value as double precision);
        txt = json_value;
        when any do
          err = 1;
      end
    end
    if (err = 1) then
    begin
      json_value = replace(json_value, '\', '\\');
      json_value = replace(json_value, '"', '\"');
      txt = '"' || json_value || '"';
    end
  end
  return txt;
end
F_JSON_INFO(OBJ_KIND, OBJ_ID, OBJ_FIELDS)→ D_STR_8100
OBJ_KIND VARCHAR(50)
OBJ_ID BIGINT
OBJ_FIELDS VARCHAR(1024)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_JSON_INFO(
    OBJ_KIND D_STR_CODE,
    OBJ_ID D_ID,
    OBJ_FIELDS D_STR_LONG)
RETURNS D_STR_8100
AS
declare variable sql_text d_str_long;
declare variable json_value d_str_8100;
declare variable field_name d_str_code;
declare variable select_field d_str_long;
declare variable key_field d_str_code;
begin
  -- Функция возвращает значения полей в формате JSON.
  -- Список полей передается в параметре OBJ_FIELDS через запятую

  json_value = '';
  if (trim(coalesce(obj_fields, '')) = '') then
    return json_value;

  key_field = f_objkind(obj_kind, 'field');

  sql_text = '';
  for select trim(res_str)
  from hp_split_str(:obj_fields, ',')
  into :field_name
  do
  begin
    select_field = 'f_json_make_pair(''' || field_name || ''', ' || field_name || ', ''str'')';
    if (sql_text <> '') then
      sql_text = sql_text || ' || '', '' || ';

    sql_text = sql_text || select_field;
  end

  if (sql_text <> '') then
  begin
    sql_text = 'select ' || sql_text || ' from ' || obj_kind || ' where '|| key_field || ' = ' || obj_id;
    json_value = sql_text;
    execute statement (sql_text)
    into json_value;

    if (trim(coalesce(json_value, '')) <> '') then
      json_value = '{' || json_value || '}';
  end

  return json_value;
end
F_JSON_MAKE_PAIR(JSON_KEY, JSON_VALUE, JSON_DATATYPE, ADD_COMMA)→ D_STR_8100
JSON_KEY VARCHAR(255)
JSON_VALUE VARCHAR(8100)
JSON_DATATYPE VARCHAR(50)
ADD_COMMA SMALLINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_JSON_MAKE_PAIR(
    JSON_KEY D_STR_255,
    JSON_VALUE D_STR_8100,
    JSON_DATATYPE D_STR_CODE,
    ADD_COMMA D_BOOL = 0)
RETURNS D_STR_8100
AS
declare variable res_str d_str_8100;
begin
  if (coalesce(json_key, '') = '') then
    exception e_default 'Json key is emptry';

  res_str = f_json_escape(json_key, 'str') || ':' || f_json_escape(json_value, json_datatype);
  if (add_comma = 1) then
    res_str = res_str || ',';

  return res_str;
end
F_JSON_STR_GET_COUNT(STR_OBJECT, ARRAY_PATH)→ D_INTEGER
Возврщает количество элементов массива внутри JSON, входящий параметр STR 8100
STR_OBJECT VARCHAR(8100)
ARRAY_PATH VARCHAR(1024)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_JSON_STR_GET_COUNT(
    STR_OBJECT D_STR_8100,
    ARRAY_PATH D_STR_LONG)
RETURNS D_INTEGER
AS
begin
  -- Возвращает количество элементов массива, или -1, если не найден массив
  -- Названия объектов в array_path разделяются точкой.
  -- Если объект является массивом, после названия объекта в квадратных скобках нужно указать номер элемента.
  -- Первый элемент начинается с 0
  -- Пример ключа: Glossary.GlossList[2].id

  return udf_json_str_get_count(str_object, array_path);
end
F_JSON_STR_GET_VALUE(STR_OBJECT, PATH, VALUE_IF_NOT_FOUND)→ D_STR_8100
STR_OBJECT VARCHAR(8100)
PATH VARCHAR(1024)
VALUE_IF_NOT_FOUND VARCHAR(255)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_JSON_STR_GET_VALUE(
    STR_OBJECT D_STR_8100,
    PATH D_STR_LONG,
    VALUE_IF_NOT_FOUND D_STR_255)
RETURNS D_STR_8100
AS
declare variable json_value d_str_8100;
declare variable marker d_str_255;
declare variable err_marker d_str_255;
declare variable null_marker d_str_255;
begin
-- Возвращает значение JSON по ключу.
-- Ключ должен содержать полный путь начиная самого первого объекта.
-- Названия объектов разделяются точкой.
-- Если объект является массивом, после названия объекта в квадратных скобках нужно указать номер элемента.
-- Первый элемент начинается с 0
-- Пример ключа: Glossary.GlossList[2].id

  marker = '!ZCXdEZ9AaxBsVY';
  err_marker = 'Err' || marker;
  null_marker = 'Null' || marker;

  json_value = udf_json_str_get(str_object, path);
  if (json_value = null_marker) then
    json_value = value_if_not_found;
  else if (json_value starting err_marker) then
    exception e_default substring(json_value from char_length(err_marker) + 1);
  return json_value;
end
F_JSON_STR_SET_VALUE(STR_OBJECT, PATH, NEW_VALUE)→ D_STR_8100
Редактирует значение ключа и возвращает новый JSON. / Если ключ не найден, добавляет пару ключ-значение
STR_OBJECT VARCHAR(8100)
PATH VARCHAR(1024)
NEW_VALUE VARCHAR(8100)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_JSON_STR_SET_VALUE(
    STR_OBJECT D_STR_8100,
    PATH D_STR_LONG,
    NEW_VALUE D_STR_8100)
RETURNS D_STR_8100
AS
declare variable json_value d_str_8100;
declare variable marker d_str_255;
declare variable err_marker d_str_255;
begin
-- Заменяет значение JSON по ключу.
-- Ключ должен содержать полный путь начиная самого первого объекта.
-- Названия объектов разделяются точкой.
-- Если объект является массивом, после названия объекта в квадратных скобках нужно указать номер элемента.
-- Первый элемент начинается с 0
-- Пример ключа: Glossary.GlossList[2].id

  marker = '!ZCXdEZ9AaxBsVY';
  err_marker = 'Err' || marker;

  json_value = udf_json_str_set(str_object, path, new_value);
  if (json_value starting err_marker) then
    exception e_default substring(json_value from char_length(err_marker) + 1);
  return json_value;
end
F_KEYNAME(TABLE_NAME, KEY_ID)→ D_STR_LONG
TABLE_NAME VARCHAR(50)
KEY_ID VARCHAR(50)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_KEYNAME(
    TABLE_NAME D_STR_CODE NOT NULL,
    KEY_ID D_STR_CODE)
RETURNS D_STR_LONG
AS
declare variable res_text d_str_long;
declare variable sql d_str_8100;
begin
  -- Функция возвращает наименование объекта по его ID

  res_text = '';

  if (coalesce(key_id, '') = '') then
    return res_text;

  -- Если имя таблицы передан через номер UxTable, пробудем преобразовать в название таблицы
  if (table_name similar to '[-]?[[:DIGIT:]]+') then
    table_name = f_objkind(table_name, 'name');

  select ukn_sql
  from uxkeyname
  where ukn_table_name = :table_name
  into sql;

  if (sql <> '') then
  begin
    execute statement(:sql) (id := :key_id)
    into res_text;

    if (res_text is null) then
      res_text = '';
  end else
    exception e_default f_format('Table "%1" not found in UxKeyName table', table_name);

  return res_text;
end
F_KNCLIENT_DATA(KC_ID, EL_CODE, VALUE_IF_NOT_FOUND)→ D_STR_CODE
KC_ID BIGINT
EL_CODE VARCHAR(50)
VALUE_IF_NOT_FOUND VARCHAR(50)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_KNCLIENT_DATA(
    KC_ID D_ID,
    EL_CODE D_STR_CODE,
    VALUE_IF_NOT_FOUND D_STR_CODE = null)
RETURNS D_STR_CODE
AS
declare variable knd_value d_str_code;
begin
  select knd_value
  from knclient_data
  where knd_from_kc_id = :kc_id
    and knd_from_el_id = f_enum_id('knclient_data', :el_code)
  order by knd_id desc
  rows 1
  into knd_value;

  if (knd_value is null) then
    return value_if_not_found;
  else
    return knd_value;
end
F_KN_DETAIL_ID(TEXT)→ D_ID
TEXT VARCHAR(255)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_KN_DETAIL_ID(
    TEXT D_STR_255)
RETURNS D_ID
AS
declare variable id d_id;
begin
  -- Функция возвращает ID  текста переданного в параметре.

  if (text is null) then
    text = '';

  select obd_id
  from kn_obdetail
  where obd_detail = :text
  into id;

  if (id is null) then
  begin
    insert into kn_obdetail (obd_detail)
    values (:text)
    returning obd_id
    into :id;
  end

  return id;
end
F_LANG(LANG_CODE, TEXT)→ D_STR_255
LANG_CODE VARCHAR(3)
TEXT VARCHAR(255)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_LANG(
    LANG_CODE D_STR_LANG,
    TEXT D_STR_255)
RETURNS D_STR_255
AS
declare variable res_text d_str_255;
declare variable need_translit d_smallint;
declare variable trm_id d_id;
begin
  res_text = text;
  if (coalesce(lang_code, '') = '') then
    lang_code = f_get_lang_code(null, null);

  if (lang_code = 'ru') then
  begin
    return res_text;
    exit;
  end

  need_translit = 0;
  if (lang_code = 'uzl') then
  begin
    lang_code = 'uz';
    need_translit = 1;
  end

  select trl_translate_text
  from sys_transmain left join sys_translate
       on (trm_id = trl_from_trm_id and trl_lang_code = :lang_code)
  where trm_orig_text = :text
  into res_text;

  if (row_count = 0) then
  begin
    insert into sys_transmain (trm_orig_text)
    values (:text)
    returning trm_id
    into :trm_id;

    when any do
    begin
    end
  end

  if (coalesce(res_text, '') = '') then
    res_text = text;

  if (need_translit = 1) then
    res_text = f_translit('cyr', 'lat', res_text, '');

  return res_text;
end
F_LEVENSTAIN(S1, S2, MAX_DISTANCE)→ D_INTEGER
S1 VARCHAR(255)
S2 VARCHAR(255)
MAX_DISTANCE INTEGER
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_LEVENSTAIN(
    S1 D_STR_255,
    S2 D_STR_255,
    MAX_DISTANCE D_INTEGER)
RETURNS D_INTEGER
AS
declare variable res d_integer;
declare variable m d_integer;
declare variable n d_integer;
declare variable s_tmp d_str_code;
declare variable s_int d_integer;
declare variable sum_cost d_integer;
declare variable cost d_integer;
declare variable ss d_str_255;
declare variable diff_found d_integer;
declare variable n_pos d_integer;
declare variable i d_integer;
declare variable d1 d_str_long;
declare variable d2 d_str_long;
declare variable j d_integer;
begin
  -- Вычисляет дистанцию Левенштейна между двумя строками
  if (char_length(s2) < char_length(s1)) then
  begin
    ss = s1;
    s1 = s2;
    s2 = ss;
  end

  m = char_length(s1);
  n = char_length(s2);
  if (s1 = s2) then
  begin
    res = 0;
    return res;
  end

  if (m <= 2 or
      n <= 2) then
  begin
    res = 255;
    return res;
  end

  if (abs(n - m) > :max_distance and
      max_distance <> -1) then
  begin
    res = 255;
    return res;
  end
  res = 0;
  i = 0;
  d1 = '';
  d2 = '';
  while (i <= n) do
  begin
    d2 = d2 || ' ' || right('0' || i, 2);
    i = i + 1;
  end
  i = 1;
  n_pos = 0;
  while (i <= m) do
  begin
    d1 = d2;
    diff_found = 0;
    j = 0;
    while (j <= n) do
    begin
      if (j = 0) then
        d2 = overlay(d2 placing ' ' || right('0' || i, 2) from (j * 3) + 1);
      else
      begin
        cost = 1;
        if (substring(s1 from i for 1) = substring(s2 from j for 1)) then
          cost = 0;

        if (substring(d2 from (j - 1) * 3 + 1 for 3) < substring(d1 from j * 3 + 1 for 3) and
            (cast(substring(d2 from (j - 1) * 3 + 1 for 3) as integer) < cast(substring(d1 from (j - 1) * 3 + 1 for 3) as integer) + cost)) then
        begin
          s_int = cast(substring(d2 from (j - 1) * 3 + 1 for 3) as integer) + 1;
          s_tmp = ' ' || right('0' || s_int, 2);

          d2 = overlay(d2 placing s_tmp from (j * 3) + 1);
        end
        else
        begin
          if (cast(substring(d1 from j * 3 + 1 for 3) as integer) < cast(substring(d1 from (j - 1) * 3 + 1 for 3) as integer) + cost) then
          begin
            s_int = cast(substring(d1 from j * 3 + 1 for 3) as integer) + 1;
            s_tmp = ' ' || right('0' || s_int, 2);
            d2 = overlay(d2 placing s_tmp from (j * 3) + 1);
          end
          else
          begin
            s_int = cast(substring(d1 from (j - 1) * 3 + 1 for 3) as integer) + cost;
            s_tmp = ' ' || right('0' || s_int, 2);
            d2 = overlay(d2 placing s_tmp from (j * 3) + 1);
          end
        end
      end
      j = j + 1;
    end
    i = i + 1;
  end
  res = cast(substring(d2 from n * 3 + 1 for 3) as integer);
  return res;
end
F_LEVENSTAIN2(S1, S2, MAX_DISTANCE)→ D_INTEGER
S1 VARCHAR(255)
S2 VARCHAR(255)
MAX_DISTANCE INTEGER
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_LEVENSTAIN2(
    S1 D_STR_255,
    S2 D_STR_255,
    MAX_DISTANCE D_INTEGER = -1)
RETURNS D_INTEGER
AS
declare variable res d_integer;
declare variable m d_integer;
declare variable n d_integer;
declare variable s_int d_integer;
declare variable cost d_integer;
declare variable ss d_str_255;
declare variable i d_integer;
declare variable d1 d_str_long;
declare variable d2 d_str_long;
declare variable j d_integer;
declare variable min_row_val d_integer;
begin
  -- Вычисляет редакционное расстояние Левенштейна между двумя строками
  -- Для ускорения работы работаем с байтами, вместо строковых "массивов"
  -- Максимальная длина входящих строк - 255 (увеличить нельзя)
  -- Указание max_distance позволяет сократить время работы, за счет
  -- прерывания расчетов. Т.к. часто нужно выяснить что расстояние меньше какого-то числа
  -- Если расстояние больше max_distance, возвращается 255
  -- Если max_distance < 0, расчеты не прерываются, но работает медленнее.
  if (char_length(s2) < char_length(s1)) then
  begin
    ss = s1;
    s1 = s2;
    s2 = ss;
  end

  m = char_length(s1);
  n = char_length(s2);
  if (s1 = s2) then
  begin
    res = 0;
    return res;
  end

  if (abs(n - m) > max_distance and
      max_distance > 0) then
  begin
    -- Невозможно за max_distance редактирований сделать равными длины строк
    res = 255;
    return res;
  end

  res = 0;
  i = 0;
  d1 = '';
  d2 = '';
  while (i <= n) do
  begin
    d2 = d2 || ascii_char(i);
    i = i + 1;
  end
  i = 1;
  while (i <= m) do
  begin
    d1 = d2;
    j = 0;
    min_row_val = max_distance + 1;
    while (j <= n) do
    begin
      if (j = 0) then
        d2 = overlay(d2 placing ascii_char(i) from j + 1);
      else
      begin
        cost = 1;
        if (substring(s1 from i for 1) = substring(s2 from j for 1)) then
          cost = 0;

        s_int = minvalue(
          ascii_val(substring(d2 from j for 1)) + 1,
          ascii_val(substring(d1 from j + 1 for 1)) + 1,
          ascii_val(substring(d1 from j for 1)) + cost);
        min_row_val = minvalue(min_row_val, s_int);
        d2 = overlay(d2 placing ascii_char(s_int) from j + 1);
      end
      j = j + 1;
    end

    if (max_distance > 0 and
        min_row_val > max_distance) then
      return 255; -- Превышено максимальное число редактирований
    i = i + 1;
  end

  res = ascii_val(substring(d2 from n + 1 for 1));
  if (max_distance > 0 and
      res > max_distance) then
    return 255; -- Превышено максимальное число редактирований
  else
    return res;
end
F_LEVENSTAIN_IS_SAME(TEXT_1, TEXT_2)→ D_SMALLINT
TEXT_1 VARCHAR(400)
TEXT_2 VARCHAR(400)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_LEVENSTAIN_IS_SAME(
    TEXT_1 VARCHAR(400),
    TEXT_2 VARCHAR(400))
RETURNS D_SMALLINT
AS
declare variable res_same d_integer;
declare variable txt1 d_str_100;
declare variable txt2 d_str_100;
declare variable n d_integer;
declare variable min_tt d_integer;
declare variable lev_distance d_integer;
declare variable res d_integer;
begin
  -- Функция сравнивает похожесть двух текстов
  -- Используют расстояние Левенштейна и схожесть метафона

  res_same = 1;
  if (text_1 = '' or
      text_2 = '') then
  begin
    res_same = 0;
    return res_same;
  end

  -- исключаем подряд идущие символы на одинарные
  n = 1;
  while (n < char_length(text_1)) do
  begin
    if (substring(text_1 from n for 1) = substring(text_1 from n + 1 for 1)) then
    begin
      if (n + 2 <= char_length(text_1)) then
        text_1 = left(text_1, n) || substring(text_1 from n + 2);
      else
        text_1 = left(text_1, n);
    end
    else
      n = n + 1;
  end

  n = 1;
  while (n < char_length(text_2)) do
  begin
    if (substring(text_2 from n for 1) = substring(text_2 from n + 1 for 1)) then
    begin
      if (n + 2 <= char_length(text_2)) then
        text_2 = left(text_2, n) || substring(text_2 from n + 2);
      else
        text_2 = left(text_2, n);
    end
    else
      n = n + 1;
  end

  while (text_1 <> '') do
  begin
    txt1 = '';
    txt2 = '';
    n = position(' ', :text_1);
    if (n > 0) then
    begin
      txt1 = left(text_1, n - 1);
      text_1 = substring(text_1 from n + 1);
    end
    else
    begin
      txt1 = text_1;
      text_1 = '';
    end
    n = position(' ', :text_2);
    if (n > 0) then
    begin
      txt2 = left(text_2, n - 1);
      text_2 = substring(text_2 from n + 1);
    end
    else
    begin
      txt2 = text_2;
      text_2 = '';
    end

    if (txt1 <> '' and
        txt2 <> '' and
        not(txt1 starting txt2) and
        not(txt2 starting txt1)) then
    begin
      min_tt = minvalue(char_length(:txt1), char_length(:txt2));
      lev_distance = 0;
      if (min_tt >= 4) then
        lev_distance = 1;
      if (min_tt >= 8) then
        lev_distance = 2;
      res = 255;
      if (abs(char_length(:txt1) - char_length(:txt2)) > (lev_distance + 1)) then -- если длина слов слишком отличается, завершаем
      begin
        res_same = 0;
        return res_same;
      end
      begin
        res = f_levenstain(left(:txt1, :min_tt + :lev_distance), left(:txt2, :min_tt + :lev_distance), :lev_distance);
        if (res > lev_distance) then
        begin
          res_same = 0;
          return res_same;
        end
      end
    end

    txt1 = f_metaphone(:txt1);
    txt2 = f_metaphone(:txt2);

    if (txt1 <> '' and
        txt2 <> '' and
        not(txt1 starting txt2) and
        not(txt2 starting txt1)) then
    begin
      min_tt = minvalue(char_length(:txt1), char_length(:txt2));
      lev_distance = 0;
      if (min_tt >= 4) then
        lev_distance = 1;
      res = 255;
      if (abs(char_length(:txt1) - char_length(:txt2)) > (lev_distance + 1)) then -- если длина слов слишком отличается, завершаем
      begin
        res_same = 0;
        return res_same;
      end
      begin
        res = f_levenstain(left(:txt1, :min_tt + :lev_distance), left(:txt2, :min_tt + :lev_distance), :lev_distance);
        if (res > lev_distance) then
        begin
          res_same = 0;
          return res_same;
        end
      end
    end

  end
  return res_same;
end
F_LOAN_IS_ONLINE(K_ID)→ D_BOOL
K_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_LOAN_IS_ONLINE(
    K_ID D_ID)
RETURNS D_BOOL
AS
declare variable disb_date d_date;
  declare variable is_online d_bool;
  declare variable c_id d_id;
  declare variable edoc_date d_date;
begin
  is_online = 0;

  select k_disb_date, iif(k_disb_kind in (7,8), 1, 0)
  from kontract
  where k_id = :k_id
  into disb_date, is_online;

  if (is_online = 1) then
  begin
    for select kc_from_c_id
    from knclient
    where kc_from_k_id = :k_id and
          kc_amount_approved > 0
    into c_id
    do
    begin
      edoc_date = null;
      -- Проверка наличие подписанного договора АСП на дату выдачи
      select edl_blank_date
      from edoc_list
      where edl_object_kind = 0 and -- Clients
            edl_object_id = :c_id and
            edl_from_edc_id = f_edoc_kind('asp.agreement', 'id') and
            edl_status = 1 and
            edl_blank_date <= :disb_date and
            coalesce(edl_date_end, :disb_date) >= :disb_date
      order by edl_blank_date desc
      rows 1
      into edoc_date;

      if (edoc_date is null)
      then is_online = 0;
      else
      begin
        -- Если есть подписанный договор АСП, проверяем имеются ли другие выдачи
        if (not exists(select *
                       from knclient
                       where kc_from_c_id = :c_id and
                             kc_date_disb between :edoc_date and :disb_date and
                             kc_from_k_id <> :k_id and
                             kc_amount_approved > 0)) then
          is_online = 0;
      end
    end
  end

  -- Проверка по продукту и по дате .
  if (is_online = 1) then
  begin
    if (exists(select *
               from kontract
               inner join product  on pr_id = k_from_pr_id
               where k_id = :k_id and
                     pr_code containing '.reliable_emp.' and
                     k_fact_disb_date >= '24.04.2023')) then
      is_online = 0;
  end

  return is_online;
end
F_LOYAL(OL_ID, REP_DATE)→ D_CURRENCY
OL_ID BIGINT
REP_DATE DATE
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_LOYAL(
    OL_ID D_ID,
    REP_DATE D_DATE)
RETURNS D_CURRENCY
AS
declare variable k_id d_id;
declare variable res d_bool;
declare variable k_disb_date d_date;
declare variable sh_balance d_currency;
begin
  -- Возвращает закрыт ли первый платеж по OL_ID выдачи займа
  -- Также проверяет прошло ли 30 дней от выдачи
  -- Используется для вычисления остатка балла агентам
  res = 0;

  -- Нужно установить дату запуска данной функции. Пока везде возращаем 1
--  if (rep_date < cast('01.02.2024' as date)) then
  return 1;

  select ks_from_k_id, ks_date_begin
  from kontstate
  where ks_from_ol_id = :ol_id and
        ks_state = 1 and
        ks_ol_is_delete = 0
  into k_id, k_disb_date;

  if (k_id is not null) then
  begin
    if (datediff(day, k_disb_date, rep_date) >= 30) then
    begin
      sh_balance = 0;
      select sum(ok_os + ok_perc + ok_sh_os + ok_sh_perc)
      from operkred
      where ok_from_k_id = :k_id and
            ok_ol_is_active = 1 and
            ok_ol_date <= :rep_date and
            ok_type <= 4 and
            ok_sh_id = 1
      into sh_balance;

      if (sh_balance is null) then
        sh_balance = 0;

      if (sh_balance <= 0) then
        res = 1;
    end
  end else
    res = 1;

  return res;
end
F_LO_MGR_C_ID(LML_ID)→ D_ID DETERMINISTIC
LML_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_LO_MGR_C_ID(
    LML_ID D_ID)
RETURNS D_ID DETERMINISTIC
AS
declare variable MGR_NAME D_STR_100;
declare variable C_ID D_ID;

begin
  c_id = null;
  select lml_from_c_id
  from loanmgrlist
  where lml_id = :lml_id
  into c_id;
  c_id = coalesce(c_id, -1);
  return c_id;
end
F_LO_MGR_ID(K_ID, REP_DATE, LO_ID, REG_ID)→ D_INTEGER
K_ID BIGINT
REP_DATE DATE
LO_ID BIGINT
REG_ID INTEGER
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_LO_MGR_ID(
    K_ID D_ID,
    REP_DATE D_DATE NOT NULL,
    LO_ID D_ID = null,
    REG_ID D_INTEGER = null)
RETURNS D_INTEGER
AS
declare variable LML_ID D_INTEGER;
declare variable LML_FROM_REG_ID D_INTEGER;
declare variable OKS_DATE_BEGIN D_DATE;
declare variable OKS_DATE_END D_DATE;
declare variable KF_ID D_ID;
begin
  if (reg_id is null or lo_id is null) then
  begin
    select oks_kc_id, oks_region_id, oks_date_begin, oks_date_end, k_from_kf_id
    from oksummary, kontract
    where oks_from_k_id = :k_id and
          oks_date_begin <= :rep_date and
          oks_from_k_id + 0 = k_id  -- +0 сделали чтобы индекс k_id не работал т.е. когда работает то больше записей читает из OKSUMARY
    order by oks_from_k_id desc, oks_date_begin desc
    rows 1
    into lo_id, reg_id, oks_date_begin, oks_date_end, kf_id;

    if (rep_date not between oks_date_begin and oks_date_end) then
    begin
      select kal_new_kf_officer, kal_new_reg_id from knaktlist
      where kal_from_kf_id = :kf_id
      and knaktlist.is_delete = 0
      and :rep_date between kal_date and kal_date_end
      into lo_id, reg_id;
    end
  end
  /*  lml_from_c_id -- в этом поле не хранится ID Кредитного специалиста
  if (lml_id is null) then
  begin
    select lml_id
    from loanmgrlist
    where lml_from_reg_id = :reg_id and
          lml_from_c_id = :lo_id and
          lml_date_begin <= :rep_date and
          lml_date_end >= :rep_date
    into lml_id;
  end */
  if (lml_id is null) then
  begin
    if (lo_id <> 1000000000004) then -- КЦ Сирена
    begin
      select ke_from_lml_id
      from krexpert
      where ke_from_p_id = :lo_id and
            ke_from_reg_id = :reg_id and
            ke_date <= :rep_date
      order by ke_date desc
      rows 1
      into lml_id;
    end else
      if (k_id is not null) then
      begin
        select lmk_from_lml_id, lml_from_reg_id
        from loanmgrkont, loanmgrlist
        where lmk_from_k_id = :k_id and
              lmk_date_begin <= :rep_date and
              lmk_from_lml_id = lml_id
        order by lmk_date_begin desc, lmk_id desc
        rows 1
        into lml_id, lml_from_reg_id;
  
        if (lml_from_reg_id <> reg_id) then
          lml_id = null;
      end
  end

  if (lml_id is null) then
  begin
    select lml_id
    from loanmgrlist
    where lml_from_reg_id = :reg_id and
          lml_from_c_id is null
    into lml_id;
  end

  return lml_id;
end

F_MASK(TEXT, MASK_KIND, ADD_TEXT)→ D_STR_255
TEXT VARCHAR(255)
MASK_KIND VARCHAR(50)
ADD_TEXT VARCHAR(100)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_MASK(
    TEXT D_STR_255,
    MASK_KIND D_STR_CODE,
    ADD_TEXT D_STR_100 = null)
RETURNS D_STR_255
AS
declare variable res_text d_str_255;
begin
  res_text = uxf$mask(text, mask_kind);

  if (coalesce(add_text, '') <> '') then
    res_text = res_text || ', ' || add_text;

  return res_text;
end
F_MASK_TEXT(VAL, START_VISIBLE, END_VISIBLE)→ D_STR_CODE
VAL VARCHAR(50)
START_VISIBLE INTEGER
END_VISIBLE INTEGER
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_MASK_TEXT(
    VAL D_STR_CODE,
    START_VISIBLE D_INTEGER,
    END_VISIBLE D_INTEGER)
RETURNS D_STR_CODE
AS
declare variable len d_integer;
declare variable mask_len d_integer;
begin
    -- если входная строка пустая, возвращаем её же
    if (val is null or val = '') then
        return val;

    len = char_length(val);

    -- если строка слишком короткая для маскировки, возвращаем оригинал
    if (len <= start_visible + end_visible) then
        return val;

    -- вычисляем длину "скрытой" части
    mask_len = len - start_visible - end_visible;

    -- собираем строку:
    -- left(...) - берем начало
    -- rpad(...) - генерируем нужное количество звездочек
    -- right(...) - берем конец
    return left(val, start_visible) || 
           rpad('', mask_len, '*') || 
           right(val, end_visible);
end
F_MBH_ANNU(K_ID)→ BOOLEAN
K_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_MBH_ANNU(
    K_ID D_ID)
RETURNS BOOLEAN
AS
declare variable res boolean;
begin
  res = exists(
    select *
    from kontract
      inner join product
        on pr_id = k_from_pr_id
          and pr_code containing '.murabaha.'
          and pr_percent_kind = 0
    where k_id = :k_id);
  return res;
end
F_MD5(SOURCE_STR)→ CHAR(32) CHARACTER SET OCTETS
SOURCE_STR VARCHAR(4096)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_MD5(
    SOURCE_STR VARCHAR(4096) CHARACTER SET OCTETS)
RETURNS CHAR(32) CHARACTER SET OCTETS
AS
declare variable a bigint = 0x0067452301;
declare variable b bigint = 0x00efcdab89;
declare variable c bigint = 0x0098badcfe;
declare variable d bigint = 0x0010325476;
declare variable aa bigint;
declare variable bb bigint;
declare variable cc bigint;
declare variable dd bigint;
declare variable buf char(64) character set octets;
declare variable len bigint;
declare variable m integer;
declare variable n integer;

declare function f(a bigint, b bigint, c bigint, d bigint, k int, s int, t bigint, p int, v char(64) character set octets) returns bigint
as
declare variable ff bigint;
declare variable r bigint;
declare variable x bigint;
begin
x = bin_or(ascii_val(substring(v from k * 4 + 1 for 1)), bin_shl(ascii_val(substring(v from k * 4 + 2 for 1)), 8), bin_shl(ascii_val(substring(v from k * 4 + 3 for 1)), 16), bin_shl(ascii_val(substring(v from k * 4 + 4 for 1)), 24));

if (p = 1) then
/* f */
ff = bin_or(bin_and(b, c), bin_and(bin_not(b), d));
if (p = 2) then
/* g */
ff = bin_or(bin_and(b, d), bin_and(bin_not(d), c));
if (p = 3) then
/* h */
ff = bin_xor(b, c, d);
if (p = 4) then
/* i */
ff = bin_xor(c, bin_or(bin_not(d), b));

r = bin_and(0x00ffffffff, a + ff + x + t);
r = bin_and(0x00ffffffff, b + bin_or(bin_shl(r, s), bin_shr(r, 32 - s)));
return r;
end

declare function inttochar4 (v bigint) returns char (4) character set octets
as
begin
return ascii_char(bin_and(0xff, v)) || ascii_char(bin_and(0xff, bin_shr(v, 8))) || ascii_char(bin_and(0xff, bin_shr(v, 16))) || ascii_char(bin_and(0xff, bin_shr(v, 24)));
end

declare function inttohex (v bigint) returns char (8) character set octets
as
declare variable h char(16) = '0123456789ABCDEF';
begin
return substring(h from bin_and(0x0f, bin_shr(v, 4)) + 1 for 1) || substring(h from bin_and(0x0f, v) + 1 for 1) || substring(h from bin_and(0x0f, bin_shr(v, 12)) + 1 for 1) || substring(h from bin_and(0x0f, bin_shr(v, 8)) + 1 for 1) || substring(h from bin_and(0x0f, bin_shr(v, 20)) + 1 for 1) || substring(h from bin_and(0x0f, bin_shr(v, 16)) + 1 for 1) || substring(h from bin_and(0x0f, bin_shr(v, 28)) + 1 for 1) || substring(h from bin_and(0x0f, bin_shr(v, 24)) + 1 for 1);
end

begin
  len = octet_length(source_str);
  m = 56 - mod(len + 1, 64);
  if (m < 0) then
    m = m + 64;
  source_str = source_str || rpad(x'80', m + 1, x'00') || inttochar4(bin_and(0x00ffffffff, bin_shl(len, 3))) || inttochar4(bin_shr(len, 32 - 3));

  n = 0;
  while (n < (len + m + 9) / 64) do
  begin
    aa = a;
    bb = b;
    cc = c;
    dd = d;

    buf = substring(source_str from n * 64 + 1 for 64);

    /* Round 1 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 0, 7, 0x00d76aa478, 1, buf);/* 1 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 1, 12, 0x00e8c7b756, 1, buf);/* 2 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 2, 17, 0x00242070db, 1, buf);/* 3 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 3, 22, 0x00c1bdceee, 1, buf);/* 4 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 4, 7, 0x00f57c0faf, 1, buf);/* 5 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 5, 12, 0x004787c62a, 1, buf);/* 6 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 6, 17, 0x00a8304613, 1, buf);/* 7 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 7, 22, 0x00fd469501, 1, buf);/* 8 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 8, 7, 0x00698098d8, 1, buf);/* 9 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 9, 12, 0x008b44f7af, 1, buf);/* 10 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 10, 17, 0x00ffff5bb1, 1, buf);/* 11 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 11, 22, 0x00895cd7be, 1, buf);/* 12 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 12, 7, 0x006b901122, 1, buf);/* 13 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 13, 12, 0x00fd987193, 1, buf);/* 14 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 14, 17, 0x00a679438e, 1, buf);/* 15 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 15, 22, 0x0049b40821, 1, buf);/* 16 */

    /* Round 2 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 1, 5, 0x00f61e2562, 2, buf);/* 17 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 6, 9, 0x00c040b340, 2, buf);/* 18 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 11, 14, 0x00265e5a51, 2, buf);/* 19 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 0, 20, 0x00e9b6c7aa, 2, buf);/* 20 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 5, 5, 0x00d62f105d, 2, buf);/* 21 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 10, 9, 0x0002441453, 2, buf);/* 22 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 15, 14, 0x00d8a1e681, 2, buf);/* 23 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 4, 20, 0x00e7d3fbc8, 2, buf);/* 24 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 9, 5, 0x0021e1cde6, 2, buf);/* 25 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 14, 9, 0x00c33707d6, 2, buf);/* 26 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 3, 14, 0x00f4d50d87, 2, buf);/* 27 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 8, 20, 0x00455a14ed, 2, buf);/* 28 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 13, 5, 0x00a9e3e905, 2, buf);/* 29 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 2, 9, 0x00fcefa3f8, 2, buf);/* 30 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 7, 14, 0x00676f02d9, 2, buf);/* 31 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 12, 20, 0x008d2a4c8a, 2, buf);/* 32 */

    /* Round 3 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 5, 4, 0x00fffa3942, 3, buf);/* 33 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 8, 11, 0x008771f681, 3, buf);/* 34 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 11, 16, 0x006d9d6122, 3, buf);/* 35 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 14, 23, 0x00fde5380c, 3, buf);/* 36 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 1, 4, 0x00a4beea44, 3, buf);/* 37 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 4, 11, 0x004bdecfa9, 3, buf);/* 38 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 7, 16, 0x00f6bb4b60, 3, buf);/* 39 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 10, 23, 0x00bebfbc70, 3, buf);/* 40 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 13, 4, 0x00289b7ec6, 3, buf);/* 41 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 0, 11, 0x00eaa127fa, 3, buf);/* 42 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 3, 16, 0x00d4ef3085, 3, buf);/* 43 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 6, 23, 0x0004881d05, 3, buf);/* 44 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 9, 4, 0x00d9d4d039, 3, buf);/* 45 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 12, 11, 0x00e6db99e5, 3, buf);/* 46 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 15, 16, 0x001fa27cf8, 3, buf);/* 47 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 2, 23, 0x00c4ac5665, 3, buf);/* 48 */

    /* Round 4 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 0, 6, 0x00f4292244, 4, buf);/* 49 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 7, 10, 0x00432aff97, 4, buf);/* 50 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 14, 15, 0x00ab9423a7, 4, buf);/* 51 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 5, 21, 0x00fc93a039, 4, buf);/* 52 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 12, 6, 0x00655b59c3, 4, buf);/* 53 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 3, 10, 0x008f0ccc92, 4, buf);/* 54 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 10, 15, 0x00ffeff47d, 4, buf);/* 55 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 1, 21, 0x0085845dd1, 4, buf);/* 56 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 8, 6, 0x006fa87e4f, 4, buf);/* 57 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 15, 10, 0x00fe2ce6e0, 4, buf);/* 58 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 6, 15, 0x00a3014314, 4, buf);/* 59 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 13, 21, 0x004e0811a1, 4, buf);/* 60 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 4, 6, 0x00f7537e82, 4, buf);/* 61 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 11, 10, 0x00bd3af235, 4, buf);/* 62 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 2, 15, 0x002ad7d2bb, 4, buf);/* 63 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 9, 21, 0x00eb86d391, 4, buf);/* 64 */

    a = bin_and(0x00ffffffff, aa + a);
    b = bin_and(0x00ffffffff, bb + b);
    c = bin_and(0x00ffffffff, cc + c);
    d = bin_and(0x00ffffffff, dd + d);
    n = n + 1;
  end

  return inttohex(a) || inttohex(b) || inttohex(c) || inttohex(d);
end
F_MD5_BLOB(SOURCE_BLOB)→ CHAR(32) CHARACTER SET OCTETS
SOURCE_BLOB BLOB SUB_TYPE TEXT SEGMENT SIZE 80
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_MD5_BLOB(
    SOURCE_BLOB BLOB SUB_TYPE TEXT SEGMENT SIZE 80)
RETURNS CHAR(32) CHARACTER SET OCTETS
AS
declare variable a bigint = 0x0067452301;
declare variable b bigint = 0x00efcdab89;
declare variable c bigint = 0x0098badcfe;
declare variable d bigint = 0x0010325476;
declare variable aa bigint;
declare variable bb bigint;
declare variable cc bigint;
declare variable dd bigint;
declare variable buf char(64) character set octets;
declare variable len bigint;
declare variable m integer;
declare variable n integer;

declare function f(a bigint, b bigint, c bigint, d bigint, k int, s int, t bigint, p int, v char(64) character set octets) returns bigint
as
declare variable ff bigint;
declare variable r bigint;
declare variable x bigint;
begin
x = bin_or(ascii_val(substring(v from k * 4 + 1 for 1)), bin_shl(ascii_val(substring(v from k * 4 + 2 for 1)), 8), bin_shl(ascii_val(substring(v from k * 4 + 3 for 1)), 16), bin_shl(ascii_val(substring(v from k * 4 + 4 for 1)), 24));

if (p = 1) then
/* f */
ff = bin_or(bin_and(b, c), bin_and(bin_not(b), d));
if (p = 2) then
/* g */
ff = bin_or(bin_and(b, d), bin_and(bin_not(d), c));
if (p = 3) then
/* h */
ff = bin_xor(b, c, d);
if (p = 4) then
/* i */
ff = bin_xor(c, bin_or(bin_not(d), b));

r = bin_and(0x00ffffffff, a + ff + x + t);
r = bin_and(0x00ffffffff, b + bin_or(bin_shl(r, s), bin_shr(r, 32 - s)));
return r;
end

declare function inttochar4 (v bigint) returns char (4) character set octets
as
begin
return ascii_char(bin_and(0xff, v)) || ascii_char(bin_and(0xff, bin_shr(v, 8))) || ascii_char(bin_and(0xff, bin_shr(v, 16))) || ascii_char(bin_and(0xff, bin_shr(v, 24)));
end

declare function inttohex (v bigint) returns char (8) character set octets
as
declare variable h char(16) = '0123456789ABCDEF';
begin
return substring(h from bin_and(0x0f, bin_shr(v, 4)) + 1 for 1) || substring(h from bin_and(0x0f, v) + 1 for 1) || substring(h from bin_and(0x0f, bin_shr(v, 12)) + 1 for 1) || substring(h from bin_and(0x0f, bin_shr(v, 8)) + 1 for 1) || substring(h from bin_and(0x0f, bin_shr(v, 20)) + 1 for 1) || substring(h from bin_and(0x0f, bin_shr(v, 16)) + 1 for 1) || substring(h from bin_and(0x0f, bin_shr(v, 28)) + 1 for 1) || substring(h from bin_and(0x0f, bin_shr(v, 24)) + 1 for 1);
end

begin
  len = octet_length(source_blob);
  m = 56 - mod(len + 1, 64);
  if (m < 0) then
    m = m + 64;
  source_blob = source_blob || rpad(x'80', m + 1, x'00') || inttochar4(bin_and(0x00ffffffff, bin_shl(len, 3))) || inttochar4(bin_shr(len, 32 - 3));

  n = 0;
  while (n < (len + m + 9) / 64) do
  begin
    aa = a;
    bb = b;
    cc = c;
    dd = d;

    buf = substring(source_blob from n * 64 + 1 for 64);

    /* Round 1 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 0, 7, 0x00d76aa478, 1, buf);/* 1 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 1, 12, 0x00e8c7b756, 1, buf);/* 2 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 2, 17, 0x00242070db, 1, buf);/* 3 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 3, 22, 0x00c1bdceee, 1, buf);/* 4 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 4, 7, 0x00f57c0faf, 1, buf);/* 5 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 5, 12, 0x004787c62a, 1, buf);/* 6 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 6, 17, 0x00a8304613, 1, buf);/* 7 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 7, 22, 0x00fd469501, 1, buf);/* 8 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 8, 7, 0x00698098d8, 1, buf);/* 9 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 9, 12, 0x008b44f7af, 1, buf);/* 10 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 10, 17, 0x00ffff5bb1, 1, buf);/* 11 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 11, 22, 0x00895cd7be, 1, buf);/* 12 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 12, 7, 0x006b901122, 1, buf);/* 13 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 13, 12, 0x00fd987193, 1, buf);/* 14 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 14, 17, 0x00a679438e, 1, buf);/* 15 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 15, 22, 0x0049b40821, 1, buf);/* 16 */

    /* Round 2 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 1, 5, 0x00f61e2562, 2, buf);/* 17 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 6, 9, 0x00c040b340, 2, buf);/* 18 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 11, 14, 0x00265e5a51, 2, buf);/* 19 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 0, 20, 0x00e9b6c7aa, 2, buf);/* 20 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 5, 5, 0x00d62f105d, 2, buf);/* 21 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 10, 9, 0x0002441453, 2, buf);/* 22 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 15, 14, 0x00d8a1e681, 2, buf);/* 23 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 4, 20, 0x00e7d3fbc8, 2, buf);/* 24 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 9, 5, 0x0021e1cde6, 2, buf);/* 25 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 14, 9, 0x00c33707d6, 2, buf);/* 26 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 3, 14, 0x00f4d50d87, 2, buf);/* 27 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 8, 20, 0x00455a14ed, 2, buf);/* 28 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 13, 5, 0x00a9e3e905, 2, buf);/* 29 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 2, 9, 0x00fcefa3f8, 2, buf);/* 30 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 7, 14, 0x00676f02d9, 2, buf);/* 31 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 12, 20, 0x008d2a4c8a, 2, buf);/* 32 */

    /* Round 3 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 5, 4, 0x00fffa3942, 3, buf);/* 33 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 8, 11, 0x008771f681, 3, buf);/* 34 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 11, 16, 0x006d9d6122, 3, buf);/* 35 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 14, 23, 0x00fde5380c, 3, buf);/* 36 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 1, 4, 0x00a4beea44, 3, buf);/* 37 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 4, 11, 0x004bdecfa9, 3, buf);/* 38 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 7, 16, 0x00f6bb4b60, 3, buf);/* 39 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 10, 23, 0x00bebfbc70, 3, buf);/* 40 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 13, 4, 0x00289b7ec6, 3, buf);/* 41 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 0, 11, 0x00eaa127fa, 3, buf);/* 42 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 3, 16, 0x00d4ef3085, 3, buf);/* 43 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 6, 23, 0x0004881d05, 3, buf);/* 44 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 9, 4, 0x00d9d4d039, 3, buf);/* 45 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 12, 11, 0x00e6db99e5, 3, buf);/* 46 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 15, 16, 0x001fa27cf8, 3, buf);/* 47 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 2, 23, 0x00c4ac5665, 3, buf);/* 48 */

    /* Round 4 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 0, 6, 0x00f4292244, 4, buf);/* 49 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 7, 10, 0x00432aff97, 4, buf);/* 50 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 14, 15, 0x00ab9423a7, 4, buf);/* 51 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 5, 21, 0x00fc93a039, 4, buf);/* 52 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 12, 6, 0x00655b59c3, 4, buf);/* 53 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 3, 10, 0x008f0ccc92, 4, buf);/* 54 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 10, 15, 0x00ffeff47d, 4, buf);/* 55 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 1, 21, 0x0085845dd1, 4, buf);/* 56 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 8, 6, 0x006fa87e4f, 4, buf);/* 57 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 15, 10, 0x00fe2ce6e0, 4, buf);/* 58 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 6, 15, 0x00a3014314, 4, buf);/* 59 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 13, 21, 0x004e0811a1, 4, buf);/* 60 */
    a = f(a, b, c, d, 4, 6, 0x00f7537e82, 4, buf);/* 61 */
    d = f(d, a, b, c, 11, 10, 0x00bd3af235, 4, buf);/* 62 */
    c = f(c, d, a, b, 2, 15, 0x002ad7d2bb, 4, buf);/* 63 */
    b = f(b, c, d, a, 9, 21, 0x00eb86d391, 4, buf);/* 64 */

    a = bin_and(0x00ffffffff, aa + a);
    b = bin_and(0x00ffffffff, bb + b);
    c = bin_and(0x00ffffffff, cc + c);
    d = bin_and(0x00ffffffff, dd + d);
    n = n + 1;
  end

  return inttohex(a) || inttohex(b) || inttohex(c) || inttohex(d);
end
F_MD5_UUID(TEXT)→ D_UUID
TEXT VARCHAR(1024)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_MD5_UUID(
    TEXT D_STR_LONG)
RETURNS D_UUID
AS
declare variable str_md5 char(32) character set octets;
declare variable res_uuid d_uuid;
begin
  -- Преобразовывает переданный текст в GUID
  text = lower(text);

  str_md5 = f_md5(text);
  res_uuid = char_to_uuid(left(str_md5, 8) ||
             '-' || substring(str_md5 from 9 for 4) ||
             '-' || substring(str_md5 from 13 for 4) ||
             '-' || substring(str_md5 from 17 for 4) ||
             '-' || substring(str_md5 from 21 for 12));

  return res_uuid;
end
F_MEET_PASSED(C_ID)→ D_SMALLINT
C_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_MEET_PASSED(
    C_ID D_ID)
RETURNS D_SMALLINT
AS
declare variable RES D_SMALLINT;
begin
  res = 0;

  if (f_get_const('qr_verify_enabled') = 0) then
    res = 1;
  else
  begin
    if (exists(select *
               from qr_list
               where qrl_from_c_id = :c_id and
                     qrl_state = 2 and
                     qrl_is_delete = 0)) then
    res = 1;
    if (res = 0 and f_get_const('qr_verify_enabled') = 2) then
      res = 2;
  end

  return res;
end
F_MEET_VALID(C_ID, LAST_MINUTE)→ D_SMALLINT
C_ID BIGINT
LAST_MINUTE INTEGER
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_MEET_VALID(
    C_ID D_ID,
    LAST_MINUTE D_INTEGER)
RETURNS D_SMALLINT
AS
declare variable start_datetime d_datetime;
declare variable res d_smallint;
begin
  -- Возвращает 0 или 1 в зависимости была ли подтверждение встречи за последний LAST_MINUTE минут
  -- Подтверждать встречу можно через QR-код, либо через фотографирование клиента через МП менеджера
  res = 0;

  if (f_get_const('qr_verify_enabled') = 0) then
    res = 1;
  else
  begin
    start_datetime = dateadd(minute, -last_minute, cast('now' as timestamp));
    if (exists(select *
               from qr_list, qr_verify
               where qrl_from_c_id = :c_id and
                     qrl_state = 2 and
                     qrl_is_delete = 0 and
                     qrl_id = qrv_from_qrl_id and
                     qrv_datetime >= :start_datetime)) then
    res = 1;
    if (res = 0 and f_get_const('qr_verify_enabled') = 2) then
      res = 2;
  end

  return res;
end
F_MEMO_ID(MEMO_TEXT)→ D_ID
Возвращает ID по тексту
MEMO_TEXT VARCHAR(1024)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_MEMO_ID(
    MEMO_TEXT D_STR_LONG)
RETURNS D_ID
AS
declare variable mml_id d_id;
declare variable hash_code d_str_code;
begin
  -- Функция возвращает ID длинного текста переданного в параметре.

  -- таблица MemoList используется как хранилище длинных текстов
  -- В некоторых таблицах, где текст часто повторяется, рекомендуется использовать ID вместо самого текста

  if (memo_text is null) then
    memo_text = '';

  hash_code = f_md5(memo_text);

  select mml_id
  from memolist
  where mml_hash_code = :hash_code and
        mml_memo = :memo_text
  into mml_id;

  if (mml_id is null) then
  begin
    insert into memolist (mml_hash_code, mml_memo)
    values (:hash_code, :memo_text)
    returning mml_id
    into :mml_id;
  end

  return mml_id;
end
F_MEMO_TEXT(MEMO_ID)→ D_STR_LONG
Возвращает текст по его ID
MEMO_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_MEMO_TEXT(
    MEMO_ID D_ID)
RETURNS D_STR_LONG
AS
declare variable mml_memo d_str_long;
begin
  -- Функция возвращает текст соответствующей переданному в парамету.

  -- таблица MemoList используется как хранилище длинных текстов
  -- В некоторых таблицах, где текст часто повторяется, рекомендуется использовать ID вместо самого текста

  if (memo_id is null) then return '';

  select mml_memo
  from memolist
  where mml_id = :memo_id
  into mml_memo;
  if (mml_memo is null) then
    exception e_default 'Memo_ID value not found: ' || memo_id;
  else
    return mml_memo;
end
F_METAPHONE(TXT)→ VARCHAR(400)
TXT VARCHAR(400)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_METAPHONE(
    TXT VARCHAR(400))
RETURNS VARCHAR(400)
AS
declare variable res_txt varchar(400);
declare variable alf varchar(400);
declare variable cns1 varchar(400);
declare variable cns2 varchar(400);
declare variable cns3 varchar(400);
declare variable ch varchar(400);
declare variable ct varchar(400);
declare variable s varchar(400);
declare variable v varchar(400);
declare variable b integer;
declare variable c varchar(10);
declare variable old_c varchar(10);
declare variable w varchar(400);
declare variable s1 varchar(1);
declare variable sym_old varchar(2);
declare variable sym_new varchar(2);
declare variable w_new varchar(400);
declare variable len integer;
declare variable i integer;
begin
  if (txt is null) then
    txt = '';
  if (txt = '') then
  begin
    res_txt = '';
    return res_txt;
  end
  w = txt;

  w = replace(w, 'ya', 'я');
  w = replace(w, 'yu', 'ю');
  w = replace(w, 'yo', 'ё');
  w = replace(w, 'zh', 'ж');
  w = replace(w, 'sh', 'ш');
  w = replace(w, 'ch', 'ч');
  w = replace(w, 'ck', 'к');
  w = replace(w, 'kh', 'х');
  w = replace(w, 'ph', 'ф');

  if (left(w, 1) = 'e' or
      left(w, 1) = 'ә' or
      left(w, 1) = 'Ə') then
  begin
    w = 'а' || substring(w from 2);
  end
  len = char_length(w);
  w_new = '';
  i = 1;
  while (i <= len) do
  begin
    sym_old = substring(w from i for 1);
    sym_new = (case sym_old
                 when 'Ң' then 'нг'
                 when 'ң' then 'нг'
                 when 'a' then 'а'
                 when 'b' then 'б'
                 when 'c' then 'ж'
                 when 'd' then 'д'
                 when 'e' then 'е'
                 when 'f' then 'ф'
                 when 'g' then 'г'
                 when 'h' then 'х'
                 when 'i' then 'и'
                 when 'j' then 'ж'
                 when 'k' then 'к'
                 when 'l' then 'л'
                 when 'm' then 'м'
                 when 'n' then 'н'
                 when 'o' then 'о'
                 when 'p' then 'п'
                 when 'q' then 'к'
                 when 'r' then 'р'
                 when 's' then 'с'
                 when 't' then 'т'
                 when 'u' then 'у'
                 when 'v' then 'в'
                 when 'w' then 'в'
                 when 'x' then 'х'
                 when 'y' then 'и'
                 when 'z' then 'з'
                 when 'Ç' then 'ч'
                 when 'ç' then 'ч'
                 when 'ҫ' then 'ч'
                 when 'Ə' then 'э'
                 when 'ә' then 'э'
                 when 'Ğ' then 'г'
                 when 'ğ' then 'г'
                 when 'İ' then 'и'
                 when 'i' then 'и'
                 when 'ı' then 'и'
                 when 'Ö' then 'о'
                 when 'ö' then 'о'
                 when 'Ş' then 'ш'
                 when 'ş' then 'ш'
                 when 'Ü' then 'у'
                 when 'ü' then 'у'
                 when 'Ө' then 'о'
                 when '�©' then 'о'
                 when 'Ү' then 'у'
                 when 'ү' then 'у'
                 else :sym_old
               end);

    w_new = w_new || sym_new;
    i = i + 1;
  end
  w = w_new;
  w_new = '';

  w = replace(w, 'дж', 'ж');
  w = replace(w, 'дз', 'з');
  w = lower(w);

  res_txt = '';
  alf = 'ОЕАИУЭЮЯПСТРКЛМНБВГДЖЗЙФХЦЧШ�©ЁЫ';
  cns1 = 'БЗДВГМ�©Ц';
  cns2 = 'ПСТФКНШС';
  cns3 = 'ПСТКБВГДЖЗФХЦЧШ�©';
  ch = 'ОЭЯЮЕЁЫ';
  ct = 'АААУИИИ';
  s = '';
  v = '';
  i = 1;

  while (i <= char_length(w)) do
  begin
    c = substring(w from i for 1);
    if (position(c, alf) > 0) then
      s = s || c;
    i = i + 1;
  end
  if (char_length(s) = 0) then
  begin
    res_txt = '';
    return res_txt;
  end

  if (char_length(s) > 1) then
  begin
    i = 1;
    s1 = left(s, 1);
    while (i <= 8) do
    begin
      s = replace(s, substring(cns1 from i for 1), substring(cns2 from i for 1));
      i = i + 1;
    end
    s = s1 || substring(s from 2);
  end

  old_c = ' ';
  i = 1;
  while (i <= char_length(s)) do
  begin
    c = substring(s from i for 1);
    b = position(c, ch);
    if (b > 0) then
    begin
      if (old_c = 'й' or
          old_c = 'и') then
      begin
        if (c = 'о' or
            c = 'е') then
        begin
          old_c = 'и';
          s = left(s, char_length(s) - 1) || old_c;
        end
        else
        begin
          if (c <> old_c) then
            v = v || substring(ct from b for 1);
        end
      end
      else
      begin
        if (c <> old_c) then
          v = v || substring(ct from b for 1);
      end
    end
    else
    begin
      if (c <> old_c and
          position(c, cns3) > 0) then
      begin
        b = position(old_c, cns1);
        if (b > 0) then
        begin
          old_c = substring(cns2 from b for 1);
          v = left(v, char_length(v) - 1) || old_c;
        end
      end
      if (c <> old_c) then
        v = v || c;
    end
    old_c = c;
    i = i + 1;
  end

  res_txt = lower(v);
  return res_txt;
end
F_MONTHNAME(DT)→ STR_20
DT DATE
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_MONTHNAME(
    DT DATE = current_date)
RETURNS STR_20
AS
begin
  return case extract(month from dt)
           when 01 then 'Январь'
           when 02 then 'Февраль'
           when 03 then 'Март'
           when 04 then 'Апрель'
           when 05 then 'Май'
           when 06 then 'Июнь'
           when 07 then 'Июль'
           when 08 then 'Август'
           when 09 then 'Сентябрь'
           when 10 then 'Октябрь'
           when 11 then 'Ноябрь'
           when 12 then 'Декабрь'
           else ''
         end;
end
F_NEED_ANTIFRAUD_CHECK(C_ID)→ D_BOOL
C_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_NEED_ANTIFRAUD_CHECK(
    C_ID D_ID)
RETURNS D_BOOL
AS
declare variable IS_WORKING D_BOOL;
declare variable C_INN D_STR_20;
declare variable AC_STATUS D_STR_CODE;
begin
    -- RFCKZ-311
    -- Проверяем включён ли антифрод API
    -- Если API выключен - проверка не нужна (используются внутренние списки)

    -- is_working = 0 - не делаем проверку
    -- is_working = 1 - делаем проверку

    :is_working = (select is_working from antifraud_api_working_status);
    if (coalesce(:is_working, 0) = 0) then
        return 0;

    :c_inn = (select c.c_inn from clients c where c.c_id = :c_id);

    select first 1 f_enum('antifraud_status', ac.ac_status, 'code')
    from antifraud_check ac
    where ac.ac_inn = :c_inn
        and ac.ac_date = current_date
        and f_enum('antifraud_status', ac.ac_status, 'code') in ('in_process', 'not_found', 'was_found')
    order by ac.ac_id desc
    into :ac_status;

    -- Если проверка производилась не сегодняшним числом, тогда возвращаем is_working = 1
    if (coalesce(:ac_status, '') = '') then
        return 1;

    return 0;
end
F_NEED_SMS(C_ID, CONFIG, PHONE)→ D_INTEGER
C_ID DECIMAL(18,0)
CONFIG VARCHAR(100)
PHONE VARCHAR(100)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_NEED_SMS(
    C_ID ID_TYPE,
    CONFIG STR_100,
    PHONE STR_100)
RETURNS D_INTEGER
AS
declare variable RES D_INTEGER;
begin
/*
   функция определяет нужно ли оправлять смс клиенту
   res = 0 - отправляем только пуш
   res = 1 - отправяем только смс
   res = 2 - отправляем и пуш, и смс
*/
    res = 0;
    if (config = 'need_sms') then
    begin
        if ((select mcl_google_id
             from mc_login mcl
             where mcl_from_c_id = :c_id
             and mcl_login = :phone
             and mcl_is_delete = 0) <> '') then
            begin
                if ((select mod(extract(day from c_birth_date), 2)
                     from clients
                     where c_id = :c_id) = 0) then
                res = 0 ;
                else
                res = 2;
            end
        else
        res = 1;
    end
    return res;
end
F_NEW_MARKUP_MBH(K_ID, DB_ID, PR_CODE, DISB_DATE)→ BOOLEAN
K_ID BIGINT — Учет наценки по продукту Мурабаха по новому /
DB_ID INTEGER
PR_CODE VARCHAR(100)
DISB_DATE DATE
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_NEW_MARKUP_MBH(
    K_ID D_ID,
    DB_ID D_INTEGER = null,
    PR_CODE D_STR_100 = null,
    DISB_DATE D_DATE = null)
RETURNS BOOLEAN
AS
declare variable dt_appl d_date;
begin
  dt_appl = f_get_const('newmarkup_murabaha');
  return (
    iif(db_id is not null and pr_code is not null and disb_date is not null,
      db_id in (39801, 41701) and pr_code containing '.murabaha.' and disb_date >= dt_appl,
      f_get_db_id() in (39801, 41701) and
      exists(
        select *
        from kontract
          inner join product
            on pr_id = k_from_pr_id
              and pr_code containing '.murabaha.'
        where k_id = :k_id
          and k_disb_date >= :dt_appl)));
end
F_NEW_PERC_ACTIVE(REP_DATE)→ D_BOOL
REP_DATE DATE
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_NEW_PERC_ACTIVE(
    REP_DATE D_DATE)
RETURNS D_BOOL
AS
declare variable res d_bool;
declare variable start_date d_date;
begin
  -- Временная функция для определения активности нового алгоритма начисления процентов

  res = f_tmp_const('new_classic_perc_active');
  if (res = 1) then
  begin
    start_date = f_tmp_const('new_classic_perc_start_date');
    if (rep_date < start_date) then
      res = 0;
  end

  return res;
end
F_NUMTOSTR(NUMBER, SCALE)→ D_STR_CODE
NUMBER DECIMAL(18,4)
SCALE SMALLINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_NUMTOSTR(
    NUMBER D_CURRENCY,
    SCALE D_SMALLINT)
RETURNS D_STR_CODE
AS
declare variable str d_str_code;
declare variable res d_str_code;
declare variable len d_smallint;
begin
  if (number is null) then return '';
  scale = coalesce(scale, 0);
  if (scale < 0) then scale = 0;
  if (scale > 4) then scale = 4;

  -- Вставляем пробел, после каждых трех цифр
  str = trunc(number, 0);
  res = '';
  len = char_length(str);
  while (len > 3) do
  begin
    res = ' ' || right(str, 3) || res;
    len = len - 3;
    str = left(str, len);
  end
  res = str || res;

  -- Добавляем дробную часть
  if (scale > 0) then
  res = res || right(round(number, scale), scale + 1);
  return res;
end
F_OBJKIND(OBJ_KIND, RES_KIND)→ D_STR_100 DETERMINISTIC
OBJ_KIND VARCHAR(100)
RES_KIND VARCHAR(50)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_OBJKIND(
    OBJ_KIND D_STR_100,
    RES_KIND D_STR_CODE)
RETURNS D_STR_100 DETERMINISTIC
AS
declare variable table_id d_str_100;
declare variable table_name d_str_100;
declare variable table_field d_str_100;
declare variable result d_str_100;
declare variable num d_integer;
begin
  if (res_kind = 'code') then
    res_kind = 'name'; -- часто путаем с enum и пишем на автомате code. Решено принять слово code как синоним name.

  if (res_kind not in ('id', 'name', 'field')) then
    exception e_default 'Invalid res_kind: ' || coalesce(res_kind, '<NULL>');

  if (coalesce(obj_kind, '') = '') then
    exception e_default 'Empty obj_kind';

  begin
    num = obj_kind;
    when any do
      num = null;
  end

  if (num is not null) then
  begin
    select obk_id, obk_code, obk_pk_field
    from objkind
    where obk_id = :num
    into :table_id, :table_name, :table_field;
  end else
  begin
    select obk_id, obk_code, obk_pk_field
    from objkind
    where obk_code = :obj_kind
    into :table_id, :table_name, :table_field;
  end

  if (row_count = 0) then
    exception e_default 'Table or ID not found, obj_kind: ' || coalesce(obj_kind, '<NULL>');

  result = case res_kind
             when 'id' then table_id
             when 'name' then table_name
             else table_field
           end;

  return result;
end
F_OBJNAME(OBJ_KIND, OBJ_ID)→ D_STR_LONG
Функция возвращает наименование объекта по его ID
OBJ_KIND VARCHAR(50)
OBJ_ID VARCHAR(50)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION F_OBJNAME(
    OBJ_KIND D_STR_CODE,
    OBJ_ID D_STR_CODE)
RETURNS D_STR_LONG
AS
declare variable res_text d_str_long;
declare variable db_id d_integer;
begin
  -- Функция возвращает наименование объекта по его ID
  -- Данная функция в основном используется в процедурах поиска Find_US_...
  -- и при загрузке данных для окон редактирования, где используется компонент CxFsButtonEdit

  db_id = f_get_db_id();

  if (coalesce(obj_id, '') = '') then
    return '';
  obj_kind = f_objkind(obj_kind, 'name');

  res_text = '';
  if (obj_kind = 'ref_account') then
  begin
    select f_fmt_acc(acc_id) || '      ' || coalesce(acc_name, '')
    from ref_account
    where acc_id = :obj_id
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'acc_list') then
  begin
    select al_account || '   ' || al_currency || '      ' || al_name
    from acc_list
    where al_id = :obj_id
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'clients') then
  begin
    select c_name_fio
    from clients
    where c_id = :obj_id
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'mc_login') then
  begin
    select mcl.mcl_login
    from mc_login  mcl
    where mcl.mcl_id = :obj_id
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'kontmatag') then
  begin
    select k_kontract_no || ' от ' || f_datetostr(k_disb_date) || ' г.'
    from kontract k
    inner join kontmatag kma on kma.kma_from_k_id = k.k_id and
          kma.kma_id = :obj_id

    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'cardaktclient') then
  begin
    select c.c_name_fio
    from clients c
    inner join cardaktclient cac on cac.cac_from_c_id = c.c_id
    where cac.cac_id = :obj_id
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'cardblockopers') then
  begin
    select c.c_name_fio
    from clients c
    inner join cardblockopers cbo on cbo.cbo_from_c_id = c.c_id
    where cbo.cbo_id = :obj_id
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'kontract_istinaa_prepay') then
  begin
    select k_kontract_no || ' от ' || f_datetostr(k_disb_date) || ' г.'
    from kontract k
    inner join kontract_istinaa_prepay kip on kip.kip_from_k_id = k.k_id and
          kip.kip_id = :obj_id
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'reconciliation_info') then
  begin
    select c_name_fio || f_datetostr(ri_date_begin) || ' по ' || f_datetostr(ri_date_end)
    from reconciliation_info
    inner join clients cl on c_id = ri_from_c_id
    where ri_id = :obj_id
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'kn_obdetail') then
  begin
    select obd_detail
    from kn_obdetail
    where obd_id = :obj_id
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'ref_accbalance') then
  begin
    select ab_code || '      ' || coalesce(ab_name, '')
    from ref_accbalance
    where ab_id = :obj_id
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'kontract') then
  begin
    select k_kontract_no || ' от ' || iif(:db_id <> 81001, f_datetostr(k_disb_date), f_datetostr(k_kontract_date)) || ' г.'
    from kontract
    where k_id = :obj_id
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'knclient') then
  begin
    select k_kontract_no || ' от ' || iif(:db_id <> 81001, f_datetostr(k_disb_date), f_datetostr(k_kontract_date)) || ' г.'
    from kontract kont
    inner join knclient kncl on kont.k_id = kncl.kc_from_k_id
    where kc_id = :obj_id
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'miniapp') then
  begin
    select ma_no || ' от ' || f_datetostr(ma_date_app) || ' г.'
    from miniapp
    where ma_id = :obj_id
    order by ma_id, ma_number
    rows 1
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'agreements') then
  begin
    select rd_name || ' №' || coalesce(ag_agreement_no, ag_id) || ' от ' || f_datetostr(ag_agreement_date) || ' г.'
    from agreements, refdata
    where ag_id = :obj_id and
          ag_from_rd_id = rd_id
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'ref_moneyoper') then
  begin
    select rmo_code || iif(coalesce(rmo_name, '-') <> '-', '      ' || coalesce(rmo_name, ''), '')
    from ref_moneyoper
    where rmo_id = :obj_id
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'com_list') then
  begin
    select cml_id || ' от ' || f_datetostr(cml_date) || ' г.'
    from com_list
    where cml_id = :obj_id
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'kontpsk') then
  begin
    select 'Информационный расчет для дог. № ' || k_kontract_no || ' от ' || ol_date || ' г.'
    from kontpsk, operlist, kontract
    where kps_id = :obj_id and
          kps_from_ol_id = ol_id and
          kps_from_k_id = k_id
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'ft_anketa_list') then
  begin
    select fal_id || ' от ' || f_datetostr(fal_date) || ' г.' || iif(fal_need_check = 1, ' (треб. проверка)', '')
    from ft_anketa_list
    where fal_id = :obj_id
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'expressapp') then
  begin
    select '№ ' || exa_id || ', ' || c_name_fio
    from expressapp, clients
    where exa_id = :obj_id and
          exa_from_c_id = c_id
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'finerefund') then
  begin
    select k_kontract_no || ' от ' || f_datetostr(k_disb_date) || ' г.'
    from kontract kt
    inner join finerefund fr on fr.fr_from_k_id = kt.k_id
    where fr.fr_id = :obj_id
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'app_return_consult') then
  begin
    select arc.arc_id || ' от ' || f_datetostr(arc.arc_date) || ' г.'
    from app_return_consult arc
    where arc.arc_id = :obj_id
    into :res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'overpayment') then
  begin
    select k_kontract_no || ' от ' || f_datetostr(k_disb_date) || ' г.'
    from overpayment

    where ovp_id = :obj_id
    into res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'cardpinchange') then
  begin
    select f_format('№ %1 от %2 г.', cph_id, f_datetostr(cph_date))
    from cardpinchange
    where cph_id = :obj_id
    into :res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind in ('freelancer_kontract', 'probationer_kontract', 'kochegar_kontract', 'add_for_freelancers', 'termination_freelancer')) then
  begin
    select coalesce(sd_dogovor_no, '') || ' от ' || f_datetostr(sd_date_begin) || ' г.'
    from servicedog
    where sd_id = :obj_id
    into :res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'freelancer_akt') then
  begin
    select coalesce(sd_dogovor_no, '') || ' от ' || f_datetostr(sd_date_begin) || ' г.'
    from servicesh_edoc
    inner join servicedog sd on sshe_from_sd_id = sd_id
    where sshe_id = :obj_id
    into :res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'mc_purchase') then
  begin
    select cl.c_name_fio
    from mc_purchase mp
    inner join miniappclient mac on mac.mac_from_ma_id = mp.mp_from_ma_id
    inner join clients cl on cl.c_id = mac.mac_from_c_id
    where mp.mp_id = :obj_id
    into :res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'opercash') then
  begin
    select trim(iif(opc_sign = -1, 'РКО', iif(opc_sign = 1, 'ОВН', '')) || ' № ' ||  opc_doc_no || ' от ' || f_datetostr(opc_date) || ' г.')
    from opercash
    where opc_id = :obj_id
    into :res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'transfertmc') then
  begin
    select trt_number || ' от ' || f_datetostr(trt_date) || ' г.'
    from transfertmc
    where trt_id = abs(:obj_id)
    into :res_text;
  end
  else

  begin
    select fg_id || ' от ' || f_datetostr(fg_dtwriteoff) || ' г.'
    from fuelgenreg
    where fg_id = abs(:obj_id)
    into :res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'accesscashroom') then
  begin
    select acr_id || ' от ' || f_datetostr(acr_date) || ' г.'
    from accesscashroom
    where acr_id = :obj_id
    into :res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'cashcheckmain') then
  begin
    select ccm_id || ' от ' || f_datetostr(ccm_date) || ' г.'
    from cashcheckmain
    where ccm_id = :obj_id
    into :res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'signdoc') then
  begin
    select sgn_id || ' от ' || f_datetostr(sgn_datetime) || ' г.'
    from signdoc
    where sgn_id = :obj_id
    into :res_text;
  end
  else
  if (obj_kind = 'info_loan_client') then
  begin
    select 'по состоянию на конец ' || f_datetostr(ilc_date)
    from info_loan_client
    where ilc_id = :obj_id

  if (obj_kind = 'invmember') then

    select cl.c_name_fio
    from invmember imem
    inner join clients cl on cl.c_id = imem.imem_from_c_id

           sawt_name

    where ho_id = :obj_id and
          ho_order_type = sawt_id

  end
