begin
  return null;
end
UXF$ACCESS(UN_ID, UXA_UUID, APP_NAME, UXA_UUID_CODE)→ D_SMALLINT
UN_ID BIGINT
UXA_UUID CHAR(16)
APP_NAME VARCHAR(50)
UXA_UUID_CODE VARCHAR(50)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$ACCESS(
    UN_ID D_ID,
    UXA_UUID D_UUID,
    APP_NAME D_STR_CODE = null,
    UXA_UUID_CODE D_STR_CODE = null)
RETURNS D_SMALLINT
AS
declare variable has_access d_smallint;
declare variable uxa_id d_integer;
declare variable uxa_in_mainmenu d_bool;
declare variable mm_uuid d_uuid;
declare variable ml_id d_integer;
declare variable ux_draft_user d_str_code;
declare variable ux_draft_uuid d_uuid;
declare variable un_name d_str_code;
declare variable uxa_need_access d_bool;
begin
  -- Проверяет наличие доступа пользователя к действию
  -- Результаты:
  --   -1 - нет доступа
  --    0 - доступ в режиме чтения. Пока это не используется.
  --    1 - редактирование, по настройкам формы

  if (coalesce(uxa_uuid, '') = '') then
    uxa_uuid = uxf$uniq(uxa_uuid_code);

  if (un_id is null) then
    un_id = uxf$user();

  has_access = uxf$user_is('superuser', un_id);

  if (has_access = 0) then
  begin
    has_access = -1;
  
    select uxa_id, uxa_in_mainmenu, uxa_need_access, ux_draft_user, ux_draft_uuid
    from ux$alias, ux$code
    where uxa_uuid = :uxa_uuid and
          uxa_owner = ux_uuid
    into uxa_id, uxa_in_mainmenu, uxa_need_access, ux_draft_user, ux_draft_uuid;
  
    if (uxa_in_mainmenu = 1) then
    begin
      -- Алиас прикреплен к главному меню, проверяем доступ на уровне меню
      mm_uuid = uxf$mainmenu();
  
      for select uml_ml_id
      from ux$action, ux$menulist
      where ua_owner = :mm_uuid and
            ua_uxa_uuid = :uxa_uuid and
            uml_ua_uuid = ua_uuid
      into ml_id
      do
      begin
        if (exists(select *
                   from hp_permission_access_check(:un_id, 'menulist', :ml_id, '')
                   where has_access = 1)) then
        begin
          has_access = 1;
          break;
        end
      end
    end else
    begin
      -- Проверка прав на уровне алиаса
      if (uxa_need_access = 1) then
      begin
        if (exists(select *
                   from hp_permission_access_check(:un_id, 'uxalias', :uxa_id, '')
                   where has_access = 1)) then
          has_access = 1;
      end else
        has_access = 1;
    end
  
    if (has_access <> 1) then
    begin
      -- при работе через конфигуратор, считаем что у владельца формы есть доступ
      un_name = uxf$user_login(un_id);
      if (un_name = current_user) then
      begin
        if (un_name = ux_draft_user) then
          has_access = 1;
        else
        begin
          -- при работе через конфигуратор, считаем что у владельца текущей драфт версии формы есть доступ
          select ufm_user
          from ux$form
          where ufm_uuid = :ux_draft_uuid
          into ux_draft_user;
    
          if (un_name = ux_draft_user) then
            has_access = 1;
        end
      end
    end
  end

  return has_access;
end
UXF$APPNAME()→ D_STR_CODE DETERMINISTIC
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$APPNAME
RETURNS D_STR_CODE DETERMINISTIC
AS
declare variable res d_str_code;
begin
  res = rdb$get_context('USER_SESSION', 'APP_NAME');

  if (coalesce(res, '') = '') then
    res = 'IbExpert';

  if (res starting 'FsConfig') then
    res = 'FsConfig';
  else
  if (res starting 'FinStart2') then
    res = 'FinStart2';

  return res;
end
UXF$BLOB(BLS_ID, NEED_JSON, CAN_SAVE, CAN_PRINT)→ D_STR_8100
BLS_ID BIGINT
NEED_JSON SMALLINT
CAN_SAVE SMALLINT
CAN_PRINT SMALLINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$BLOB(
    BLS_ID D_ID,
    NEED_JSON D_BOOL = 1,
    CAN_SAVE D_BOOL = 0,
    CAN_PRINT D_BOOL = 0)
RETURNS D_STR_8100
AS
declare variable res_str d_str_8100;
declare variable pos d_integer;
declare variable last_pos d_integer;
begin
  if (coalesce(need_json, '') = '') then
    need_json = 1;
 
  res_str = '';
  if (bls_id is not null) then
  begin
    select bls_filename,
           iif(bls_kind = 4 and bls_source_system = 0, 1, 0)
    from blobstore
    where bls_id = :bls_id
    into res_str, can_save;

    can_print = can_save;
  end

  if (need_json = 1) then
  begin
    if (bls_id is not null) then
    begin
      res_str = f_image_item('blobstore', bls_id, '', null, 0, can_save, can_print);
 
      -- изменим json для отображения файлов прикреплееных не в боевой базе
      if (not exists(select * from hp_db_is_actual)) then
        res_str = f_json_str_set_value(res_str, 'blob_db', '');
    end
  end
  else
  begin
    pos = position('.', res_str);
    last_pos = pos;
    while (pos > 0) do
    begin
      last_pos = pos;
      pos = position('.', res_str, pos + 1);
    end
 
    if (last_pos > 0) then
     res_str = substring(res_str from last_pos + 1);
 
    if (coalesce(res_str, '') = '') then
      res_str = '(пусто)';
    else
      res_str = upper(res_str) || ' файл';
  end
 
  return res_str;
end
UXF$CHECK(IS_OK, ERR_MESSAGE)→ BOOLEAN
IS_OK BOOLEAN
ERR_MESSAGE VARCHAR(1024)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$CHECK(
    IS_OK BOOLEAN,
    ERR_MESSAGE D_STR_LONG)
RETURNS BOOLEAN
AS
begin
  if (is_ok is null) then
    is_ok = True;

  if (not is_ok) then
  begin
    if (trim(coalesce(err_message, '')) = '') then
      err_message = 'Ошибка при выполнении команды';

    exception e_message err_message;
  end else
    return True;
end
UXF$CLASS_IS(CLASS_NAME, PARENT_CLASS)→ D_BOOL
Проверяет, является ли class_name потомком parent_class
CLASS_NAME VARCHAR(50)
PARENT_CLASS VARCHAR(50)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$CLASS_IS(
    CLASS_NAME D_STR_CODE,
    PARENT_CLASS D_STR_CODE)
RETURNS D_BOOL
AS
declare variable cls_parent d_str_code;
begin
  -- Проверяет, является ли class_name потомком parent_class

  cls_parent = class_name;
  while (cls_parent is not null) do
  begin
    if (cls_parent = parent_class) then
      return 1;

    select cls_parent
    from ux$class
    where cls_name = :cls_parent
    into cls_parent;
  end

  return 0;
end
UXF$CODE(UX_UUID, RES_KIND, ERROR_IF_NOT_EXISTS)→ D_STR_100
Возвращает инфо об объекте по UUID
UX_UUID CHAR(16)
RES_KIND VARCHAR(50)
ERROR_IF_NOT_EXISTS SMALLINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$CODE(
    UX_UUID D_UUID,
    RES_KIND D_STR_CODE,
    ERROR_IF_NOT_EXISTS D_BOOL = 1)
RETURNS D_STR_100
AS
declare variable res_str d_str_100;
declare variable ux_kind d_str_code;
declare variable ux_code d_str_100;
declare variable ux_fcode d_str_100;
declare variable ux_name d_str_100;
declare variable is_base d_bool;
declare variable ux_caption d_str_100;
declare variable item_uuid d_uuid;
declare variable tmp_code d_str_code;
begin
  res_str = '';

  if (ux_uuid is not null) then
  begin
    if (trim(coalesce(res_kind, '')) = '') then
      exception e_default 'Res_kind is empty';
  
    select uxf$kind(uu_kind, 'code'), uu_is_base
    from ux$uniq
    where uu_id = :ux_uuid
    into :ux_kind, is_base;

    if (row_count = 0) then
    begin
      select uni_code, iif(coalesce(uni_caption, '') <> '', uni_caption, uni_code)
      from ux$unititem
      where uni_uuid = :ux_uuid
      into ux_code, ux_name;

      if (row_count = 0) then
      begin
        if (error_if_not_exists = 1) then
          exception e_default f_format('Ux item not found: %1', uuid_to_char(ux_uuid));
        else
           return res_str;
      end else
      begin
        ux_kind = 'uxunit';

        if (res_kind = 'kind') then
          return ux_kind;
        else
        if (res_kind = 'fcode') then
          return uuid_to_char(ux_uuid);
        else
        if (res_kind = 'code') then
          return ux_code;
        else
        if (res_kind = 'name') then
          return ux_name;
        else
        if (res_kind = 'caption') then
        begin
          ux_caption = ux_name;
          if (ux_caption not containing ux_code) then
            ux_caption = ux_caption || '    / ' || ux_code;

          return ux_caption;
        end
      end
    end

    if (res_kind = 'kind') then
      return ux_kind;
  
    if (ux_kind = 'uxextform') then
    begin
      select efm_code, efm_name
      from ux$extform
      where efm_uuid = :ux_uuid
      into ux_code, ux_name;

      ux_caption = ux_name;
      if (ux_caption not containing ux_code) then
        ux_caption = ux_caption || '    / ' || ux_code;

      ux_fcode = ux_code;
    end else
    if (ux_kind = 'uxtree') then
    begin
      select utr_code, utr_name
      from ux$tree
      where utr_uuid = :ux_uuid
      into ux_code, ux_name;

      ux_caption = ux_name;
      if (ux_caption not containing ux_code) then
        ux_caption = ux_caption || '    / ' || ux_code;

      ux_fcode = uuid_to_char(ux_uuid);
    end else
    if (ux_kind = 'uxsource') then
    begin
      select us_code, us_name
      from ux$source
      where us_uuid = :ux_uuid
      into ux_code, ux_name;

      ux_caption = ux_name;
      if (ux_caption not containing ux_code) then
        ux_caption = ux_caption || '  (' || ux_code || ')';

      ux_fcode = ux_code;
    end else
    if (ux_kind = 'uxfield') then
    begin
      if (res_kind <> 'fcode') then
      begin
        select uf_field_name, uf_name
        from ux$field
        where uf_uuid = :ux_uuid
        into ux_code, ux_name;

        ux_caption = ux_name;
        if (ux_caption not containing ux_code) then
          ux_caption = ux_caption || '  (' || ux_code || ')';
      end else
        ux_fcode = uuid_to_char(ux_uuid);
    end else
    if (ux_kind = 'uxfritem') then
    begin
      if (res_kind = 'name') then
      begin
        select ufi_name
        from ux$fritem
        where ufi_uuid = :ux_uuid
        into ux_name;
      end else
      if (res_kind = 'caption') then
      begin
        select ufi_name,
               iif(ufi_parent_uuid is null, ufi_owner,
                   iif(ufi_uf_uuid is not null, ufi_uf_uuid,
                       iif(ufi_kind = 1, ufi_ufr_uuid, null)))
        from ux$fritem
        where ufi_uuid = :ux_uuid
        into ux_name, item_uuid;

        ux_caption = ux_name;
        if (item_uuid is not null) then
        begin
          tmp_code = uxf$code(item_uuid, 'code');
          if (ux_caption not containing tmp_code) then
            ux_caption = ux_caption || '  (' || tmp_code || ')';
        end
      end else
      begin
        ux_code = uuid_to_char(ux_uuid);
        ux_fcode = ux_code;
      end
    end else
    if (ux_kind = 'uxfrparam') then
    begin
      if (res_kind = 'name') then
      begin
        select ufp_uf_uuid
        from ux$frparam
        where ufp_uuid = :ux_uuid
        into item_uuid;

        ux_name = uxf$code(item_uuid, 'name');
      end else
      if (res_kind = 'caption') then
      begin
        select ufp_uf_uuid
        from ux$frparam
        where ufp_uuid = :ux_uuid
        into item_uuid;

        ux_caption = uxf$code(item_uuid, 'caption');
      end else
      begin
        ux_code = uuid_to_char(ux_uuid);
        ux_fcode = ux_code;
      end
    end else
    if (ux_kind = 'uxaction') then
    begin
      if (res_kind in ('name', 'caption')) then
      begin
        select ua_name
        from ux$action
        where ua_uuid = :ux_uuid
        into ux_name;

        ux_caption = ux_name;
      end else
      begin
        ux_code = uuid_to_char(ux_uuid);
        ux_fcode = ux_code;
      end
    end else
    if (ux_kind = 'uxframe') then
    begin
      select ufr_fcode, ufr_code, ufr_name
      from ux$frame
      where ufr_uuid = :ux_uuid
      into ux_fcode, ux_code, ux_name;

      ux_caption = ux_name;
      if (ux_caption not containing ux_code) then
        ux_caption = ux_caption || '  (' || ux_code || ')';
    end else
    if (ux_kind = 'uxalias') then
    begin
      select uxa_fcode, uxa_code, uxa_name
      from ux$alias
      where uxa_uuid = :ux_uuid
      into ux_fcode, ux_code, ux_name;

      ux_caption = ux_name;
      if (ux_caption not containing ux_code) then
        ux_caption = ux_caption || '  (' || ux_code || ')';
    end else
    if (ux_kind = 'uxgrid') then
    begin
      if (res_kind in ('name', 'caption')) then
      begin
        select ugr_name
        from ux$grid
        where ugr_uuid = :ux_uuid
        into ux_name;

        ux_caption = ux_name;
      end else
      begin
        ux_code = uuid_to_char(ux_uuid);
        ux_fcode = ux_code;
      end
    end else
    if (ux_kind = 'uxgritem') then
    begin
      ux_code = uuid_to_char(ux_uuid);
      ux_name = ux_code;
      ux_fcode = ux_code;
      ux_caption = ux_name;
    end else
    if (ux_kind = 'uxevent') then
    begin
      ux_code = uuid_to_char(ux_uuid);
      ux_name = ux_code;
      ux_fcode = ux_code;
      ux_caption = ux_name;
    end else
    begin
      if (is_base = 1) then
      begin
        select ux_scode, ux_fcode, ux_name
        from ux$code
        where ux_uuid = :ux_uuid
        into :ux_code, :ux_fcode, :ux_name;

        ux_caption = ux_name;
        if (ux_caption not containing ux_code) then
          ux_caption = ux_caption || '  (' || ux_code || ')';
      end else
      begin
        select ux_scode, ux_fcode, ux_name
        from ux$form, ux$code
        where ufm_uuid = :ux_uuid and
              ux_uuid = ufm_ux_uuid
        into :ux_code, :ux_fcode, :ux_name;

        ux_caption = ux_name;
        if (ux_caption not containing ux_code) then
          ux_caption = ux_caption || '  (' || ux_code || ')';
      end

      if (error_if_not_exists = 1 and row_count = 0) then
        exception e_default f_format('Unknown uuid type: ', uuid_to_char(ux_uuid));
    end

    if (res_kind = 'fcode') then
      res_str = ux_fcode;
    else
    if (res_kind = 'code') then
      res_str = ux_code;
    else
    if (res_kind = 'name') then
      res_str = ux_name;
    else
    if (res_kind = 'caption') then
      res_str = ux_caption;
    else
      f_format('Unknown res_kind: %1', res_kind);
  end
  return res_str;
end
UXF$CONST(CONST_NAME)→ D_STR_CODE DETERMINISTIC
CONST_NAME VARCHAR(50) — Кодовое имя константы
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$CONST(
    CONST_NAME D_STR_CODE NOT NULL)
RETURNS D_STR_CODE DETERMINISTIC
AS
declare variable res_value d_str_code;
begin
  -- не менять значения ux$const, это базовые настройки

  const_name = upper(const_name);
  res_value = rdb$get_context('USER_SESSION', :const_name);

  if (coalesce(res_value, '') = '') then
  begin
    select cnt_value
    from ux$const
    where cnt_code = :const_name
    into res_value;

    if (row_count = 0) then
      exception e_default 'Unknown system constant (ux$const): ' || const_name;

    rdb$set_context('USER_SESSION', :const_name, :res_value);
    when any do begin end -- Может быть Update conflicts with concurrent update, игнорируем
  end

  return res_value;
end
UXF$CRIPT(ACTION_KIND, INPUT_TEXT)→ D_STR_255
ACTION_KIND VARCHAR(50)
INPUT_TEXT VARCHAR(255)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$CRIPT(
    ACTION_KIND D_STR_CODE,
    INPUT_TEXT D_STR_255)
RETURNS D_STR_255
AS
declare variable res d_str_255;
begin
  if (input_text is null) then
  begin
    input_text = '';
    return input_text;
  end

  if (action_kind = 'on') then
  begin
    res = input_text || input_text;
  end else
  if (action_kind = 'off') then
  begin
    res = left(input_text, char_length(input_text) / 2);
  end else
    exception e_default 'Unknown action_kind: ' || coalesce(action_kind, '<Null>');

  return res;
end

  db_actual = uxf$const('db_actual');

  return (db_path = db_actual);
end

UXF$DOCFRAME(UFM_UUID)→ D_UUID
UFM_UUID CHAR(16)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$DOCFRAME(
    UFM_UUID D_UUID)
RETURNS D_UUID
AS
declare variable res_id d_integer;
declare variable add_table d_str_code;
declare variable form_kind d_smallint;
declare variable res_uuid d_uuid;
begin
  res_id = 0;

  if (uxf$pro()) then
  begin
    for select us_is_doc, ufr_add_table_fields, uu_kind, ufr_uuid
    from ux$form, ux$frame, ux$source, ux$uniq
    where ufm_uuid = :ufm_uuid and
          ufr_owner = ufm_uuid and
          ((ufm_doc_frame_code = '' and ufr_is_mainframe = 1) or (ufr_fcode = ufm_doc_frame_code)) and
          ufr_us_uuid = us_uuid and
          ufm_ux_uuid = uu_id
    rows 1
    into res_id, add_table, form_kind, res_uuid
    do
    begin
      if (uxf$kind(form_kind, 'code') in ('uxcommand', 'uxreport')) then
        res_id = 0;
      else
      begin
        if (res_id = 0 and coalesce(add_table, '') <> '') then
        begin
          select us_is_doc
          from ux$source
          where us_code = :add_table
          into res_id;
        end
      end
    end
  end

  if (res_id = 0) then
    res_uuid = null;

  return res_uuid;
end
UXF$EDOCS(EDL_ID, UN_ID)→ D_STR_LONG
EDL_ID BIGINT
UN_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$EDOCS(
    EDL_ID D_ID,
    UN_ID D_ID)
RETURNS D_STR_LONG
AS
declare variable res_data d_str_8100;
declare variable caption d_str_100;
declare variable json_item d_str_long;
declare variable json_list d_str_8100; /* d_str_8100; */
declare variable can_export d_smallint;
declare variable edb_id d_id;
begin
  -- Процедура возвращает бланки электронного документа
  res_data = '';
  can_export = 1; -- Права пользователя выгружать и печатать документы

  -- Бланки электронного документа
  json_item = '';
  json_list = '';

  if (un_id is null) then
    un_id = uxf$user();

  for select edb_id, edf_print_name
  from edoc_list, edoc_blank, edoc_frx
  where edl_id = :edl_id and
        edl_blank_status = 1 and
        edb_from_edl_id = edl_id and
        edb_from_edf_id = edf_id
  order by edb_from_c_id
  into edb_id, caption
  do
  begin
    json_item = f_image_item('edoc_blank', edb_id, caption, un_id, 0, can_export, can_export);
    json_list = json_list || iif(json_list <> '', ',', '') || json_item;
  end
  if (json_list <> '') then
  begin
    res_data = res_data || iif(res_data <> '', ',', '') ||
      '{' ||
      f_json_make_pair('caption', 'Эл.документ № ' || edl_id , 'str') || ',' ||
      '"auto_load":0, "blobs":[' || json_list || ']' ||
      '}';
  end

  res_data = '[' || res_data || ']';
  res_data = replace(res_data, ',,', ',');
  res_data = replace(res_data, '},]', '}]');

  return res_data;
end
UXF$ENUM_CODE(KIND, ID_CODE, ERROR_IF_EMPTY)→ D_STR_CODE DETERMINISTIC
KIND VARCHAR(50)
ID_CODE VARCHAR(50)
ERROR_IF_EMPTY SMALLINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$ENUM_CODE(
    KIND D_STR_CODE,
    ID_CODE D_STR_CODE,
    ERROR_IF_EMPTY D_SMALLINT = 1)
RETURNS D_STR_CODE DETERMINISTIC
AS
declare variable code d_str_code;
declare variable num d_integer;
begin
  -- возвращает Code значения enum (констант)

  begin
    num = id_code;
    when any do
      num = null;
  end

  code = '';

  if (num is not null) then
  begin
    select el_code
    from enum_list
    where el_kind = :kind and
          el_id = :num
    into :code;
  end else
  begin
    select el_code
    from enum_list
    where el_kind = :kind and
          el_code = :id_code
    into :code;
  end

  if (row_count = 0 and error_if_empty = 1) then
    exception e_default f_format('Enum not found, kind: %1, id_code: %2', kind, id_code);

  return code;
end
UXF$ENUM_ID(KIND, ID_CODE, ERROR_IF_EMPTY)→ D_INTEGER DETERMINISTIC
KIND VARCHAR(50)
ID_CODE VARCHAR(50)
ERROR_IF_EMPTY SMALLINT

CREATE FUNCTION UXF$ENUM_ID(

    ERROR_IF_EMPTY D_SMALLINT = 1)
RETURNS D_INTEGER DETERMINISTIC
AS
declare variable id d_integer;
declare variable num d_integer;
begin
  -- возвращает ID значения enum (констант)

  begin
    num = id_code;
    when any do
      num = null;
  end

  if (num is not null) then
  begin
    select el_id
    from enum_list
    where el_kind = :kind and
          el_id = :num
    into :id;
  end else
  begin
    select el_id
    from enum_list
    where el_kind = :kind and
          el_code = :id_code
    into :id;
  end

  if (row_count = 0 and error_if_empty = 1) then
    exception e_default f_format('Enum not found, kind: %1, id_code: %2', kind, id_code);

  return id;
end
UXF$ENUM_NAME(KIND, ID_CODE, ERROR_IF_EMPTY)→ D_STR_CODE DETERMINISTIC

ID_CODE VARCHAR(50)

CREATE FUNCTION UXF$ENUM_NAME(
    KIND D_STR_CODE,

    ERROR_IF_EMPTY D_SMALLINT = 1)
RETURNS D_STR_CODE DETERMINISTIC
AS
declare variable name d_str_code;
declare variable num d_integer;
begin
  -- возвращает Name значения enum (констант)
  begin
    num = id_code;
    when any do
      num = null;
  end

  name = '';

  if (num is not null) then
  begin
    select el_name
    from enum_list
    where el_kind = :kind and
          el_id = :num
    into :name;
  end else
  begin
    select el_name
    from enum_list
    where el_kind = :kind and
          el_code = :id_code
    into :name;
  end

  if (row_count = 0 and error_if_empty = 1) then
    exception e_default f_format('Enum not found, kind: %1, id_code: %2', kind, id_code);

  return name;
end
UXF$FLAG(FLAG_CODE, ACTION_KIND)→ D_BOOL
Возвращает статус флага по кодовому имени. Action_kind = On/Off включает или отключает флаг
FLAG_CODE VARCHAR(50)
ACTION_KIND VARCHAR(50)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$FLAG(
    FLAG_CODE D_STR_CODE,
    ACTION_KIND D_STR_CODE = 'get')
RETURNS D_BOOL
AS
declare variable res d_bool;
begin
  if (trim(coalesce(action_kind, '')) = '') then
    action_kind = 'get';

  if (action_kind not in ('on', 'off', 'get')) then
    exception e_default 'Unknown action_kind: ' || action_kind;

  flag_code = lower(trim(coalesce(flag_code, '')));
  if (flag_code = '') then
    exception e_default 'Empty flag_code';

  res = 0;
  if (action_kind = 'get') then
    res = coalesce(rdb$get_context('USER_TRANSACTION', :flag_code), 0);
  else
  if (action_kind = 'on') then
  begin
    rdb$set_context('USER_TRANSACTION', :flag_code, '1');
    res = 1;
  end else
  if (action_kind = 'off') then
  begin
    if (rdb$get_context('USER_TRANSACTION', :flag_code) is not null) then
      rdb$set_context('USER_TRANSACTION', :flag_code, null);
  end

  return res;
end

end

UXF$INT_TO_IP(INT_IP_ADDRESS)→ D_STR_CODE
Преобразует числовое IP адрес к текстовому
INT_IP_ADDRESS BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$INT_TO_IP(
    INT_IP_ADDRESS D_ID)
RETURNS D_STR_CODE
AS
declare variable res_ip d_str_code;
declare variable ip_block d_id;
declare variable ip_num d_integer;
begin
  if (coalesce(INT_IP_ADDRESS, 0) = 0) then
    return '';

  res_ip = '';
  while (int_ip_address > 0) do
  begin
    ip_num = ip_num + 1;
    ip_block = mod(int_ip_address, 256);
    int_ip_address = trunc(int_ip_address / 256);

    if (res_ip <> '') then
      res_ip = '.' || res_ip;

    res_ip = ip_block || res_ip;
  end

  return res_ip;
end
UXF$IP_TO_INT(IP_ADDRESS)→ D_ID
Преобразует текстовый IP адрес к числовому
IP_ADDRESS VARCHAR(50)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$IP_TO_INT(
    IP_ADDRESS D_STR_CODE)
RETURNS D_ID
AS
declare variable res_ip d_id;
declare variable pos d_integer;
declare variable ip_block d_id;
declare variable ip_num d_integer;
begin
  if (coalesce(ip_address, '') = '') then
    return 0;

  pos = position('/', ip_address);
  if (pos > 0) then
    ip_address = left(ip_address, pos - 1);

  ip_num = 0;
  res_ip = 0;

  for select trim(res_str)
  from uxp$split_str(:ip_address, '.')
  into :ip_block
  do
  begin
    ip_num = ip_num + 1;
    res_ip = res_ip * 256 + ip_block;
  end

  if (ip_num not in (4, 6)) then
    exception e_default 'Incorrect IP address: ' || ip_address;

  return res_ip;
end

end
UXF$IS_RUS(TEXT)→ D_BOOLEAN
TEXT VARCHAR(255)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$IS_RUS(
    TEXT D_STR_255)
RETURNS D_BOOLEAN
AS
declare variable c d_str_sym;
declare variable i d_integer;
declare variable len d_integer;
begin
  len = char_length(coalesce(text, ''));
  if (len > 0) then
  begin
    I = 0;
    while (I < len) do
    begin
      I = I + 1;
      c = substring(text from I for 1);
      if (c between 'а' and 'я' or
          c between 'А' and 'Я' or
          c = 'ё' or c = 'Ё') then
        return true;
    end
  end

  return false;
end
UXF$JOIN_STR(STR_1, STR_2, DELIMITER)→ D_STR_LONG
Объединение строк
STR_1 VARCHAR(255)
STR_2 VARCHAR(255)
DELIMITER VARCHAR(1)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$JOIN_STR(
    STR_1 D_STR_255,
    STR_2 D_STR_255,
    DELIMITER D_STR_SYM = null)
RETURNS D_STR_LONG
AS
declare variable res d_str_long;
begin
  if (coalesce(delimiter, '') = '') then
    delimiter = '';

  res = '';
  str_1 = trim(coalesce(str_1, ''));
  str_2 = trim(coalesce(str_2, ''));

  if (str_1 <> '' or str_2 <> '') then
  begin
    if (str_1 = '' or str_2 containing str_1) then
      res = str_2;
    else
    if (str_2 = '' or str_1 containing str_2) then
      res = str_1;
    else
    begin
      res = str_1;
      if (delimiter = '') then
        res = res || '    (' || str_2 || ')';
      else
        res = res || '    ' || delimiter || ' ' || str_2;
    end
  end

  return res;
end
UXF$KIND(UX_KIND, RES_KIND)→ D_STR_CODE DETERMINISTIC
Числовые и кодовые обозначения типа объекта в поле UxCode.Ux_Kind
UX_KIND VARCHAR(100)
RES_KIND VARCHAR(50)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$KIND(
    UX_KIND D_STR_100,
    RES_KIND D_STR_CODE)
RETURNS D_STR_CODE DETERMINISTIC
AS
declare variable id d_str_100;
declare variable code d_str_100;
declare variable caption d_str_100;
declare variable prefix d_str_100;
declare variable result d_str_100;
begin
  if (res_kind not in ('id', 'code', 'caption', 'prefix')) then
    exception e_default 'Invalid res_kind: ' || coalesce(res_kind, '<NULL>');

  if (coalesce(ux_kind, '') = '') then
    exception e_default 'Empty ux_kind';

  if (ux_kind in ('-4', 'uxextform')) then
  begin
    id = -4;
    code = 'uxextform';
    caption = 'Внешняя форма';
    prefix = '';
  end else
  if (ux_kind in ('-3', 'uxtree')) then
  begin
    id = -3;
    code = 'uxtree';
    caption = 'Структура';
    prefix = '';
  end else
  if (ux_kind in ('-2', 'uxfield')) then
  begin
    id = -2;
    code = 'uxfield';
    caption = 'Поле';
    prefix = '';
  end else
  if (ux_kind in ('-1', 'uxsource')) then
  begin
    id = -1;
    code = 'uxsource';
    caption = 'Источник';
    prefix = '';
  end else
  if (ux_kind in ('0', 'uxframe')) then
  begin
    id = 0;
    code = 'uxframe';
    caption = 'Фрейм';
    prefix = 'fr_';
  end else
  if (ux_kind in ('1', 'uxform')) then
  begin
    id = 1;
    code = 'uxform';
    caption = 'Форма';
    prefix = 'fm_';
  end else
  if (ux_kind in ('2', 'uxalias')) then
  begin
    id = 2;
    code = 'uxalias';
    caption = 'Алиас';
    prefix = 'a';
  end else
  if (ux_kind in ('3', 'uxcommand')) then
  begin
    id = 3;
    code = 'uxcommand';
    caption = 'Команда';
    prefix = 'cm_';
  end else
  if (ux_kind in ('4', 'uxreport')) then
  begin
    id = 4;
    code = 'uxreport';
    caption = 'Отчет';
    prefix = 'rp_';
  end else
  if (ux_kind in ('5', 'uxfritem')) then
  begin
    id = 5;
    code = 'uxfritem';
    caption = 'Элементы интерфейса';
    prefix = '';
  end else
  if (ux_kind in ('6', 'uxaction')) then
  begin
    id = 6;
    code = 'uxaction';
    caption = 'Действия';
    prefix = '';
  end else
  if (ux_kind in ('7', 'uxgrid')) then
  begin
    id = 7;
    code = 'uxgrid';
    caption = 'Шаблон';
    prefix = '';
  end else
  if (ux_kind in ('8', 'uxgritem')) then
  begin
    id = 8;
    code = 'uxgritem';
    caption = 'Поля шаблона';
    prefix = '';
  end else
  if (ux_kind in ('9', 'uxfrparam')) then
  begin
    id = 9;
    code = 'uxfrparam';
    caption = 'Поля фрейма';
    prefix = '';
  end else
  if (ux_kind in ('10', 'uxevent')) then
  begin
    id = 10;
    code = 'uxevent';
    caption = 'Обработчики';
    prefix = '';
  end else
  if (ux_kind in ('11', 'uxbugtest')) then
  begin
    id = 11;
    code = 'uxbugtest';
    caption = 'Задачи';
    prefix = '';
  end else
  if (ux_kind in ('12', 'uxfilter')) then
  begin
    id = 11;
    code = 'uxfilter';
    caption = 'Фильтр';
    prefix = '';
  end else
    exception e_default f_format('Unknown ux_kind: %1', ux_kind);

  result = trim(case res_kind
             when 'id' then id
             when 'code' then code
             when 'caption' then caption
             else prefix
           end);

  return result;
end
UXF$LANG(TEXT, LANG_CODE)→ D_STR_255
Перевод текста
TEXT VARCHAR(255) — Оригинальный текст
LANG_CODE VARCHAR(3) — Язык для перевода
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$LANG(
    TEXT D_STR_255,
    LANG_CODE D_STR_LANG = null)
RETURNS D_STR_255
AS
declare variable res_text d_str_255;
declare variable need_translit d_smallint;
declare variable trm_dont_translate d_bool;
declare variable need_temp d_bool;
declare variable is_found d_bool;
begin
  text = trim(coalesce(text, ''));
  res_text = text;
  if (text = '' or text containing '&nbsp') then
    return text;

  -- Получить язык от пользователя
  if (coalesce(lang_code, '') = '') then
    lang_code = uxf$userlang();

  if (lang_code = 'ru' and current_user <> 'sislamov') then
    return text;

  if (not uxf$pro()) then
    return text;

  if (not(uxf$is_rus(text))) then
    return text;

  is_found = 0;
  need_translit = 0;
  select trm_dont_translate, coalesce(trl_actual_text, trl_translate_text)
  from ux$transmain
         left join ux$translate on (trm_id = trl_from_trm_id and trl_lang_code = :lang_code)
  where trm_orig_text = :text
  into trm_dont_translate, res_text;

  if (row_count = 1) then
    is_found = 1;

  if (lang_code = 'uzl' and is_found = 1 and coalesce(res_text, '') = '') then
  begin
    lang_code = 'uz';
    need_translit = 1;
    is_found = 0;

    select trm_dont_translate, coalesce(trl_actual_text, trl_translate_text)
    from ux$transmain
           left join ux$translate on (trm_id = trl_from_trm_id and trl_lang_code = :lang_code)
    where trm_orig_text = :text
    into trm_dont_translate, res_text;

    if (row_count = 1) then
      is_found = 1;
  end

  need_temp = 0;
  if (is_found = 0) then
  begin
    if (not uxf$readonly()) then
    begin
      in autonomous transaction do
      begin
        insert into ux$transmain (trm_orig_text)
        values (:text);
      end
  
      when any do
        need_temp = 1;
    end else
      need_temp = 1;

    if (need_temp = 1) then
    begin
      in autonomous transaction do
      begin
        if (not exists(select * from ux$transtemp
                       where orig_text = :text)) then
        begin
          insert into ux$transtemp (orig_text)
          values (:text);
        end
      end
    end
  end

  if (coalesce(res_text, '') = '' or trm_dont_translate = 1) then
    res_text = text;
  else
  if (need_translit = 1) then
    res_text = uxf$translit('cyr', 'lat', res_text, 'uz');

  return res_text;
end
UXF$MAINMENU()→ D_UUID DETERMINISTIC
Возвращает UUID формы для главного меню

                ux_uuid = ufm_ux_uuid);
end
UXF$MASK(TEXT, MASK_KIND)→ D_STR_LONG
TEXT VARCHAR(1024) — Текст для обработки маской
MASK_KIND VARCHAR(20) — Вид маски: text, fio, cell, email, date, datetime, inn
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$MASK(
    TEXT D_STR_LONG,
    MASK_KIND D_STR_20)
RETURNS D_STR_LONG
AS
declare variable res_text d_str_long;
declare variable text_len d_integer;
declare variable at_pos d_integer;
declare variable tmp_date d_datetime;
begin
  if (text is null) then
    text = '';

  if (mask_kind is null) then
    mask_kind = '';

  if (mask_kind = '' or text = '') then
    return text;

  res_text = '';
  text = trim(text);
  text_len = char_length(text);

  -- если длина текста меньше 2 символов, не применяем маску
  if (text_len <= 2) then
    return text;

  if (mask_kind = 'fio') then
  begin
    at_pos = position(' ', text);
    if (at_pos = 0) then
      mask_kind = 'text';
  end else
  if (mask_kind = 'cell') then
  begin
    if (:text not similar to '[+]?[0-9]+') then
      mask_kind = 'text';
  end else
  if (mask_kind = 'email') then
  begin
    -- если требуется маска по имейл, но нет символа собачки, то применяем обычную маску
    at_pos = position('@', text);
    if (at_pos = 0) then
      mask_kind = 'text';
  end else
  if (mask_kind = 'date') then
  begin
    tmp_date = cast(text as date);
    when any do
      mask_kind = 'text';
  end else
  if (mask_kind = 'datetime') then
  begin
    tmp_date = cast(text as timestamp);
    when any do
      mask_kind = 'text';
  end else
  if (mask_kind = 'inn') then
  begin
    if (:text not similar to '[0-9]+') then
      mask_kind = 'text';
  end else
  if (mask_kind <> 'text') then
    exception e_default f_format('Unknown mask_kind: %1', mask_kind);

  if (mask_kind = 'text') then
  begin
    -- В каждом слове показывает по две начальные буквы, остальное заменяется звездочкой
    select list(rpad(left(res_str, 2), char_length(res_str), '*'), ' ')
    from uxp$split_str(:text, ' ')
    into :res_text;
  end else
  if (mask_kind = 'fio') then
  begin
    -- Имя + Отчетство + Первая буква фамилии
    res_text = trim(substring(text from at_pos + 1)) || ' ' || left(text, 1) || '.';
  end else
  if (mask_kind = 'cell') then
  begin
    -- 4 символа до последних двух заменяется символом *
    if (text_len > 6) then
      res_text = left(text, text_len - 6) || '****' || right(text, 2);
    else
      res_text = rpad(left(text, 1), text_len - 1, '*') || right(text, 1);
  end else
  if (mask_kind = 'inn') then
  begin
    -- 2 символа до последних двух заменяется символом *
    if (text_len > 4) then
      res_text = left(text, text_len - 4) || '**' || right(text, 2);
    else
      res_text = text;
  end else
  if (mask_kind = 'email') then
  begin
    -- Первые две буквы и остальное до @ заменяется символом *

    res_text = rpad(left(text, 2), at_pos - 1, '*');
    res_text = res_text || substring(text from at_pos);
  end else
  if (mask_kind = 'date') then
  begin
    -- Год заменяется символом *
    res_text = lpad(extract(day from tmp_date), 2, '0') || '.' ||
               lpad(extract(month from tmp_date), 2, '0')  || '.' || '100' || right(extract(year from tmp_date), 1);
  end else
  if (mask_kind = 'datetime') then
  begin
    -- Год заменяется символом *
    res_text = lpad(extract(day from tmp_date), 2, '0') || '.' ||
               lpad(extract(month from tmp_date), 2, '0')  || '.' || '100' || right(extract(year from tmp_date), 1) || ' ' ||
               lpad(extract(hour from tmp_date), 2, '0') || ':' ||
               lpad(extract(minute from tmp_date), 2, '0') ||
               trim(iif(extract(second from tmp_date) <> 0, ':' || lpad(extract(minute from tmp_date), 2, '0'), ''));
  end else
    exception e_default f_format('Unknown mask_kind: %1', mask_kind);

  return res_text;
end
UXF$MD5_UUID(TEXT)→ D_UUID
TEXT VARCHAR(1024)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$MD5_UUID(
    TEXT D_STR_LONG)
RETURNS D_UUID
AS
declare variable str_md5 char(32) character set octets;
declare variable res_uuid d_uuid;
begin
  -- Преобразовывает переданный текст в GUID
  str_md5 = f_md5(text);
  res_uuid = char_to_uuid(left(str_md5, 8) ||
             '-' || substring(str_md5 from 9 for 4) ||
             '-' || substring(str_md5 from 13 for 4) ||
             '-' || substring(str_md5 from 17 for 4) ||
             '-' || substring(str_md5 from 21 for 12));

  return res_uuid;
end

UXF$MENUPATH(ITEM_ID, ITEM_EXCEPT)→ D_STR_LONG
ITEM_ID BIGINT — Id элемента меню
ITEM_EXCEPT VARCHAR(255) — Текст - исключение
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$MENUPATH(
    ITEM_ID D_ID,
    ITEM_EXCEPT D_STR_255 = null)
RETURNS D_STR_LONG
AS
declare variable res_text d_str_long;
declare variable item_name d_str_255;
begin
  res_text = '';
  item_except = trim(coalesce(item_except, ''));

  while (item_id is not null) do
  begin
    select ml_parent_id, ml_caption
    from menulist
    where ml_id = :item_id
    into item_id, item_name;

    if (row_count = 0) then
      item_id = null;
    else
    if (coalesce(item_name, '') <> '' and item_except <> item_name) then
    begin
      if (res_text <> '') then
        res_text = ' -> ' || res_text;

      res_text = item_name || res_text;
    end
  end

  return res_text;
end
UXF$NUMTOSTR(NUMBER, SCALE)→ D_STR_CODE
NUMBER DECIMAL(18,4) — Число
SCALE SMALLINT — Кол-во после запятой
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$NUMTOSTR(
    NUMBER D_CURRENCY,
    SCALE D_SMALLINT)
RETURNS D_STR_CODE
AS
declare variable str d_str_code;
declare variable res d_str_code;
declare variable len d_smallint;
begin
  if (number is null) then return '';
  scale = coalesce(scale, 0);
  if (scale < 0) then scale = 0;
  if (scale > 4) then scale = 4;

  -- Вставляем пробел, после каждых трех цифр
  str = trunc(number, 0);
  res = '';
  len = char_length(str);
  while (len > 3) do
  begin
    res = ' ' || right(str, 3) || res;
    len = len - 3;
    str = left(str, len);
  end
  res = str || res;

  -- Добавляем дробную часть
  if (scale > 0) then
    res = res || right(round(number, scale), scale + 1);

  return res;
end
UXF$OWN(US_CODE, USER_NAME)→ D_BOOL
US_CODE VARCHAR(50)
USER_NAME VARCHAR(50)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$OWN(
    US_CODE D_STR_CODE,
    USER_NAME D_STR_CODE)
RETURNS D_BOOL
AS
declare variable us_uuid d_uuid;
declare variable tmp_user d_str_code;
begin
  if (coalesce(user_name, '') = '') then
    user_name = current_user;

  select us_uuid, us_user
  from ux$source
  where us_code = :us_code
  into us_uuid, tmp_user;

  if (tmp_user = user_name) then
    return 1;

  if (exists(select * from ux$code
  where ux_us_uuid = :us_uuid and
        ux_draft_user = :user_name)) then
    return 1;

  if (exists(select * from ux$code, ux$form
  where ux_us_uuid = :us_uuid and
        ux_uuid = ufm_ux_uuid and
        ufm_user = :user_name)) then
    return 1;

  if (exists(select * from ux$form, ux$frame
  where ufr_us_uuid = :us_uuid and
        ufr_owner = ufm_uuid and
        ufm_user = :user_name)) then
    return 1;
end
UXF$OWNER(UX_UUID, RES_KIND, ERROR_IF_NOT_EXISTS)→ D_STR_100
Возвращает информацию о владельце по UUID потомка
UX_UUID CHAR(16)
RES_KIND VARCHAR(50)
ERROR_IF_NOT_EXISTS SMALLINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$OWNER(
    UX_UUID D_UUID,
    RES_KIND D_STR_CODE,
    ERROR_IF_NOT_EXISTS D_BOOL = 1)
RETURNS D_STR_100
AS
declare variable res_str d_str_100;
declare variable ux_owner d_uuid;
declare variable ux_kind d_str_code;
begin
  select uxf$kind(uu_kind, 'code')
  from ux$uniq
  where uu_id = :ux_uuid
  into :ux_kind;

  if (row_count = 0 and error_if_not_exists = 1) then
    exception e_default f_format('Ux item not found: %1', uuid_to_char(ux_uuid));

  ux_owner = null;
  res_str = '';

  if (ux_kind = 'uxsource') then
  begin
    exception e_default 'У источника (uxSource) не бывает владельца.';
  end else
  if (ux_kind = 'uxfield') then
  begin
    select uf_owner
    from ux$field
    where uf_uuid = :ux_uuid
    into ux_owner;
  end else
  if (ux_kind = 'uxfritem') then
  begin
    select ufi_owner
    from ux$fritem
    where ufi_uuid = :ux_uuid
    into ux_owner;
  end else
  if (ux_kind = 'uxaction') then
  begin
    select ua_owner
    from ux$action
    where ua_uuid = :ux_uuid
    into ux_owner;
  end else
  if (ux_kind in ('uxform', 'uxcommand', 'uxreport')) then
  begin
    select ux_us_uuid
    from ux$code
    where ux_uuid = :ux_uuid
    into ux_owner;

    if (row_count = 0) then
    begin
      select ux_us_uuid
      from ux$code, ux$form
      where ufm_uuid = :ux_uuid and
            ux_uuid = ufm_ux_uuid
      into ux_owner;
    end
  end else
  if (ux_kind = 'uxframe') then
  begin
    select ufr_owner
    from ux$frame
    where ufr_uuid = :ux_uuid
    into ux_owner;
  end else
  if (ux_kind = 'uxalias') then
  begin
    select uxa_owner
    from ux$alias
    where uxa_uuid = :ux_uuid
    into ux_owner;
  end else
    exception e_default f_format('Unknown ux_kind: %1', ux_kind);

  if (ux_owner is not null) then
    res_str = uxf$code(ux_owner, res_kind, error_if_not_exists);

  return res_str;
end
UXF$PAIR(OLD_UUID)→ D_UUID
Возвращает новый UUID при копировании объекта
OLD_UUID CHAR(16)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$PAIR(
    OLD_UUID D_UUID)
RETURNS D_UUID
AS
declare variable new_uuid d_uuid;
declare variable uu_kind d_smallint;
begin
  if (old_uuid is not null) then
  begin
    select uu_kind
    from ux$uniq
    where uu_id = :old_uuid
    into uu_kind;
  
    if (uu_kind < 0) then -- Поле или источник, либо базовый объект
      new_uuid = old_uuid;
  
    select up_new_uuid
    from ux$pairuuid
    where up_old_uuid = :old_uuid
    into new_uuid;
  end

  return new_uuid;
end
UXF$PAIRUUID(OLD_UUID)→ D_STR_CODE
Возвращает строку UUID для замены во время экспорта
OLD_UUID CHAR(16)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$PAIRUUID(
    OLD_UUID D_UUID)
RETURNS D_STR_CODE
AS
declare variable new_uuid d_uuid;
declare variable uu_kind d_smallint;
declare variable uu_is_base d_bool;
begin
  if (old_uuid is null) then
    return null;

  select uu_kind, uu_is_base
  from ux$uniq
  where uu_id = :old_uuid
  into uu_kind, uu_is_base;

  if (uu_kind < 0 or uu_is_base = 1) then -- Поле или источник, либо базовый объект
    new_uuid = old_uuid;
  else
  if (exists(select * from ux$code where ux_uuid = :old_uuid)) then
    new_uuid = old_uuid;
  else
  begin
    -- Иначе возвращается новый UUID
    select up_new_uuid
    from ux$pairuuid
    where up_old_uuid = :old_uuid
    into new_uuid;
  
    if (new_uuid is null) then
    begin
      insert into ux$pairuuid (up_old_uuid)
      values (:old_uuid)
      returning up_new_uuid
      into :new_uuid;
    end
  end

  return uuid_to_char(new_uuid);
end
UXF$PAUSE(PUASE_MILLISECONDS)→ D_BOOL
PUASE_MILLISECONDS INTEGER
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$PAUSE(
    PUASE_MILLISECONDS D_INTEGER)
RETURNS D_BOOL
AS
declare variable started d_datetime;
begin
  if (puase_milliseconds <= 0) then
    exit;

  if (puase_milliseconds > 60000) then
    exception e_default 'To much pause milliseconds';

  started = cast('NOW' as timestamp);
  while (datediff(millisecond, started, cast('NOW' as timestamp)) < puase_milliseconds) do
  begin
  end
  return 0;
end
UXF$PERSLINK(SOURCE_NAME, KEY_ID)→ D_STR_255
Возвращает текстовое значение ссылочной Id
SOURCE_NAME VARCHAR(50)
KEY_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$PERSLINK(
    SOURCE_NAME D_STR_CODE,
    KEY_ID D_ID)
RETURNS D_STR_255
AS
declare variable res d_str_255;
begin
  if (coalesce(source_name, '') = '') then
    exception e_message 'Empty source_name';

  if (key_id is null) then
    return null;

  if (source_name = 'addrlist') then
  begin
    select adl_address_str
    from addrlist
    where adl_id = :key_id
    into res;
  end else
  if (source_name = 'refdata') then
  begin
    select rd_name
    from refdata
    where rd_id = :key_id
    into res;
  end else
  if (source_name = 'contlist') then
  begin
    select clt_code
    from contlist
    where clt_id = :key_id
    into res;
  end else
    exception e_message 'Undefined Persinfo source: ' || source_name;

  return res;
end
UXF$PREBLANKS_CODE(CODE, BLANK_ID)→ D_SMALLINT
CODE VARCHAR(50)
BLANK_ID INTEGER
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$PREBLANKS_CODE(
    CODE D_STR_CODE,
    BLANK_ID D_INTEGER)
RETURNS D_SMALLINT
AS
declare variable res_id d_smallint;
declare variable ubc_id d_integer;
declare variable ubl_required d_bool;
begin
  res_id = 0;
  if (code = 'all') then
    res_id = 1;
  else
  begin
    select ubc_id
    from ux$blankcode
    where ubc_code = :code
    into ubc_id;
  
    if (ubc_id is not null) then
    begin
      select ubl_required
      from ux$blanklink
      where ubl_from_ubc_id = :ubc_id and
            ubl_blank_id = :blank_id
      into ubl_required;

      if (ubl_required = 0) then
        res_id = 1;
      else
      if (ubl_required = 1) then
        res_id = -1;
    end
  end

  return res_id;
end
UXF$PRO()→ D_BOOLEAN DETERMINISTIC

CREATE FUNCTION UXF$PRO
RETURNS D_BOOLEAN DETERMINISTIC
AS
begin
  if (exists(select * from ust$table
             where tab_id = -1 and
                   tab_table = 'refdata')) then
    return false;
  else
    return true;
end
UXF$PSWD(TEXT)→ D_STR_255
TEXT VARCHAR(255)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$PSWD(
    TEXT D_STR_255 = null)
RETURNS D_STR_255
AS
declare variable res d_str_255;
begin
  res = '';
  if (trim(coalesce(text, '')) = '') then
    res = uxf$cript('off', coalesce(rdb$get_context('USER_TRANSACTION', 'db_info'), ''));
  else
    rdb$set_context('USER_TRANSACTION', 'db_info', uxf$cript('on', text));

  return res;
end

UXF$REP_DB()→ D_STR_255 DETERMINISTIC
Путь к отчетной базе
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$REP_DB
RETURNS D_STR_255 DETERMINISTIC
AS
declare variable db_id d_integer;
declare variable res d_str_255;
begin
  -- Путь к отчетной базе
  res = '';

  -- Если это тестовая база, путь отчетной базе возвращаем пустой
  if (not uxf$db_actual()) then
    return res;

  db_id = uxf$db_id();

  res = case db_id
    when 81001 then '10.1.2.48/3050:e:\db_repl\db_mbfru.ib'
    when 41701 then '192.168.203.250/3050:d:\finance\ib_base\db_1.ib'
    when 39801 then '10.70.200.250/3050:d:\db\db_kz.ib'
    when 94401 then '10.61.0.50/3050:e:\db_repl\db_az.ib'
    else ''
  end;

  res = trim(res);

  return res;
end
UXF$SET_BLOB(UX_BLOB, UX_FILE_NAME)→ ID_TYPE
UX_BLOB BLOB SUB_TYPE BINARY SEGMENT SIZE 80
UX_FILE_NAME VARCHAR(255)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$SET_BLOB(
    UX_BLOB D_BLOB_BINARY,
    UX_FILE_NAME D_STR_255)
RETURNS ID_TYPE
AS
declare variable bls_id id_type;
declare variable bls_size d_float;
declare variable un_id d_id;
begin
  -- Вставка Blob в BlobStore и возврат новой BLS_ID

  if (ux_blob is null or trim(coalesce(ux_file_name, '')) = '') then
    return null;

  un_id = uxf$user();

  if (un_id is null) then
    exception e_default 'Пользователь неопределен';

  bls_size = char_length(:ux_blob);
  if (bls_size = 0) then
    exception e_message 'Размер файла нулевой';

  execute procedure hp_insert_into_blobstore (:ux_file_name, '', :ux_blob, :un_id, 2, 0)
  returning_values :bls_id;

  return bls_id;
end
UXF$SET_USER(UN_ID)→ D_ID
Устанавливает контекстную переменную UN_ID на уровне транзакции. / Обычно вызывется из МП. В дальнейшем данное значение используется в uxf$user()
UN_ID BIGINT

CREATE FUNCTION UXF$SET_USER(
    UN_ID D_ID NOT NULL)
RETURNS D_ID

begin
  rdb$set_context('USER_TRANSACTION', 'FINSTART_USER_ID', un_id);

    TEXT D_STR_255 = null)
RETURNS D_STR_255
AS

begin
  res = text;
  if (trim(coalesce(text, '')) = '') then
    res = coalesce(rdb$get_context('USER_TRANSACTION', 'db_path'), '');

end
UXF$SYS_ID()→ D_UUID DETERMINISTIC
Возвращает идентификатор системного объекта, который идентичен для источника, фрейма, формы и т.д.
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$SYS_ID
RETURNS D_UUID DETERMINISTIC
AS
declare variable us_uuid d_uuid;
begin
  select us_uuid
  from ux$source
  where us_code = 'sys$params'
  into us_uuid;

  if (row_count = 0) then
    exception e_default 'System uuid not found. Execute UXP$SYSPARAMS_UX proc';

end
UXF$TRANSLIT(ABC_FROM, ABC_TO, TRANSLIT_TEXT, CITIZEN_CODE)→ D_STR_LONG
ABC_FROM VARCHAR(10) — Кодировка базовая
ABC_TO VARCHAR(10) — Кодировка результат
TRANSLIT_TEXT VARCHAR(1024) — Транслируемый текст
CITIZEN_CODE VARCHAR(10) — Код гражданства
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$TRANSLIT(
    ABC_FROM D_STR_10,
    ABC_TO D_STR_10,
    TRANSLIT_TEXT D_STR_LONG,
    CITIZEN_CODE D_STR_10 = null)
RETURNS D_STR_LONG
AS
declare variable res_text d_str_long collate utf8;
declare variable all_is_upper d_smallint;
declare variable tmp_str d_str_10;
begin
  res_text = translit_text collate UTF8;

  all_is_upper = 0;
  if (res_text collate UTF8 = upper(res_text) collate UTF8) then
    all_is_upper = 1;

  if (coalesce(translit_text, '') = '') then
    return res_text;

  if (coalesce(citizen_code, '') = '') then
    citizen_code = uxf$const('citizen_code');

  if (abc_from <> 'orig' and
      abc_to <> 'orig') then
  begin
    if (abc_from not in ('cyr', 'lat', 'eng')) then
      exception e_default 'Incorrect "abc from" parameter';

    if (abc_to not in ('cyr', 'lat', 'eng')) then
      exception e_default 'Incorrect "abc to" parameter';

    if (not(abc_from = 'cyr' or abc_to = 'cyr' or (abc_from = 'lat' and
        abc_to = 'eng'))) then
      exception e_default 'Incorrect abc pair: ' || abc_from || ' -> ' || abc_to;

    if (coalesce(translit_text, '') = '') then
      return res_text;

    -- Добавляем доп пробел, чтобы была возможность делать замену в конце слова по другому
    res_text = res_text || ' ';
    res_text = replace(res_text, '`', 'ʻ');
    res_text = replace(res_text, '''', 'ʻ');

-- расширенную кириллицу на русскую кириллицу
    if (abc_from = 'cyr' and
        abc_to = 'cyr') then
    begin
      if (all_is_upper = 0) then
      begin
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ў', 'у');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ӯ', 'у');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'қ', 'к');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ғ', 'г');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ҳ', 'х');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ҷ', 'ж');
        res_text = replace(res_text, '�©', 'о');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ӊ', 'н');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ң', 'н');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ӣ', 'й');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ү', 'у');
      end

      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ў', 'У');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ӯ', 'У');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Қ', 'К');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ғ', 'Г');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ҳ', 'Х');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ҷ', 'Ж');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ө', 'О');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ӊ', 'Н');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ң', 'Н');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ӣ', 'Й');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ү', 'У');
    end

    if (abc_from = 'cyr' and
        abc_to = 'eng') then
    begin
      if (all_is_upper = 0) then
      begin
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'а', 'a');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'б', 'b');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'в', 'v');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'г', 'g');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'д', 'd');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'е', 'e');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ё', 'e');
        if (citizen_code in ('ru', 'kg'))
        then res_text = replace(res_text, 'ж', 'zh');
        else  res_text = replace(res_text, 'ж', 'j');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'з', 'z');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'и', 'i');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'й', 'i');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'к', 'k');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'л', 'l');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'м', 'm');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'н', 'n');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'о', 'o');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'п', 'p');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'р', 'r');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'с', 's');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'т', 't');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'у', 'u');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ф', 'f');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'х', 'kh');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ц', 'ts');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ч', 'ch');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ш', 'sh');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'щ', 'shch');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ъ', 'ie');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ы', 'y');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ь', '');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'э', 'e');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ю', 'iu');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'я', 'ia');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ў', 'u');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ӯ', 'u');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'қ', 'k');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ғ', 'g');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ҳ', 'h');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ҷ', 'ch');
        res_text = replace(res_text, '�©', 'o');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ӊ', 'n');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ң', 'n');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ӣ', 'y');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ү', 'u');
      end

      res_text = replace(res_text, 'А', 'A');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Б', 'B');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'В', 'V');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Г', 'G');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Д', 'D');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Е', 'E');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ё', 'E');
      if (citizen_code in ('ru', 'kg'))
      then res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ж', 'Zh');
      else  res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ж', 'J');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'З', 'Z');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'И', 'I');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Й', 'I');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'К', 'K');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Л', 'L');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'М', 'M');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Н', 'N');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'О', 'O');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'П', 'P');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Р', 'R');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'С', 'S');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Т', 'T');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'У', 'U');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ф', 'F');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Х', 'Kh');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ц', 'Ts');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ч', 'Ch');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ш', 'Sh');
      res_text = replace(res_text, '�©', 'Shch');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ъ', 'Ie');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ы', 'Y');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ь', '');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Э', 'E');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ю', 'Iu');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Я', 'Ia');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ў', 'U');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ӯ', 'U');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Қ', 'K');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ғ', 'G');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ҳ', 'H');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ҷ', 'Ch');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ө', 'O');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ӊ', 'N');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ң', 'N');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ӣ', 'Y');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ү', 'U');
    end

    if (abc_from = 'cyr' and
        abc_to = 'lat') then
    begin
      if (citizen_code <> 'az') then
      begin
        -- кириллица на латинь кроме азербаджанского
        if (all_is_upper = 0) then
        begin
          res_text = replace(res_text, 'а', 'a');
          res_text = replace(res_text, 'б', 'b');
          res_text = replace(res_text, 'в', 'v');
          res_text = replace(res_text, 'г', 'g');
          res_text = replace(res_text, 'д', 'd');
          res_text = replace(res_text, 'е', 'e');
          res_text = replace(res_text, 'ё', 'yo');
          res_text = replace(res_text, 'ж', 'j');
          res_text = replace(res_text, 'з', 'z');
          res_text = replace(res_text, 'и', 'i');
          res_text = replace(res_text, 'й', 'y');
          res_text = replace(res_text, 'к', 'k');
          res_text = replace(res_text, 'л', 'l');
          res_text = replace(res_text, 'м', 'm');
          res_text = replace(res_text, 'н', 'n');
          res_text = replace(res_text, 'о', 'o');
          res_text = replace(res_text, 'п', 'p');
          res_text = replace(res_text, 'р', 'r');

          res_text = replace(res_text, 'q', 'k');
        end
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'j', 'zh');

        if (citizen_code = 'az') then
          res_text = replace(res_text, 'ж', 'j');

        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ç', 'ch');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ə', 'e');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ğ', 'gh');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ı', 'i');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ö', 'o');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ş', 'sh');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'ü', 'u');
      end

      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Gʻ', 'G');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Oʻ', 'O');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'ʼ', '');

      if (citizen_code = 'az') then
      begin
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'C', 'Ж');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'Q', 'G');
      end else
      begin
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'C', 'S');
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'Q', 'K');
      end
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'J', 'Zh');

      if (citizen_code = 'az') then
        res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ж', 'J');

      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ç', 'Ch');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ə', 'E');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ğ', 'Gh');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'İ', 'I');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ö', 'O');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ş', 'Sh');
      res_text = replace(res_text, 'Ü', 'U');
    end
  end

  if (all_is_upper = 1) then
    res_text = upper(res_text);

  res_text = trim(trailing ' ' from res_text);

  return res_text;
end
UXF$TRANSLIT_FIO(IN_TEXT, ABC_TO_LANG, PASSPORT_CITIZEN_ID, PASSPORT_DISB_DATE)→ D_STR_LONG
IN_TEXT VARCHAR(1024)
ABC_TO_LANG VARCHAR(10)
PASSPORT_CITIZEN_ID INTEGER
PASSPORT_DISB_DATE DATE
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$TRANSLIT_FIO(
    IN_TEXT D_STR_LONG,
    ABC_TO_LANG D_STR_10,
    PASSPORT_CITIZEN_ID D_INTEGER = null,
    PASSPORT_DISB_DATE D_DATE = null)
RETURNS D_STR_LONG
AS
declare variable lang_code d_str_code;
declare variable db_id d_integer;
declare variable res_text d_str_long;
begin
  db_id = f_get_db_id();

  if (trim(coalesce(abc_to_lang, '')) = '' or trim(coalesce(in_text, '')) = '') then
    return in_text;

  if (abc_to_lang not in ('lat', 'eng', 'cyr')) then
    return in_text;

  select replace(rd_code, 'citizen.', '')
  from refdata
  where rd_id = :passport_citizen_id
  into lang_code;

  if (char_length(coalesce(lang_code, '')) <> 2) then
  begin
    if (db_id = 94401)
    then lang_code = 'az';
    else lang_code = 'ru';
  end

  if (abc_to_lang = 'lat') then
  begin
    if (db_id = 94401)
    then res_text = f_translit_fio('lat', 'cyr', in_text, lang_code, passport_disb_date);
    else res_text = f_translit_fio('cyr', 'lat', in_text, lang_code, passport_disb_date);
  end else
  if (abc_to_lang = 'eng') then
  begin
    if (db_id = 94401)
    then res_text = f_translit_fio('lat', 'eng', in_text, lang_code, passport_disb_date);
    else res_text = f_translit_fio('cyr', 'eng', in_text, lang_code, passport_disb_date);
  end else
  if (abc_to_lang = 'cyr') then
  begin
    if (db_id = 94401)
    then res_text = f_translit_fio('lat', 'cyr', in_text, lang_code, passport_disb_date);
    else res_text = f_translit_fio('eng', 'cyr', in_text, lang_code, passport_disb_date);
  end else
    res_text = in_text;

  return res_text;
end
UXF$UNIQ(UX_UUID_CODE, ERROR_IF_NOT_EXISTS)→ D_UUID
Конвертирует входящий параметр в UUID
UX_UUID_CODE VARCHAR(100) — UUID в формате XXXXXXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXXXXXXXXXX или код объекта из Ux$Code
ERROR_IF_NOT_EXISTS SMALLINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$UNIQ(
    UX_UUID_CODE D_STR_100,
    ERROR_IF_NOT_EXISTS D_BOOL = 1)
RETURNS D_UUID
AS
declare variable ux_uuid d_uuid;
declare variable ux_datetime_str d_str_code;
declare variable p d_integer;
declare variable us_code d_str_code;
declare variable uf_name d_str_code;
begin
  -- Конвертирует входящий параметр в UUID, если другой формат, ищет его по таблице

  if (ux_uuid_code is not null) then
  begin
    begin
      ux_uuid = char_to_uuid(ux_uuid_code);
      when any do
      begin
        if (position(' v', ux_uuid_code) > 0) then
        begin
          ux_datetime_str = substring(ux_uuid_code from position(' v', ux_uuid_code) + 2);
          ux_uuid_code = left(ux_uuid_code, position(' v', ux_uuid_code) - 1);
  
          select ufm_uuid
          from ux$code, ux$form, ux$uniq
          where ux_fcode = :ux_uuid_code and
                ux_uuid = ufm_ux_uuid and
                ufm_uuid = uu_id and
                f_datetimetostr(uu_datetime) = :ux_datetime_str
          into ux_uuid;
        end else
        begin
          if (ux_uuid is null) then
          begin
            select ux_uuid
            from ux$code
            where ux_fcode = :ux_uuid_code
            into ux_uuid;
          end
  
          if (ux_uuid is null) then
          begin
            select us_uuid
            from ux$source
            where us_code = :ux_uuid_code
            into ux_uuid;
          end
  
          if (ux_uuid is null) then
          begin
            select ufr_uuid
            from ux$frame
            where ufr_fcode = :ux_uuid_code
            into ux_uuid;
          end
  
          if (ux_uuid is null) then
          begin
            select uxa_uuid
            from ux$alias
            where uxa_fcode = :ux_uuid_code
            into ux_uuid;
          end

          if (ux_uuid is null) then
          begin
            us_code = '';
            uf_name = '';

            p = position('.', ux_uuid_code);
            if (p > 0) then
            begin
              us_code = left(ux_uuid_code, p - 1);
              uf_name = substring(ux_uuid_code from (p + 1));
            end

            if (us_code <> '' and uf_name <> '') then
            begin
              select uf_uuid
              from ux$field, ux$source
              where us_code = :us_code and
                    us_uuid = uf_owner and
                    uf_field_name = :uf_name
              into ux_uuid;
            end
          end
        end
      end
    end
  
    if (ux_uuid is null and error_if_not_exists = 1) then
      exception e_default f_format('Unknown ux_uuid_code: %1', ux_uuid_code);
  end

  return ux_uuid;
end
UXF$USER()→ D_ID
Получение Id пользователя
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$USER
RETURNS D_ID
AS
declare variable un_name d_str_code;
declare variable un_id d_id;
begin
  -- сначала получаем пользователя на уровне транзакции (un_id),
  -- если не установлен, потом пытаемся на уровне сессии (un_name).

  un_id = rdb$get_context('USER_TRANSACTION', 'FINSTART_USER_ID');
  if (un_id is null) then
  begin
    if (coalesce(un_name, '') = '') then
      un_name = rdb$get_context('USER_SESSION', 'FINSTART_USER_LOGIN');
  
    if (coalesce(un_name, '') = '' or un_name = 'OTHER_USERS' or un_name = 'SYSDBA') then
      un_name = current_user;
  
    if (un_name = 'SYSDBA') then
      un_id = 1000000000000;
    else
      un_id = uxf$user_id(un_name, false);
  end

  if (un_id is null) then
    exception e_default f_format('User Id not found: %1', un_name);

  return un_id;
end
UXF$USERAPP()→ D_UUID
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$USERAPP
RETURNS D_UUID
AS
declare variable res_str d_str_code;
declare variable res_uuid d_uuid;
declare variable app_name d_str_long;
declare variable db_name d_str_long;
begin
  res_str = rdb$get_context('USER_SESSION', 'COMMON_USER_APP');

  if (coalesce(res_str, '') = '') then
  begin
    app_name = rdb$get_context('SYSTEM', 'CLIENT_PROCESS');
    app_name = lower(trim(coalesce(app_name, '')));

    db_name = rdb$get_context('SYSTEM', 'DB_NAME');
    db_name = lower(trim(coalesce(db_name, '')));

    select uap_uuid
    from ux$userapp u
    where u.uap_service_name = :app_name and
          u.uap_db_name = :db_name
    into res_uuid;

    if (row_count = 0) then
    begin
      if (uxf$readonly()) then
        res_uuid = f_md5_uuid(app_name || '#' || db_name);
      else
      begin
        insert into ux$userapp (uap_service_name, uap_db_name)
        values (:app_name, :db_name)
        returning uap_uuid
        into :res_uuid;
      end
    end

    rdb$set_context('USER_SESSION', 'COMMON_USER_APP', uuid_to_char(res_uuid));
  end
  else
    res_uuid = char_to_uuid(res_str);

  return res_uuid;
end
UXF$USERIP()→ D_ID
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$USERIP
RETURNS D_ID
AS
declare variable ip_address d_str_code;
declare variable res_ip d_id;
begin
  ip_address = rdb$get_context('USER_SESSION', 'COMMON_USER_IP');

  if (coalesce(ip_address, '') = '') then
  begin
    ip_address = rdb$get_context('SYSTEM', 'CLIENT_ADDRESS');
    res_ip = uxf$ip_to_int(ip_address);
    rdb$set_context('USER_SESSION', 'COMMON_USER_IP', res_ip);
  end
  else
    res_ip = ip_address;

  return res_ip;
end
UXF$USERLANG(UN_ID)→ D_STR_LANG
UN_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$USERLANG(
    UN_ID D_ID = null)
RETURNS D_STR_LANG
AS
declare variable lang d_str_lang;
begin
  lang = rdb$get_context('USER_SESSION', 'USER_LANG');

  if (coalesce(lang, '') = '') then
  begin
    if (un_id is null) then
      un_id = uxf$user();
  
    if (un_id is not null) then
    begin
      select un_lang_code
      from username
      where un_id = :un_id
      into lang;
    end

    if (coalesce(lang, '') = '') then
      lang = 'ru';

    rdb$set_context('USER_SESSION', 'USER_LANG', lang);
  end

  return lang;
end
UXF$USER_ID(USER_LOGIN, ERROR_IF_NOT_FOUND)→ D_ID

    USER_LOGIN D_STR_CODE,
    ERROR_IF_NOT_FOUND D_BOOLEAN = true)
RETURNS D_ID
AS
declare variable res_id d_id;
begin
  if (error_if_not_found is null) then
    error_if_not_found = true;

  select un_id
  from username
  where un_name = :user_login
  into res_id;

  return res_id;
end
UXF$USER_IS(USER_KIND, UN_ID)→ D_BOOL
USER_KIND VARCHAR(50) — superuser, developer, poweruser
UN_ID BIGINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$USER_IS(
    USER_KIND D_STR_CODE,
    UN_ID D_ID = null)
RETURNS D_BOOL
AS
declare variable res_id d_bool;
declare variable role_list d_str_long;
declare variable s_name d_str_255;
begin
  if (coalesce(user_kind, '') = '') then
    exception e_default 'Empty user_kind';

  if (un_id is null) then
    un_id = uxf$user();

  if (user_kind not in ('superuser', 'poweruser', 'developer')) then
    exception e_default 'Undefined user_kind: ' || user_kind;

  role_list = 'FinStart.Superuser';

  if (user_kind in ('poweruser', 'developer')) then
    role_list = role_list || ',' || 'FinStart.Programmer';

  if (user_kind = 'poweruser') then
    role_list = role_list || ',' || 'FinStart.Permission_Administrator,FinStart.Permission_Operator';

  res_id = 0;

  for select res_str
  from uxp$split_str(:role_list, ',')
  into s_name
  do
  begin
    if (exists(select *
               from hp_permission_access_check(:un_id, 'PermActions', 0, :s_name)
               where has_access = 1)) then
    begin
      res_id = 1;
      break;
    end
  end

  return res_id;
end
UXF$USER_LOGIN(USER_ID)→ D_STR_CODE
USER_ID BIGINT — Id пользователя
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$USER_LOGIN(
    USER_ID D_ID)
RETURNS D_STR_CODE
AS
declare variable res_code d_str_code;
begin
  select un_name
  from username
  where un_id = :user_id
  into res_code;

  if (row_count = 0) then
  begin
    select usr_code
    from ux$users
    where usr_id = :user_id and
          usr_kind = 0
    into res_code;
  end

  if (coalesce(res_code, '') = '') then
    res_code = 'un' || user_id;

  return res_code;
end

UXF$USR_CID(EXCEPTION_IF_NOT_FOUND)→ D_ID
Возвращает Id клиента по текущему пользователю Usr_id
EXCEPTION_IF_NOT_FOUND SMALLINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$USR_CID(
    EXCEPTION_IF_NOT_FOUND D_BOOL = 1)
RETURNS D_ID
AS
declare variable usr_id d_id;
declare variable c_id d_id;
begin
  if (exception_if_not_found is null) then
   exception_if_not_found = 1;

  usr_id = uxf$usr_id();

  if (usr_id is null) then
  begin
    if (exception_if_not_found = 1) then
      exception e_default 'Common user id not found in context memory';
    else
      return null;
  end

  select usr_from_c_id
  from ux$users
  where usr_id = :usr_id
  into c_id;

  if (row_count = 0) then
  begin
    if (exception_if_not_found = 1) then
      exception e_default 'Usr_id not found in users list';
    else
      return null;
  end

  return c_id;
end
UXF$USR_ID()→ D_ID
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$USR_ID
RETURNS D_ID
AS
declare variable usr_id d_id;
begin
  usr_id = rdb$get_context('USER_TRANSACTION', 'FINSTART_USER_ID');
  if (usr_id is null) then
    usr_id = rdb$get_context('USER_SESSION', 'COMMON_USER_ID');

  return usr_id;
end
UXF$USR_MCL(EXCEPTION_IF_NOT_FOUND)→ D_ID
Возвращает MCL_ID по текущему пользователю Usr_id
EXCEPTION_IF_NOT_FOUND SMALLINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$USR_MCL(
    EXCEPTION_IF_NOT_FOUND D_BOOL = 1)
RETURNS D_ID
AS
declare variable usr_id d_id;
declare variable mcl_id d_id;
declare variable tmp_id d_id;
declare variable usr_kind d_smallint;
declare variable usr_from_c_id d_id;
begin
  if (exception_if_not_found is null) then
   exception_if_not_found = 1;

  usr_id = uxf$usr_id();

  if (usr_id is null) then
  begin
    if (exception_if_not_found = 1) then
      exception e_default 'Common user id not found in context memory';
    else
      return null;
  end

  select usr_code, usr_kind, usr_from_c_id
  from ux$users
  where usr_id = :usr_id
  into tmp_id, usr_kind, usr_from_c_id;

  if (row_count = 0) then
  begin
    if (exception_if_not_found = 1) then
      exception e_default 'Usr_id not found in users list';
    else
      return null;
  end

  if (usr_kind = 2) then
    mcl_id = tmp_id;
  else
  begin
    if (usr_from_c_id is not null) then
    begin
      select mcl_id
      from mc_login m
      where m.mcl_from_c_id = :usr_from_c_id
      order by m.mcl_is_delete, m.mcl_datetime desc
      rows 1
      into mcl_id;
    end

    if (mcl_id is null) then
    begin
      if (exception_if_not_found = 1) then
        exception e_default 'mc_login not found by client_id';
      else
        return null;
    end
  end

  return mcl_id;
end
UXF$USR_UN(USR_ID, EXCEPTION_IF_NOT_FOUND)→ D_ID
Возвращает UN_ID по текущему пользователю Usr_id
USR_ID BIGINT — Если Null возвращает текущего пользователя
EXCEPTION_IF_NOT_FOUND SMALLINT
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$USR_UN(
    USR_ID D_ID = null,
    EXCEPTION_IF_NOT_FOUND D_BOOL = 1)
RETURNS D_ID
AS
declare variable un_id d_id;
declare variable tmp_id d_str_code;
declare variable usr_kind d_smallint;
declare variable usr_from_c_id d_id;
begin
  if (exception_if_not_found is null) then
   exception_if_not_found = 1;

  if (usr_id is null) then
    usr_id = uxf$usr_id();

  if (usr_id is null) then
  begin
    if (exception_if_not_found = 1) then
      exception e_default 'Common user id not found in context memory';
    else
      return null;
  end

  select usr_code, usr_kind, usr_from_c_id
  from ux$users
  where usr_id = :usr_id
  into tmp_id, usr_kind, usr_from_c_id;

  if (row_count = 0) then
  begin
    if (exception_if_not_found = 1) then
      exception e_default 'Usr_id not found in users list';
    else
      return null;
  end

  if (usr_kind = 1) then
    un_id = tmp_id;
  else
  begin
    if (usr_from_c_id is not null) then
    begin
      select un_id
      from username
      where un_from_c_id = :usr_from_c_id
      order by coalesce(un_is_active, 0) desc, un_datetime desc
      rows 1
      into un_id;
    end

    if (un_id is null) then
    begin
      if (exception_if_not_found = 1) then
        exception e_default 'username not found by client_id';
      else
        return null;
    end
  end

  return un_id;
end
UXF$UUID(UX_OWNER, UX_KIND, UX_CODE)→ D_UUID
Возвращает идентификатор объекта по владельцу, тип и короткому коду
UX_OWNER CHAR(16) — UUID владельца
UX_KIND VARCHAR(50) — Тип объекта
UX_CODE VARCHAR(50) — Короткий код
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$UUID(
    UX_OWNER D_UUID,
    UX_KIND D_STR_CODE,
    UX_CODE D_STR_CODE)
RETURNS D_UUID
AS
declare variable ux_uuid d_uuid;
begin
  -- Возвращает UX_UUID по продителю и коду объекта

  ux_kind = uxf$kind(ux_kind, 'code');

  if (ux_kind = 'uxsource') then
  begin
    select us_uuid
    from ux$source
    where us_code = :ux_code
    into ux_uuid;
  end else
  if (ux_kind = 'uxfield') then
  begin
    select uf_uuid
    from ux$field
    where uf_owner = :ux_owner and
          uf_field_name = :ux_code
    into ux_uuid;
  end else
  if (ux_kind = 'uxframe') then
  begin
    select ufr_uuid
    from ux$frame
    where ufr_owner = :ux_owner and
          ufr_code = :ux_code
    into ux_uuid;
  end else
  if (ux_kind = 'uxalias') then
  begin
    select uxa_uuid
    from ux$alias
    where uxa_owner = :ux_owner and
          uxa_code = :ux_code
    into ux_uuid;
  end else
  if (ux_kind in ('uxform', 'uxcommand', 'uxreport')) then
  begin
    select ux_uuid
    from ux$code
    where ux_us_uuid = :ux_owner and
          ux_scode = :ux_code
    into ux_uuid;
  end else
    exception e_default f_format('Unique code is absent, ux_kind: %1', ux_kind);

  return ux_uuid;
end
UXF$VER(UX_UUID, RES_KIND)→ D_UUID
Возвращает необходимую версию формы для базовый объектов
UX_UUID CHAR(16)
RES_KIND VARCHAR(50)
Показать код
CREATE FUNCTION UXF$VER(
    UX_UUID D_UUID,
    RES_KIND D_STR_CODE = null)
RETURNS D_UUID
AS
declare variable ufm_uuid d_uuid;
declare variable ux_kind d_str_code;
declare variable is_base d_bool;
declare variable ux_release_uuid d_uuid;
declare variable ux_draft_uuid d_uuid;
declare variable ux_inactive d_bool;
declare variable res_kind_empty d_bool;
begin
  -- Возвращает необходимую версию для базовых объектов.
  -- Если объект не базовый (не из Ux$Code), возвращает переданные UUID

  res_kind_empty = 0;
  if (coalesce(res_kind, '') = '') then
  begin
    res_kind_empty = 1;
    res_kind = 'release'; -- Инфо берется из настроек
  end

  if (res_kind not in ('release', 'draft')) then
  begin
    ufm_uuid = char_to_uuid(res_kind);
    return ufm_uuid;

    when any do
      exception e_default f_format('Unknown res_kind: %1', res_kind);
  end

  select uxf$kind(uu_kind, 'code'), uu_is_base
  from ux$uniq
  where uu_id = :ux_uuid
  into ux_kind, is_base;

  if (row_count = 0) then
    exception e_default f_format('UUID not found: %1', uuid_to_char(ux_uuid));

  if (is_base = 0) then
  begin
    if (ux_kind = 'uxalias') then
    begin
      is_base = 1;
  
      select uxa_owner
      from ux$alias
      where uxa_uuid = :ux_uuid
      into ux_uuid;
    end else
    if (ux_kind = 'uxaction') then
    begin
      is_base = 1;
  
      select uxa_owner
      from ux$action, ux$alias
      where ua_uuid = :ux_uuid and
            ua_uxa_uuid = uxa_uuid
      into ux_uuid;
    end
  end

  if (is_base = 0) then
    ufm_uuid = ux_uuid;
  else
  begin
    select ux_release_uuid, ux_draft_uuid, ux_inactive
    from ux$code
    where ux_uuid = :ux_uuid
    into ux_release_uuid, ux_draft_uuid, ux_inactive;

    if (ux_inactive = 1) then
      exception e_message f_format('Базовая форма более не доступна, uuid: %1', uuid_to_char(ux_uuid));

    if (res_kind_empty = 1 and res_kind = 'release' and ux_release_uuid is null) then
      res_kind = 'draft';

    if (res_kind = 'draft') then
      ufm_uuid = ux_draft_uuid;
    else
    begin
      if (ux_release_uuid is null) then
        exception e_message f_format('Релиз версия формы отсутствует, uuid: %1', uuid_to_char(ux_uuid));

      ufm_uuid = ux_release_uuid;
    end
  end

  return ufm_uuid;
end
